응용패턴 통합: 흐름도와 실제 코드

공통 구현·전체 API 사례응용패턴·그네 통합

의미 동작·육면체 모드 · 정점 입력·내부 수정 순서 · 패턴 조립·API 압축

함수 이름을 외우는 대신 실제 상황에서 무엇을 저장하고 무엇을 바꾸는지 읽는다. 공개 함수·오버로드·생성자·소멸자·삭제된 복사 연산을 현재 framework/include 헤더에서 수록했다. graphics::detail 및 private 구현은 공개 호출 대상으로 나열하지 않고 공통 구현 설명에서 다룬다.

모든 코드는 현재 선언·구현에 근거한 문서다. 새 사용 사례는 함께 쓸 API를 묶은 함수 단편이며, 독립 main인 예제는 별도로 표시한다. 실행 성공을 확인한 코드라는 뜻은 아니다. 외부 자산 경로는 사용자가 준비한다.

응용패턴은 목적 → 변수 역할 → 흐름도 → 응용 코드 → 예상 결과 순으로 짧게 읽는다. 반복되는 렌더링 루프·GPU 자원 구현은 API 문서의 공통 구현 절에서 자세히 설명한다.

콜백으로 응용 상태 연결자전과 공전을 분리경로 전환·왕복순차 동작과 끝 자세 유지모션 공유와 대칭 분기시간 정지와 변환 기여 끄기바인딩을 유지한 레이어 삽입부품 계층과 소유자 기저선면 힌지와 추가 로컬 행렬클릭 선택과 바닥 배치메시·UV·재질 편집선택적 OBJ·텍스처두 위치 사이 선형보간지정 주기의 진자 운동격자 비율·셀·지그재그 배치거리 기반 컨베이어와 코너 방향회전하는 방과 이탈 후 낙하보행·점프·장애물 착지카메라 방향의 벽 개방편집 가능한 사람형·관절 트랙관절 선택·SRT·피벗 편집그네(진자) → 좌석 → 사람여러 카메라로 같은 Scene 표시의미 동작·육면체 모드·정점 입력 편집면의 독립·계층과 힌지·자전·공전

콜백으로 응용 상태 연결

회전하는 상자를 만들고 Esc로 종료하는 상황이다. Application은 창과 루프를 소유하고, box는 Scene 안의 상자를 다시 찾는 번호다. 콜백은 등록할 때 실행되지 않는다. run이 초기화·이벤트·프레임 시점에 각각 실행한다.

주요 상태: app은 run 종료까지 살아 있는 소유 객체다. box는 초기화 콜백에서 채우는 optional ID다. dt는 이번 프레임 경과 초이며 angle은 누적 각도다. camera와 options는 Application 내부 상태를 수정하는 참조다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

#include "framework/Framework.h"
#include <iostream>
int main() {
    graphics::Application app;
    std::optional<graphics::EntityId> box;
    float angle = 0;
    app.setInitHandler([&](graphics::Application& current) {
        box = current.getScene().addCuboid({1,1,1}, {0,0,0});
        auto& camera = current.getCamera(); camera.position = {4,3,6};
        current.getRenderOptions().depthTest = true;
        return box.has_value();
    });
    app.setKeyHandler([](graphics::Application& current,int key,int action) {
        if(key==GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE && action==GLFW_PRESS) current.requestClose();
    });
    app.setModifiedKeyHandler([](graphics::Application& current,int key,int action,int mods) {
        if(key==GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE && action==GLFW_PRESS) current.requestClose();
        std::cout << "수정키 비트=" << mods << '\n';
    });
    app.setFocusHandler([](graphics::Application&,bool focused) {
        std::cout << "포커스=" << focused << '\n';
    });
    app.setMouseHandler([](graphics::Application& current,const graphics::MouseEvent& e) {
        if(e.action!=GLFW_PRESS) return;
        if(auto hit=current.pickAt(e.windowX,e.windowY)) std::cout << hit->entityId << '\n';
    });
    app.setCursorHandler([](graphics::Application& current,double x,double y) {
        auto hit=current.pickAtCursor();
        (void)hit; (void)x; (void)y;
    });
    app.setUpdateHandler([&](graphics::Application& current,float dt) {
        angle += 45*dt;
        graphics::Transform3D pose;
        pose.rotation=glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1),glm::radians(angle),glm::vec3(0,1,0)));
        if(!box || !current.getScene().setTransform(*box,pose)) current.requestClose();
    });
    app.setRenderHandler([](graphics::Application& current) {
        const auto size=current.getFramebufferSize();
        GLFWwindow* window=current.getWindow(); (void)window;
        if(size[0]<3 || size[1]<2) return true;
        return current.renderViews(graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(
            current.getCamera(),size[0],size[1],current.getRenderOptions()));
        // 단일 뷰로 교체할 때는 return current.renderScene();
    });
    graphics::ApplicationConfig config;
    return app.run(config);
} // ~Application이 호출된다. 복사 생성/대입은 =delete이므로 호출할 수 없다.

수정키 콜백을 등록하면 일반 키 콜백 대신 호출된다. 이 예제의 Esc 처리는 두 콜백에 모두 있지만 실행되는 것은 수정키 콜백이다. update가 실패하면 종료를 요청하고, run은 GPU를 현재 컨텍스트에서 정리한다. getAssets의 사용 예는 자산 그룹에 따로 둔다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/main.cpp
#include "Console.h"

/// 콜백을 등록하고 애플리케이션을 실행한다.
int main() {
    graphics::Application app;
    Console sample(app);

    app.setInitHandler([&](graphics::Application&) {
        return sample.initScene();
    });
    app.setKeyHandler([&](graphics::Application&, int key, int action) {
        sample.onKey(key, action);
    });
    app.setMouseHandler([&](graphics::Application&, const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {
        sample.onMouse(event);
    });
    app.setUpdateHandler([&](graphics::Application&, float dt) {
        sample.onUpdate(dt);
    });
    app.setRenderHandler([&](graphics::Application&) {
        return sample.onRender();
    });

    graphics::ApplicationConfig config;

    config.title = "standard - Motion, Grid, diagnostics and render passes";

    return app.run(config);
}
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/detail/Application.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include "Internal.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <iostream>
#include <utility>

namespace graphics::detail {
bool& applicationActive() {
    static bool active = false;

    return active;
}

unsigned& contextErrorSerial() {
    static unsigned value = 0;

    return value;
}
}

namespace graphics {

Application::~Application() {
    shutdown();
}

bool Application::configureRenderer(const RenderConfig& config, const std::filesystem::path& vertexPath,
                                    const std::filesystem::path& fragmentPath) {
    if (!ensureContext("configureRenderer", __FILE__, __LINE__))
        return false;
    if (renderer_.initialize(vertexPath, fragmentPath, config))
        return true;
    requestClose();
    return false;
}

void Application::setInitHandler(std::function<bool(Application&)> handler) {
    initHandler_ = std::move(handler);
}

void Application::setUpdateHandler(std::function<void(Application&, float)> handler) {
    updateHandler_ = std::move(handler);
}

void Application::setKeyHandler(std::function<void(Application&, int, int)> handler) {
    keyHandler_ = std::move(handler);
}

void Application::setModifiedKeyHandler(std::function<void(Application&, int, int, int)> handler) {
    modifiedKeyHandler_ = std::move(handler);
}

void Application::setFocusHandler(std::function<void(Application&, bool)> handler) {
    focusHandler_ = std::move(handler);
}

void Application::setRenderHandler(std::function<bool(Application&)> handler) {
    renderHandler_ = std::move(handler);
}

bool Application::ensureContext(const char* operation, const char* file, int line) {
    Diagnostics::locate(file, line, operation);

    if (contextFailed_)
        return false;

    if (!window_ || GetCurrentThreadId() != contextThread_) {
        contextFailed_ = true;

        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Runtime, "Application::ensureContext",
            "창이 없거나 컨텍스트를 생성한 스레드와 다른 스레드에서 사용했습니다.",
            "창 생성·렌더링·종료를 같은 스레드에서 수행하세요. Application::run과 사용자 렌더 콜백을 확인하세요.");
    }
    using CurrentProc = void*(WINAPI*)();

    const auto module = GetModuleHandleW(L"opengl32.dll");
    const auto current =
        module ? reinterpret_cast<CurrentProc>(::GetProcAddress(module, "wglGetCurrentContext")) : nullptr;

    if (nativeContext_ && detail::glfw().GetCurrentContext() == window_ && current && current() == nativeContext_)
        return true;

    const auto serial = detail::contextErrorSerial();

    detail::glfw().MakeContextCurrent(window_);

    if (serial != detail::contextErrorSerial() || detail::glfw().GetCurrentContext() != window_ || !current ||
        !current()) {
        contextFailed_ = true;

        return Diagnostics::fail(
            PipelineStage::Runtime, "glfwMakeContextCurrent",
            "WGL이 요청한 컨텍스트를 연결하지 못하여 GL 명령 전에 렌더링을 중단했습니다.",
            "소스 위치는 연결 실패 호출입니다. 창 수명·렌더 스레드·컨텍스트를 해제/파괴하는 외부 콜백을 먼저 확인하세요. 이상이 없으면 GPU 드라이버·세션을 확인하세요. 이 로그만으로 드라이버 원인을 확정할 수 없습니다.");
    }
    nativeContext_ = current();

    return true;
}

bool Application::initialize(const ApplicationConfig& config) {
    contextThread_ = GetCurrentThreadId();
    contextFailed_ = false;
    nativeContext_ = nullptr;
    Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Runtime, "Application::initialize");

    const auto root = detail::relativePathInside(detail::executableDirectory(), config.projectRoot);
    const auto runtime = detail::relativePathInside(root, config.runtimeDirectory);

    detail::glfw().load(runtime);
    detail::glfw().SetErrorCallback([](int code, const char* message) {
        ++detail::contextErrorSerial();
        Diagnostics::fail(
            PipelineStage::Runtime, "GLFW/WGL", std::to_string(code) + ": " + (message ? message : "알 수 없는 오류"),
            "표시된 소스 위치·호출 경로에서 컨텍스트 연결이 실패했습니다. Application의 창 수명과 스레드를 먼저 확인하세요. OS/드라이버 원문도 함께 보세요. 렌더 패스마다 별도 WGL 컨텍스트를 만들 필요는 없습니다.");
    });

    if (config.width <= 0 || config.height <= 0)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Runtime, "Application::initialize",
                                   "창 너비와 높이는 양수여야 합니다.");

    if (!detail::glfw().Init())
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Runtime, "glfwInit",
                                   "GLFW 초기화 실패: 앞선 GLFW 오류를 확인하세요.");
    glfwInitialized_ = true;
    detail::glfw().WindowHint(GLFW_CONTEXT_VERSION_MAJOR, 3);
    detail::glfw().WindowHint(GLFW_CONTEXT_VERSION_MINOR, 3);
    detail::glfw().WindowHint(GLFW_OPENGL_PROFILE, GLFW_OPENGL_CORE_PROFILE);
    detail::glfw().WindowHint(GLFW_DOUBLEBUFFER, GLFW_TRUE);
    detail::glfw().WindowHint(GLFW_DEPTH_BITS, 24);
    window_ = detail::glfw().createWindow(config.width, config.height, config.title.c_str(), nullptr, nullptr);

    if (window_ == nullptr) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Runtime, "glfwCreateWindow",
                                   "OpenGL 3.3 코어 창 생성 실패.",
                                   "앞선 GLFW 오류와 그래픽 드라이버의 OpenGL 지원을 확인하세요.");
    }

    if (!ensureContext("initialize -> attach new window", __FILE__, __LINE__))
        return false;
    detail::glfw().SwapInterval(config.verticalSync ? 1 : 0);

    const auto vertexPath = config.shaderDirectory.empty()
                                ? std::filesystem::path{}
                                : detail::relativePathInside(root, config.shaderDirectory) / "VertexShader.glsl";
    const auto fragmentPath = config.shaderDirectory.empty()
                                  ? std::filesystem::path{}
                                  : detail::relativePathInside(root, config.shaderDirectory) / "FragmentShader.glsl";

    Diagnostics::locate(__FILE__, __LINE__, "initialize -> Renderer::initialize -> shader source/path setup");

    if (!renderer_.initialize(vertexPath, fragmentPath, config.renderConfig))
        return false;
    assets_.emplace(root, config.features);
    renderOptions_.textures = config.features.textures;

    detail::glfw().SetInputMode(window_, GLFW_LOCK_KEY_MODS, GLFW_TRUE);
    detail::glfw().SetWindowUserPointer(window_, this);
    detail::glfw().SetKeyCallback(window_, [](GLFWwindow* window, int key, int, int action, int mods) {
        auto& app = *static_cast<Application*>(detail::glfw().GetWindowUserPointer(window));

        app.dispatch([&] {
            if (app.modifiedKeyHandler_)
                app.modifiedKeyHandler_(app, key, action, mods);
            else if (app.keyHandler_)
                app.keyHandler_(app, key, action);
        });
    });
    detail::glfw().SetWindowFocusCallback(window_, [](GLFWwindow* window, int focused) {
        auto& app = *static_cast<Application*>(detail::glfw().GetWindowUserPointer(window));

        app.dispatch([&] {
            if (app.focusHandler_)
                app.focusHandler_(app, focused == GLFW_TRUE);
        });
    });
    detail::glfw().SetMouseButtonCallback(window_, [](GLFWwindow* window, int button, int action, int mods) {
        auto& app = *static_cast<Application*>(detail::glfw().GetWindowUserPointer(window));

        app.dispatch([&] {
            if (!app.mouseHandler_)
                return;

            MouseEvent event;

            event.button = button;
            event.action = action;
            event.mods = mods;
            detail::glfw().GetCursorPos(window, &event.windowX, &event.windowY);

            int ww = 0, wh = 0, fw = 0, fh = 0;

            detail::glfw().GetWindowSize(window, &ww, &wh);
            detail::glfw().GetFramebufferSize(window, &fw, &fh);

            if (ww <= 0 || wh <= 0 || fw <= 0 || fh <= 0)
                return;
            event.framebufferX = event.windowX * fw / ww;
            event.framebufferY = event.windowY * fh / wh;
            app.mouseHandler_(app, event);
        });
    });
    detail::glfw().SetCursorPosCallback(window_, [](GLFWwindow* window, double x, double y) {
        auto& app = *static_cast<Application*>(detail::glfw().GetWindowUserPointer(window));

        app.dispatch([&] {
            if (app.cursorHandler_)
                app.cursorHandler_(app, x, y);
        });
    });

    return true;
}

void Application::shutdown() {
    assets_.reset();

    if (window_ != nullptr) {
        if (!contextFailed_ && ensureContext("shutdown -> GPU cleanup", __FILE__, __LINE__))
            renderer_.destroy();
        else
            renderer_.abandon();
        detail::glfw().SetWindowUserPointer(window_, nullptr);
        detail::glfw().DestroyWindow(window_);
        window_ = nullptr;
    }

    if (glfwInitialized_) {
        detail::glfw().Terminate();
        glfwInitialized_ = false;
    }
}

void Application::requestClose() {
    if (window_ != nullptr) {
        detail::glfw().SetWindowShouldClose(window_, GLFW_TRUE);
    }
}

bool Application::renderScene() {
    if (window_ == nullptr) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Framebuffer, "Application::renderScene",
                                   "활성 창 또는 컨텍스트가 없습니다.");
    }

    if (!ensureContext("renderScene -> single pass", __FILE__, __LINE__))
        return false;

    int width = 0;
    int height = 0;

    detail::glfw().GetFramebufferSize(window_, &width, &height);

    return renderer_.render(scene_, camera_, width, height, renderOptions_);
}

std::array<int, 2> Application::getFramebufferSize() const {
    std::array<int, 2> size{};

    if (window_)
        detail::glfw().GetFramebufferSize(window_, &size[0], &size[1]);

    return size;
}

bool Application::renderViews(const std::vector<RenderPass>& passes) {
    if (!window_)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Framebuffer, "Application::renderViews",
                                   "활성 창 또는 컨텍스트가 없습니다.");

    if (!ensureContext("renderViews -> shared context for all viewports", __FILE__, __LINE__))
        return false;

    const auto size = getFramebufferSize();

    return renderer_.renderViews(scene_, passes, size[0], size[1]);
}

int Application::run(const ApplicationConfig& config) {
    Diagnostics::configure(config.diagnostics);
    Diagnostics::clear();

    if (running_) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Runtime, "Application::run",
                            "run() 재귀 호출은 허용하지 않습니다.");

        return -1; // 콜백에서 run()을 재귀 호출하지 않는다.
    }

    if (detail::applicationActive()) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Runtime, "Application::run",
                            "동시에 실행 중인 Application은 하나만 지원합니다.");

        return -1;
    }
    // /utf-8 encodes narrow strings; the Windows console must decode them as UTF-8.
    SetConsoleOutputCP(CP_UTF8);
    detail::applicationActive() = true;
    callbackError_ = nullptr;
    running_ = true;

    int exitCode = 0;

    try {
        if (!initialize(config)) {
            std::cerr << "애플리케이션 초기화 실패.\n";
            exitCode = -1;
        } else if (initHandler_ && !initHandler_(*this)) {
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Application::initHandler",
                                "사용자 초기화 콜백이 false를 반환했습니다.",
                                "앞선 API 오류와 장면 생성 콜백을 확인하세요.");
            std::cerr << "사용자 장면 초기화 실패.\n";
            exitCode = -1;
        } else {
            double previousTime = detail::glfw().GetTime();

            while (!detail::glfw().WindowShouldClose(window_)) {
                Diagnostics::locate(__FILE__, __LINE__, "run -> glfwPollEvents/input callbacks");
                detail::glfw().PollEvents();

                if (callbackError_)
                    std::rethrow_exception(callbackError_);

                if (detail::glfw().WindowShouldClose(window_)) {
                    break;
                }

                int width = 0;
                int height = 0;

                detail::glfw().GetFramebufferSize(window_, &width, &height);

                if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) {
                    detail::glfw().WaitEvents();

                    if (callbackError_)
                        std::rethrow_exception(callbackError_);
                    previousTime = detail::glfw().GetTime();
                    continue;
                }

                const double currentTime = detail::glfw().GetTime();
                const float deltaTime = static_cast<float>(std::clamp(currentTime - previousTime, 0.0, 0.05));

                previousTime = currentTime;

                if (updateHandler_) {
                    Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Input, "Application::updateHandler");
                    updateHandler_(*this, deltaTime);
                }

                if (detail::glfw().WindowShouldClose(window_)) {
                    break;
                }

                if (!scene_.updateMotions(deltaTime)) {
                    std::cerr << "모션 갱신 실패.\n";
                    exitCode = -1;
                    break;
                }

                if (!ensureContext("run -> render callback", __FILE__, __LINE__)) {
                    exitCode = -1;
                    break;
                }

                const bool rendered = renderHandler_ ? renderHandler_(*this) : renderScene();

                if (!rendered) {
                    std::cerr << "렌더링 실패.\n";
                    exitCode = -1;
                    break;
                }
                Diagnostics::locate(__FILE__, __LINE__, "run -> glfwSwapBuffers");

                const auto swapSerial = detail::contextErrorSerial();

                detail::glfw().SwapBuffers(window_);

                if (swapSerial != detail::contextErrorSerial()) {
                    contextFailed_ = true;
                    exitCode = -1;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Runtime, "Application::run", error.what(),
            "앞선 파이프라인 단계/API 오류를 확인하세요. 사용자 콜백 예외는 자원 정리 후 다시 전달됩니다.");
        shutdown();
        running_ = false;
        detail::applicationActive() = false;
        throw;
    } catch (...) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Runtime, "Application::run",
                            "콜백 또는 실행 처리에서 표준 예외가 아닌 예외가 발생했습니다.");
        shutdown();
        running_ = false;
        detail::applicationActive() = false;
        throw; // 사용자 예외를 숨기지 않고 컨텍스트 자원부터 정리한다.
    }
    shutdown();
    running_ = false;
    detail::applicationActive() = false;

    return exitCode;
}

void Application::setMouseHandler(std::function<void(Application&, const MouseEvent&)> handler) {
    mouseHandler_ = std::move(handler);
}

void Application::setCursorHandler(std::function<void(Application&, double, double)> handler) {
    cursorHandler_ = std::move(handler);
}

std::optional<PickResult> Application::pickAt(double x, double y) const {
    if (!window_)
        return {};

    int ww = 0, wh = 0, fw = 0, fh = 0;

    detail::glfw().GetWindowSize(window_, &ww, &wh);
    detail::glfw().GetFramebufferSize(window_, &fw, &fh);

    if (ww <= 0 || wh <= 0 || fw <= 0 || fh <= 0)
        return {};

    return graphics::pick(scene_, camera_, x * fw / ww, y * fh / wh, {0, 0, double(fw), double(fh)});
}

std::optional<PickResult> Application::pickAtCursor() const {
    if (!window_)
        return {};

    double x = 0, y = 0;

    detail::glfw().GetCursorPos(window_, &x, &y);

    return pickAt(x, y);
}

AssetLoader& Application::getAssets() {
    if (!assets_)
        throw std::logic_error("AssetLoader는 run 실행 중 콜백에서만 사용할 수 있습니다.");

    return *assets_;
}
} // namespace graphics

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

자전과 공전을 분리

두 물체에 같은 경로 시계를 연결하되 한 분기만 반사시키는 경우다. MotionId는 시간 상태를 찾고 LayerId는 변환 합성 경로를 찾는다. 같은 모션을 공유하면 위상도 공유한다.

주요 상태: system은 Scene 안의 MotionSystem이다. rootLayer는 삭제할 수 없는 0번 루트다. parent는 변환 트리 부모이며 motion의 World/Local 기여는 합성 위치를 나눈다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

bool connectMotion(graphics::Scene& scene,graphics::EntityId entity) {
    auto& system=scene.getMotions();
    const auto& constSystem=static_cast<const graphics::Scene&>(scene).getMotions(); (void)constSystem;
    graphics::Motion motion;
    graphics::RotationMotion rotation;
    if(!motion.append(graphics::Motion::rotation(rotation))) return false;
    auto id=system.createMotion(motion); if(!id) return false;
    if(!system.setMotion(*id,motion)||!system.seekMotion(*id,1)
       ||!system.setMotionPlaying(*id,false)||!system.setMotionEnabled(*id,true)) return false;
    const auto* state=system.getMotion(*id); if(!state) return false;
    auto layer=system.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer()); if(!layer) return false;
    graphics::LayerConfig config; config.motion=*id;
    if(!system.setLayer(*layer,config)||!system.bindMotion(*layer,*id)) return false;
    auto inserted=system.insertLayer(*layer); if(!inserted) return false;
    if(!system.reparentLayer(*layer,graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer())
       ||!system.removeLayer(*inserted)) return false;
    const auto* layerState=system.getLayer(*layer); (void)layerState;
    auto matrices=system.layerMatrices(*layer); if(!matrices) return false;
    if(!scene.bindEntity(entity,*layer)) return false;
    auto binding=scene.getEntityLayer(entity); (void)binding;
    if(!system.update(.016)||!scene.updateMotions(.016)) return false;
    // 위 두 호출은 수동 진행의 시연이다. Application 루프에서는 자동 진행하므로 호출하지 않는다.
    if(!scene.unbindEntity(entity)||!system.bindMotion(*layer,std::nullopt)) return false;
    if(!system.popLayer(*layer)||!system.removeMotion(*id)) return false;
    auto spin=system.createSpin(rotation);
    auto orbit=system.createOrbit(rotation);
    auto orbitConfig=rotation; orbitConfig.space=graphics::TransformSpace::World;
    auto spinConfig=rotation; spinConfig.space=graphics::TransformSpace::Local;
    auto combined=system.createMotion({graphics::Motion::rotation(orbitConfig), graphics::Motion::rotation(spinConfig)});
    graphics::PathMotion path; path.path=graphics::PathSystem::circle({0,0,0},1);
    auto moving=system.createMotion({graphics::Motion::followPath(path), graphics::Motion::rotation(spinConfig)});
    if(!spin||!orbit||!combined||!moving) return false;
    system.clear(); // 기존 레이어 연결을 사용하지 않는 별도 예제 종료 지점
    return true;
}

playing=false는 시간 정지, enabled=false는 변환 기여 제거다. popLayer는 자식 없는 레이어만 제거한다. removeLayer는 자식을 부모 쪽으로 승격한다. system.clear 전에 Scene 엔티티 바인딩을 해제해야 한다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <iostream>

/// 선택한 경로로 이동 모션을 바꾼다.
bool Console::choosePath(int kind) {
    graphics::PathMotion path;

    path.durationSeconds = 6;

    const glm::vec3 center(1.7f, 0, 0);

    if (kind == 1) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::ellipse(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 2) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::rectangle(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 3) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polygon(
            {{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.4f, 0}, {2.1f, .6f, 0}, {1.4f, .8f, 0}, {.9f, .2f, 0}});
    } else if (kind == 4) {
        path.path = [center](double u) {
            const double angle = u * 6.283185307179586;

            return center +
                   glm::vec3(float(.7 * std::sin(2 * angle)), float(.6 * std::sin(angle)), float(.3 * std::cos(angle)));
        };
        path.interpolation = graphics::PathSystem::smoothStep;
    } else if (kind == 5) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::circle(center, .65f, {0, 0, 1});
    } else if (kind == 6) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polyline({{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.5f, 0}, {2, .6f, 0}});
        path.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;
    } else {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::Motion motion;
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();

    if (!pathId_ || !motion.append(graphics::Motion::followPath(path))) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!motions.setMotion(*pathId_, motion) || !motions.seekMotion(*pathId_, 0)) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "경로 " << kind << ": 시작 시각 0초로 변경\n";

    return true;
}

/// 장면 객체와 초기 상태를 구성한다.
bool Console::initScene() {
    demo_ = Demo::Motion;

    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    paused_ = false;
    spinEnabled_ = true;
    globalEnabled_ = false;
    reflection_ = 1;
    insertedLayer_.reset();

    const auto first = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
    const auto second = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});

    if (!first || !second) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!scene.setTint(*first, {1, .35f, .2f}) || !scene.setTint(*second, {.2f, .7f, 1})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& motions = scene.getMotions();

    pathId_ = motions.createMotion(graphics::Motion{});

    graphics::RotationMotion spin;

    spin.degreesPerSecond = 90;
    spinId_ = motions.createSpin(spin);

    graphics::RotationMotion global;

    global.degreesPerSecond = 15;
    globalId_ = motions.createOrbit(global);

    if (!pathId_ || !spinId_ || !globalId_ || !choosePath(1)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, false) ||
        !motions.bindMotion(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), *globalId_)) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto normalPath = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer());
    const auto reflection = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});

    if (!normalPath || !reflection) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig mirror;

    mirror.transform = *reflection;
    mirrorLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), mirror);

    if (!mirrorLayer_) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto mirroredPath = motions.pushLayer(*mirrorLayer_);

    normalSpinLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(*normalPath);

    if (!mirroredPath || !normalSpinLayer_) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto mirroredSpin = motions.pushLayer(*mirroredPath);

    if (!mirroredSpin || !motions.bindMotion(*normalPath, *pathId_) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredPath, *pathId_) ||
        !motions.bindMotion(*normalSpinLayer_, *spinId_) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredSpin, *spinId_) ||
        !scene.bindEntity(*first, *normalSpinLayer_) || !scene.bindEntity(*second, *mirroredSpin)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *first) ||
        !scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *second) ||
        !scene.addLine({0, -1.4f, 0}, {0, 1.4f, 0}, {.5f, .5f, .5f})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& camera = app_.getCamera();

    camera.position = {5, 3, 8};
    camera.target = {0, 0, 0};
    camera.up = {0, 1, 0};
    printHelp();

    return true;
}

/// 반사 변환을 순서대로 변경한다.
bool Console::changeReflection() {
    const int next = (reflection_ + 1) % 4;
    std::optional<glm::mat4> matrix = glm::mat4(1);

    if (next == 1) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 2) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 3) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectLine(graphics::AxisLine{});
    }

    if (!mirrorLayer_ || !matrix) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig config;

    config.transform = *matrix;

    if (!app_.getScene().getMotions().setLayer(*mirrorLayer_, config)) {
        return false;
    }
    reflection_ = next;

    const char* names[] = {"없음", "YZ평면 반사", "원점 점대칭", "Y축 직선대칭"};

    std::cout << "반사: " << names[reflection_] << '\n';

    return true;
}

/// 크기 레이어를 삽입하거나 제거한다.
bool Console::toggleInsertedLayer() {
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();

    if (insertedLayer_) {
        if (!motions.removeLayer(*insertedLayer_)) {
            return false;
        }
        insertedLayer_.reset();
        std::cout << "추가 크기 레이어 삭제\n";

        return true;
    }

    const auto scale = graphics::TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(1.5f));

    if (!normalSpinLayer_ || !scale) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig config;

    config.space = graphics::TransformSpace::Local;
    config.transform = *scale;
    insertedLayer_ = motions.insertLayer(*normalSpinLayer_, config);

    if (!insertedLayer_) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "첫 객체에 1.5배 Local 크기 레이어 삽입\n";

    return true;
}

/// 키 입력에 따라 동작을 변경한다.
void Console::onKey(int key, int action) {
    if (action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_7 || key == GLFW_KEY_8 || key == GLFW_KEY_9 || key == GLFW_KEY_0) {
        const bool ok = key == GLFW_KEY_7   ? initGrid()
                        : key == GLFW_KEY_8 ? initRoom()
                        : key == GLFW_KEY_9 ? initAnimation()
                                            : initScene();

        if (!ok)
            app_.requestClose();

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_V) {
        threeViews_ = !threeViews_;

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_D) {
        trace_ = !trace_;
        graphics::Diagnostics::configure({true, trace_});

        return;
    }

    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation && handleJointKey(key))
        return;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_F && demo_ == Demo::Room) {
        floorOpen_ = !floorOpen_;

        return;
    }

    if (demo_ != Demo::Motion) {
        if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE)
            clock_.playing = !clock_.playing;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE)
            app_.requestClose();
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H)
            printHelp();

        return;
    }

    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();
    bool ok = true;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
        app_.requestClose();
    } else if (key >= GLFW_KEY_1 && key <= GLFW_KEY_6) {
        ok = choosePath(key - GLFW_KEY_1 + 1);
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE) {
        const bool playing = paused_;

        ok = pathId_ && spinId_ && globalId_ && motions.setMotionPlaying(*pathId_, playing) &&
             motions.setMotionPlaying(*spinId_, playing) && motions.setMotionPlaying(*globalId_, playing);

        if (ok) {
            paused_ = !paused_;
            std::cout << (paused_ ? "시간 정지\n" : "시간 재개\n");
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_S) {
        ok = spinId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*spinId_, !spinEnabled_);

        if (ok) {
            spinEnabled_ = !spinEnabled_;
            std::cout << "자전 기여: " << spinEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_G) {
        ok = globalId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, !globalEnabled_);

        if (ok) {
            globalEnabled_ = !globalEnabled_;
            std::cout << "전체 공전 기여: " << globalEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_M) {
        ok = changeReflection();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_I) {
        ok = toggleInsertedLayer();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H) {
        printHelp();
    }

    if (!ok) {
        std::cerr << "샘플 명령 적용 실패. 장면을 종료합니다.\n";
        app_.requestClose();
    }
}

/// 마우스 버튼 입력을 처리한다.
void Console::onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {
    if (event.button != GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT || event.action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }

    std::optional<graphics::PickResult> hit;

    if (threeViews_) {
        const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

        if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
            return;

        const auto passes =
            graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions());

        for (const auto& pass : passes) {
            const auto& v = pass.viewport;
            const double top = size[1] - v.y - v.height;

            if (event.framebufferX >= v.x && event.framebufferX < v.x + v.width && event.framebufferY >= top &&
                event.framebufferY < top + v.height)
                hit = graphics::pick(app_.getScene(), pass.camera, event.framebufferX, event.framebufferY,
                                     {double(v.x), top, double(v.width), double(v.height)});
        }
    } else
        hit = app_.pickAt(event.windowX, event.windowY);

    if (hit) {
        std::cout << "entity=" << hit->entityId << " face=" << hit->faceIndex << '\n';
    }
}

/// 조작 방법을 출력한다.
void Console::printHelp() const {
    std::cout
        << "standard: 프레임워크 Motion·반사·레이어 샘플\n"
        << "1 타원 / 2 사각형 / 3 다각형 / 4 사용자 3D 경로+보간 / 5 원 / 6 왕복 꺾은선\n"
        << "Space 시간 정지·재개 / S 자전 기여 / G 전체 공전 기여\n"
        << "M 반사 변경 / I 크기 레이어 삽입·삭제 / H 도움말 / Esc 종료\n"
        << "초기 반사: YZ평면. M은 점대칭 -> Y축 직선대칭 -> 없음 -> YZ평면 순환\n"
        << "왼쪽 클릭: 물체의 entity/face ID 출력\n"
        << "7 Grid/거리 기반 배치 / 8 실내 미끄럼·낙하 / 0 기존 Motion 예제\n"
        << "9 선형보간·진자·사람형·그네→사람 계층 애니메이션\n"
        << "9번 관절: Tab/Backspace 선택, X/Y/Z 축, 좌우 회전, 상하 이동, PageUp/Down 피벗, +/- 균일 배율, R 복원\n"
        << "V 3뷰 전환 / D 단계 추적 로그 / F 실내 예제 바닥 개방 / Space 정지·재개\n";
}

/// 거리 기반 격자 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initGrid() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Grid;
    clock_.reset();
    gridBoxes_.clear();

    graphics::GridLayout grid;

    grid.origin = {-3, -.1f, -2};
    grid.extent = {6, .2f, 4};
    grid.cells = {6, 1, 4};

    for (int z = 0; z < 4; ++z)
        for (int x = 0; x < 6; ++x) {
            const auto center = grid.cellCenter({x, 0, z}), size = grid.relativeSize({.15f, 1, .22f});

            if (!center || !size ||
                !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, *size, *center,
                                                 (x + z) % 2 ? graphics::GlColor{.25f, .3f, .4f}
                                                             : graphics::GlColor{.4f, .45f, .55f}))
                return false;
        }

    if (!distancePath_.setPoints({{-2.5f, .35f, -1.5f}, {2.5f, .35f, -1.5f}, {2.5f, .35f, 1.5f}}))
        return false;

    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        const auto box =
            graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .5f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {.25f + .14f * i, .7f - .1f * i, .3f});

        if (!box || !graphics::PatternSystem::addNumber(scene, i + 1, {-.07f, -.1f, .205f}, .2f, {1, 1, 1}, *box))
            return false;
        gridBoxes_.push_back(*box);
    }
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {7, 6, 8};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 0, 0};
    app_.getCamera().orthographicHalfExtent = 4;

    return true;
}

/// 회전하는 방 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initRoom() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Room;
    clock_.reset();
    floorOpen_ = false;

    const auto room = graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene, {{-2, -1, -2}, {2, 2, 2}});
    const auto cube = graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.5f, .5f, .5f}, {0, -.75f, 0}, {1, .7f, .2f});

    if (!room || !cube)
        return false;
    room_ = *room;
    roomCube_ = *cube;
    roomBody_ = graphics::RoomBody{};
    roomBody_.localPosition = {0, -.75f, 0};
    roomBody_.halfExtent = {.25f, .25f, .25f};
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {5, 3, 8};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 0, 0};

    return true;
}

/// 선형보간과 그네·사람의 부모·자식 애니메이션을 구성한다.
bool Console::initAnimation() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Animation;
    clock_.reset();
    animation_.clear();
    jointControls_.clear();
    selectedJoint_ = 0;
    selectedAxis_ = 0;

    if (!graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {8, .15f, 6}, {0, -.15f, 0}, {.25f, .3f, .35f}))
        return false;

    graphics::SwingConfig swingConfig;

    swingConfig.pivot = {1, 3, 0};
    swingConfig.pendulum.amplitudeDegrees = 25;
    swingConfig.pendulum.periodSeconds = 3;

    const auto swing = graphics::AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider(scene, swingConfig);

    if (!swing)
        return false;
    animation_ = swing->animation;

    graphics::HumanoidConfig personConfig;

    personConfig.grid.frame = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), glm::vec3(-2, 0, 0));

    const auto person = graphics::AnimationSystem::addHumanoid(scene, personConfig);

    if (!person)
        return false;

    auto pose = graphics::HumanoidPose::seated();

    pose.hips = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};
    pose.knees = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};

    if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid(scene, *person, pose))
        return false;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        graphics::PendulumMotion sway;

        sway.amplitudeDegrees = 30;
        sway.periodSeconds = 2;
        sway.phaseDegrees = i == 0 ? 0 : 180;

        graphics::Motion motion;

        if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::pendulum(sway)))
            return false;

        const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, person->shoulders[i], motion);

        if (!track)
            return false;
        animation_.push_back(*track);
    }

    const auto marker = scene.addGroup();

    if (!marker || !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .4f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {1, .85f, .15f}, *marker))
        return false;

    graphics::LinearMotion move;

    move.from = {-2, .3f, 2};
    move.to = {2, .3f, 2};
    move.durationSeconds = 4;
    move.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;

    graphics::Motion motion;

    if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::linear(move)))
        return false;

    const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, *marker, motion);

    if (!track)
        return false;
    animation_.push_back(*track);

    const auto control = [&](graphics::EntityId id, const std::string& name) {
        std::size_t index = 0;

        while (index < animation_.size() && animation_[index].joint != id)
            ++index;

        if (index == animation_.size()) {
            auto fixed = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, id, graphics::Motion{});

            if (!fixed)
                return false;
            animation_.push_back(*fixed);
        }
        jointControls_.push_back({index, name, animation_[index].rest});

        return true;
    };

    const auto personControls = [&](const graphics::HumanoidRig& rig, const std::string& prefix) {
        if (!control(rig.root, prefix + " root") || !control(rig.pelvis, prefix + " pelvis") ||
            !control(rig.torso, prefix + " torso") || !control(rig.neck, prefix + " neck"))
            return false;

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
            const auto side = prefix + (i == 0 ? " left " : " right ");

            if (!control(rig.shoulders[i], side + "shoulder") || !control(rig.elbows[i], side + "elbow") ||
                !control(rig.hands[i], side + "hand") || !control(rig.hips[i], side + "hip") ||
                !control(rig.knees[i], side + "knee") || !control(rig.feet[i], side + "foot"))
                return false;
        }

        return true;
    };

    if (!control(swing->frame, "swing frame") || !control(swing->hinge, "swing hinge") ||
        !control(swing->seat, "swing seat") || !personControls(swing->person, "rider") ||
        !personControls(*person, "standing"))
        return false;
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {8, 5, 10};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 1.4f, 0};
    std::cout << "9: 노란 상자는 4초 편도 선형보간, 그네는 3초 주기 진자, 좌석의 사람은 계층 변환을 따릅니다.\n";
    printSelectedJoint();

    return graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, 0);
}

/// 선택한 관절의 기준 자세를 편집하고 같은 시각의 애니메이션에 반영한다.
bool Console::handleJointKey(int key) {
    if (jointControls_.empty())
        return false;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_TAB)
        selectedJoint_ = (selectedJoint_ + 1) % jointControls_.size();
    else if (key == GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE)
        selectedJoint_ = (selectedJoint_ + jointControls_.size() - 1) % jointControls_.size();
    else if (key == GLFW_KEY_X || key == GLFW_KEY_Y || key == GLFW_KEY_Z)
        selectedAxis_ = key == GLFW_KEY_X ? 0 : key == GLFW_KEY_Y ? 1 : 2;
    else {
        auto& track = animation_[jointControls_[selectedJoint_].track];
        const auto saved = track.rest;

        if (key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT || key == GLFW_KEY_RIGHT) {
            glm::vec3 axis(0);

            axis[selectedAxis_] = 1;
            track.rest.rotation =
                track.rest.rotation *
                glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), glm::radians(key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT ? -5.0f : 5.0f), axis));
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_UP || key == GLFW_KEY_DOWN)
            track.rest.basis[selectedAxis_] += key == GLFW_KEY_UP ? .05f : -.05f;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP || key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_DOWN)
            track.rest.localBasis[selectedAxis_] += key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP ? .05f : -.05f;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD || key == GLFW_KEY_MINUS ||
                 key == GLFW_KEY_KP_SUBTRACT) {
            const bool grow = key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD;

            track.rest.scale = glm::vec3(std::clamp(track.rest.scale.x * (grow ? 1.1f : 1 / 1.1f), .1f, 3.0f));
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_R)
            track.rest = jointControls_[selectedJoint_].initialRest;
        else
            return false;

        if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(app_.getScene(), animation_, clock_.seconds)) {
            track.rest = saved;
            std::cerr << "관절 편집 거절: 기준 자세를 복원했습니다.\n";
        }
    }
    printSelectedJoint();

    return true;
}

/// 선택 관절·축·이동·피벗·균일 배율을 출력한다.
void Console::printSelectedJoint() const {
    if (jointControls_.empty())
        return;

    const auto& control = jointControls_[selectedJoint_];
    const auto& rest = animation_[control.track].rest;

    std::cout << "joint " << selectedJoint_ + 1 << '/' << jointControls_.size() << ": " << control.name
              << " axis=" << "XYZ"[selectedAxis_] << " position=" << rest.basis.x << ',' << rest.basis.y << ','
              << rest.basis.z << " pivot=" << rest.localBasis.x << ',' << rest.localBasis.y << ',' << rest.localBasis.z
              << " scale=" << rest.scale.x << '\n';
}

/// 프레임 시간으로 상태와 장면을 갱신한다.
void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    if (demo_ == Demo::Motion || !clock_.playing)
        return;

    if (!clock_.advance(dt)) {
        app_.requestClose();

        return;
    }

    auto& scene = app_.getScene();
    bool ok = true;

    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation) {
        ok = graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, clock_.seconds);
    } else if (demo_ == Demo::Grid) {
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < gridBoxes_.size(); ++i)
            ok = graphics::PatternSystem::follow(scene, gridBoxes_[i], distancePath_,
                                                 clock_.seconds + distancePath_.length() * i / 5) &&
                 ok;
    } else {
        const float angle = .4f * float(std::sin(clock_.seconds));
        const auto matrix = glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), angle, glm::vec3(0, 0, 1));
        graphics::Transform3D roomTransform;

        roomTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix);
        roomTransform.basis = glm::vec3(matrix * glm::vec4(0, .5f, 0, 1));
        ok =
            scene.setTransform(room_, roomTransform) && scene.setFaceVisible(room_, 5, !floorOpen_) &&
            graphics::CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(roomBody_, {{-2, -1, -2}, {2, 2, 2}}, matrix, dt, floorOpen_, false,
                                                    {0, -9.8f, 0}, {0, 0, .4f * float(std::cos(clock_.seconds))});

        graphics::Transform3D cubeTransform;

        cubeTransform.basis = roomBody_.position(matrix);

        if (!roomBody_.released)
            cubeTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix);
        ok = scene.setTransform(roomCube_, cubeTransform) && ok;
    }

    if (!ok)
        app_.requestClose();
}

/// 현재 장면을 지정한 뷰로 렌더링한다.
bool Console::onRender() {
    if (!threeViews_)
        return app_.renderScene();

    const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

    if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
        return true;

    return app_.renderViews(
        graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions()));
}
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/examples/motion.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include <iostream>

int main() {
    using namespace graphics;

    Application app;
    std::optional<MotionId> pathId, spinId, globalId;
    std::optional<LayerId> mirrorLayer, normalSpinLayer, insertedLayer;
    bool paused = false, spinEnabled = true, globalEnabled = false;
    int reflection = 1;

    const auto choosePath = [&](int kind) {
        PathMotion path;

        path.durationSeconds = 6;

        const glm::vec3 center(1.7f, 0, 0);

        if (kind == 1)
            path.path = PathSystem::ellipse(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
        else if (kind == 2)
            path.path = PathSystem::rectangle(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
        else if (kind == 3)
            path.path =
                PathSystem::polygon({{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.4f, 0}, {2.1f, .6f, 0}, {1.4f, .8f, 0}, {.9f, .2f, 0}});
        else if (kind == 4) {
            path.path = [center](double u) {
                const double angle = u * 6.283185307179586;

                return center + glm::vec3(float(.7 * std::sin(2 * angle)), float(.6 * std::sin(angle)),
                                          float(.3 * std::cos(angle)));
            };
            path.interpolation = PathSystem::smoothStep;
        } else if (kind == 5)
            path.path = PathSystem::circle(center, .65f, {0, 0, 1});
        else {
            path.path = PathSystem::polyline({{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.5f, 0}, {2, .6f, 0}});
            path.playback = Playback::PingPong;
        }

        Motion motion;
        auto& motions = app.getScene().getMotions();

        return pathId && motion.append(Motion::followPath(path)) && motions.setMotion(*pathId, motion) &&
               motions.seekMotion(*pathId, 0);
    };

    app.setInitHandler([&](Application& current) {
        auto& scene = current.getScene();
        auto& motions = scene.getMotions();
        const auto first = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
        const auto second = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});

        if (!first || !second)
            return false;

        if (!scene.setTint(*first, {1, .35f, .2f}) || !scene.setTint(*second, {.2f, .7f, 1}))
            return false;
        pathId = motions.createMotion(Motion{});

        RotationMotion spin;

        spin.degreesPerSecond = 90;
        spinId = motions.createSpin(spin);

        RotationMotion global;

        global.degreesPerSecond = 15;
        globalId = motions.createOrbit(global);

        if (!pathId || !spinId || !globalId || !choosePath(1))
            return false;

        if (!motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId, false) || !motions.bindMotion(MotionSystem::rootLayer(), *globalId))
            return false;

        const auto normalPath = motions.pushLayer(MotionSystem::rootLayer());
        const auto reflectionMatrix = TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});

        if (!normalPath || !reflectionMatrix)
            return false;

        LayerConfig mirror;

        mirror.transform = *reflectionMatrix;
        mirrorLayer = motions.pushLayer(MotionSystem::rootLayer(), mirror);

        if (!mirrorLayer)
            return false;

        const auto mirroredPath = motions.pushLayer(*mirrorLayer);

        normalSpinLayer = motions.pushLayer(*normalPath);

        if (!mirroredPath || !normalSpinLayer)
            return false;

        const auto mirroredSpin = motions.pushLayer(*mirroredPath);

        if (!mirroredSpin || !motions.bindMotion(*normalPath, *pathId) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredPath, *pathId) ||
            !motions.bindMotion(*normalSpinLayer, *spinId) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredSpin, *spinId) ||
            !scene.bindEntity(*first, *normalSpinLayer) || !scene.bindEntity(*second, *mirroredSpin))
            return false;

        if (!scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *first) ||
            !scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *second) ||
            !scene.addLine({0, -1.4f, 0}, {0, 1.4f, 0}, {.5f, .5f, .5f}))
            return false;
        current.getCamera().position = {5, 3, 8};
        std::cout << "1 타원, 2 사각형, 3 다각형, 4 사용자 경로+보간, 5 원, 6 왕복 꺾은선\n"
                  << "Space 시간 정지/재개, S 자전 표시, G 전체 공전, M 반사 변경, I 레이어 삽입/삭제\n"
                  << "M: 없음 -> YZ평면 반사 -> 점대칭 -> Y직선 대칭 순환. 클릭: 객체 ID. Esc 종료\n";

        return true;
    });
    app.setKeyHandler([&](Application& current, int key, int action) {
        if (action != GLFW_PRESS)
            return;

        auto& motions = current.getScene().getMotions();
        bool ok = true;

        if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE)
            current.requestClose();
        else if (key >= GLFW_KEY_1 && key <= GLFW_KEY_6)
            ok = choosePath(key - GLFW_KEY_1 + 1);
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE) {
            paused = !paused;
            ok = motions.setMotionPlaying(*pathId, !paused) && motions.setMotionPlaying(*spinId, !paused) &&
                 motions.setMotionPlaying(*globalId, !paused);
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_S) {
            spinEnabled = !spinEnabled;
            ok = motions.setMotionEnabled(*spinId, spinEnabled);
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_G) {
            globalEnabled = !globalEnabled;
            ok = motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId, globalEnabled);
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_M) {
            reflection = (reflection + 1) % 4;

            std::optional<glm::mat4> matrix = glm::mat4(1);

            if (reflection == 1)
                matrix = TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});

            if (reflection == 2)
                matrix = TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0, 0, 0});

            if (reflection == 3)
                matrix = TransformSystem::reflectLine(AxisLine{});

            LayerConfig config;

            if (!matrix)
                ok = false;
            else {
                config.transform = *matrix;
                ok = motions.setLayer(*mirrorLayer, config);
            }
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_I) {
            if (insertedLayer) {
                ok = motions.removeLayer(*insertedLayer);

                if (ok)
                    insertedLayer.reset();
            } else {
                LayerConfig config;

                config.space = TransformSpace::Local;

                const auto scale = TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(1.5f));

                if (!scale)
                    ok = false;
                else {
                    config.transform = *scale;
                    insertedLayer = motions.insertLayer(*normalSpinLayer, config);
                    ok = insertedLayer.has_value();
                }
            }
        }

        if (!ok)
            current.requestClose();
    });
    app.setMouseHandler([](Application& current, const MouseEvent& event) {
        if (event.button != GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT || event.action != GLFW_PRESS)
            return;

        const auto hit = current.pickAt(event.windowX, event.windowY);

        if (hit)
            std::cout << "entity=" << hit->entityId << " face=" << hit->faceIndex << '\n';
    });

    ApplicationConfig config;

    config.title = "Motion and layer tree";

    return app.run(config);
}

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

경로 전환·왕복

공이 원 경로를 따라 이동하고 한 축을 중심으로 도는 상황이다. TransformSystem은 행렬을 계산하고 PathSystem은 진행률을 위치로 바꾸는 함수를 만든다. 이 계산만으로 Scene이 변경되지는 않는다.

주요 상태: axis.point는 회전축 위의 한 점, direction은 축 방향이다. u는 시간 자체가 아니라 경로 진행률이다. smoothStep은 u를 느리게 시작·끝나는 진행률로 바꾼다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

void calculateTransforms() {
    graphics::AxisLine axis{{0,0,0},{0,1,0}};
    auto t=graphics::TransformSystem::translation({1,0,0});
    auto s=graphics::TransformSystem::scaling({2,1,1},{0,0,0});
    auto r=graphics::TransformSystem::rotation(axis,90);
    auto point=graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0,0,0});
    auto line=graphics::TransformSystem::reflectLine(axis);
    auto plane=graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0,0,0},{1,0,0});
    if(!t||!s||!r||!point||!line||!plane) return;
    bool affine=graphics::TransformSystem::validAffine(*t * *r * *s); (void)affine;
    auto circle=graphics::PathSystem::circle({0,0,0},2);
    auto ellipse=graphics::PathSystem::ellipse({0,0,0},{2,0,0},{0,0,1});
    auto polyline=graphics::PathSystem::polyline({{0,0,0},{1,0,0},{1,0,1}});
    auto polygon=graphics::PathSystem::polygon({{0,0,0},{1,0,0},{0,0,1}});
    auto rectangle=graphics::PathSystem::rectangle({0,0,0},{1,0,0},{0,0,1});
    if(!circle||!ellipse||!polyline||!polygon||!rectangle) return;
    auto u=graphics::PathSystem::linear(.25);
    auto eased=graphics::PathSystem::smoothStep(u);
    glm::vec3 position=circle(eased); (void)position;
}

optional 행렬이 비어 있거나 경로 std::function이 비면 입력이 유효하지 않다. 0 길이 축이나 0 반지름을 넘긴 실패를 identity 행렬 성공으로 간주하지 않는다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/detail/Transform.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include "Internal.h"
#include <algorithm>

namespace graphics {
namespace detail {
std::optional<glm::vec3> motionUnitVector(const glm::vec3& direction) {
    if (!finiteVector(direction))
        return {};

    const double length = glm::length(glm::dvec3(direction));

    if (!std::isfinite(length) || length <= 1e-12)
        return {};

    return glm::vec3(glm::dvec3(direction) / length);
}

std::optional<glm::mat4> motionAround(const glm::vec3& center, const glm::mat4& matrix) {
    if (!finiteVector(center))
        return {};

    const auto result = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), center) * matrix * glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), -center);

    return TransformSystem::validAffine(result) ? std::optional<glm::mat4>(result) : std::nullopt;
}

bool motionPlane(const glm::vec3& u, const glm::vec3& v) {
    const auto a = motionUnitVector(u), b = motionUnitVector(v);

    return a && b && glm::length(glm::cross(*a, *b)) > 1e-6f;
}
} // namespace detail

bool TransformSystem::validAffine(const glm::mat4& m) {
    return detail::finiteMatrix(m) && m[0][3] == 0 && m[1][3] == 0 && m[2][3] == 0 && m[3][3] == 1;
}

std::optional<glm::mat4> TransformSystem::translation(const glm::vec3& offset) {
    if (!detail::finiteVector(offset))
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "TransformSystem::translation",
                                "Rejected condition: !detail::finiteVector(offset)",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            std::nullopt);

    return glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), offset);
}

std::optional<glm::mat4> TransformSystem::scaling(const glm::vec3& factors, const glm::vec3& center) {
    if (!detail::finiteVector(factors))
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "TransformSystem::scaling",
                                "Rejected condition: !detail::finiteVector(factors)",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            std::nullopt);

    return detail::motionAround(center, glm::scale(glm::mat4(1), factors));
}

std::optional<glm::mat4> TransformSystem::rotation(const AxisLine& axis, double degrees) {
    const auto unit = detail::motionUnitVector(axis.direction);

    if (!unit || !std::isfinite(degrees))
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "TransformSystem::rotation",
                                "Rejected condition: !unit || !std::isfinite(degrees)",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            std::nullopt);

    const float radians = static_cast<float>(std::remainder(degrees, 360.0) * 3.14159265358979323846 / 180.0);

    return detail::motionAround(axis.point, glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), radians, *unit));
}

std::optional<glm::mat4> TransformSystem::reflectPoint(const glm::vec3& center) {
    return scaling(glm::vec3(-1), center);
}

std::optional<glm::mat4> TransformSystem::reflectLine(const AxisLine& line) {
    const auto unit = detail::motionUnitVector(line.direction);

    if (!unit)
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "TransformSystem::reflectLine",
                                "Rejected condition: !unit",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            std::nullopt);

    glm::mat3 matrix(0);

    for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c)
        for (int r = 0; r < 3; ++r)
            matrix[c][r] = 2 * (*unit)[c] * (*unit)[r] - (c == r ? 1.0f : 0.0f);

    return detail::motionAround(line.point, glm::mat4(matrix));
}

std::optional<glm::mat4> TransformSystem::reflectPlane(const glm::vec3& point, const glm::vec3& normal) {
    const auto unit = detail::motionUnitVector(normal);

    if (!unit)
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "TransformSystem::reflectPlane",
                                "Rejected condition: !unit",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            std::nullopt);

    glm::mat3 matrix(1);

    for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c)
        for (int r = 0; r < 3; ++r)
            matrix[c][r] -= 2 * (*unit)[c] * (*unit)[r];

    return detail::motionAround(point, glm::mat4(matrix));
}

PathFunction PathSystem::ellipse(const glm::vec3& center, const glm::vec3& u, const glm::vec3& v) {
    if (!detail::finiteVector(center) || !detail::motionPlane(u, v))
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::ellipse",
                                "Rejected condition: !detail::finiteVector(center) || !detail::motionPlane(u, v)",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            PathFunction{});

    return [center, u, v](double progress) {
        const double angle = progress * 6.28318530717958647692;

        return center + u * static_cast<float>(std::cos(angle)) + v * static_cast<float>(std::sin(angle));
    };
}

PathFunction PathSystem::circle(const glm::vec3& center, float radius, const glm::vec3& normal) {
    const auto n = detail::motionUnitVector(normal);

    if (!n || !std::isfinite(radius) || radius <= 0)
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::circle",
                                "Rejected condition: !n || !std::isfinite(radius) || radius <= 0",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            PathFunction{});

    const auto reference = std::abs(n->x) < .9f ? glm::vec3(1, 0, 0) : glm::vec3(0, 0, 1);
    const auto u = glm::normalize(glm::cross(reference, *n));

    return ellipse(center, radius * u, radius * glm::cross(*n, u));
}

PathFunction PathSystem::polyline(std::vector<glm::vec3> points, bool closed) {
    if (points.size() < 2)
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::polyline",
                                "Rejected condition: points.size() < 2",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            PathFunction{});

    for (const auto& p : points)
        if (!detail::finiteVector(p))
            return (Diagnostics::failAt(
                        __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::polyline",
                        "Rejected condition: !detail::finiteVector(p)",
                        "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
                    PathFunction{});

    if (closed) {
        if (glm::length(glm::dvec3(points.back()) - glm::dvec3(points.front())) > 1e-12)
            points.push_back(points.front());

        if (points.size() < 4)
            return (Diagnostics::failAt(
                        __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::polyline",
                        "Rejected condition: points.size() < 4",
                        "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
                    PathFunction{});
    }

    std::vector<double> distances{0};

    for (std::size_t i = 1; i < points.size(); ++i) {
        const double length = glm::length(glm::dvec3(points[i]) - glm::dvec3(points[i - 1]));

        if (!std::isfinite(length) || length <= 1e-12)
            return (Diagnostics::failAt(
                        __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::polyline",
                        "Rejected condition: !std::isfinite(length) || length <= 1e-12",
                        "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
                    PathFunction{});
        distances.push_back(distances.back() + length);
    }

    return [points = std::move(points), distances = std::move(distances)](double progress) {
        const double distance = std::clamp(progress, 0.0, 1.0) * distances.back();
        auto end = std::upper_bound(distances.begin(), distances.end(), distance);
        const std::size_t i = end == distances.end() ? distances.size() - 1 : std::size_t(end - distances.begin());
        const double weight = (distance - distances[i - 1]) / (distances[i] - distances[i - 1]);

        return glm::vec3(glm::dvec3(points[i - 1]) * (1 - weight) + glm::dvec3(points[i]) * weight);
    };
}

PathFunction PathSystem::polygon(std::vector<glm::vec3> points) {
    return polyline(std::move(points), true);
}

PathFunction PathSystem::rectangle(const glm::vec3& center, const glm::vec3& u, const glm::vec3& v) {
    if (!detail::finiteVector(center) || !detail::motionPlane(u, v))
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::rectangle",
                                "Rejected condition: !detail::finiteVector(center) || !detail::motionPlane(u, v)",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            PathFunction{});

    const auto a = detail::motionUnitVector(u), b = detail::motionUnitVector(v);

    if (std::abs(glm::dot(*a, *b)) > 1e-5f)
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::rectangle",
                                "Rejected condition: std::abs(glm::dot(*a, *b)) > 1e-5f",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            PathFunction{});

    return polygon({center - u - v, center + u - v, center + u + v, center - u + v});
}

double PathSystem::linear(double progress) {
    return progress;
}

double PathSystem::smoothStep(double progress) {
    return progress * progress * (3 - 2 * progress);
}
} // namespace graphics

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

순차 동작과 끝 자세 유지

상자가 2초 이동한 뒤 1초 회전하는 모션을 편집한다. append는 단계를 배열에 더할 뿐 시작 시간을 뒤로 밀지 않는다. 순차 실행은 then을 사용한다.

주요 상태: Motion은 단계들의 값 묶음이다. MotionStep의 콜백이 받는 초는 해당 단계가 시작된 이후의 시간이다. sample(2.5)는 dt=.5가 아니라 모션 시작 후 2.5초의 자세를 요구한다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

std::optional<graphics::Motion> makeMotion() {
    graphics::Motion motion;
    graphics::LinearMotion move; move.to={2,0,0}; move.durationSeconds=2;
    if(!motion.append(graphics::Motion::linear(move))) return {};
    graphics::RotationMotion spin; spin.degreesPerSecond=90;
    auto rotate=graphics::Motion::rotation(spin); rotate.durationSeconds=1;
    if(!motion.then(rotate)) return {};
    auto fixed=graphics::Motion::fixed(glm::mat4(1));
    if(!motion.insert(0,fixed)||!motion.replace(0,fixed)||!motion.erase(0)) return {};
    const auto& steps=motion.getSteps(); (void)steps;
    auto pose=motion.sample(2.5); if(!pose) return {};
    graphics::PathMotion path; path.path=graphics::PathSystem::circle({0,0,0},1);
    graphics::Motion looping;
    if(!looping.append(graphics::Motion::followPath(path))) return {};
    graphics::PendulumMotion sway; sway.periodSeconds=3;
    graphics::Motion pendulum;
    if(!pendulum.append(graphics::Motion::pendulum(sway))) return {};
    return motion;
}

유한 종료 시간이 없는 루프 단계 뒤에는 then을 사용할 수 없다. Once 단계는 종료 자세를 유지한다. 진자는 중력 적분이 아니라 지정 주기의 sin 각도 함수다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/detail/Motion.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include "Internal.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace graphics {
bool Motion::validStep(const MotionStep& step) {
    return (bool(step.transform) != bool(step.action)) && std::isfinite(step.startSeconds) && step.startSeconds >= 0 &&
           (!step.durationSeconds || (std::isfinite(*step.durationSeconds) && *step.durationSeconds >= 0 &&
                                      std::isfinite(step.startSeconds + *step.durationSeconds)));
}

bool Motion::append(const MotionStep& step) {
    return insert(steps_.size(), step);
}

bool Motion::insert(std::size_t index, const MotionStep& step) {
    if (index > steps_.size() || !validStep(step))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::insert",
            "Rejected condition: index > steps_.size() || !validStep(step)",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
    steps_.insert(steps_.begin() + index, step);

    return true;
}

MotionStep Motion::action(MotionAction callback, double startSeconds, std::optional<double> durationSeconds) {
    MotionStep step;
    step.action = std::move(callback);
    step.startSeconds = startSeconds;
    step.durationSeconds = durationSeconds;
    return step;
}

bool Motion::insertAction(std::size_t index, MotionAction callback, double startSeconds,
                          std::optional<double> durationSeconds) {
    return insert(index, action(std::move(callback), startSeconds, durationSeconds));
}

bool Motion::replace(std::size_t index, const MotionStep& step) {
    if (index >= steps_.size() || !validStep(step))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::replace",
            "Rejected condition: index >= steps_.size() || !validStep(step)",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
    steps_[index] = step;

    return true;
}

bool Motion::erase(std::size_t index) {
    if (index >= steps_.size())
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::erase", "Rejected condition: index >= steps_.size()",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
    steps_.erase(steps_.begin() + index);

    return true;
}

bool Motion::then(MotionStep step) {
    double end = 0;

    for (const auto& current : steps_) {
        if (!current.durationSeconds)
            return Diagnostics::failAt(
                __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::then",
                "Rejected condition: !current.durationSeconds",
                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
        end = std::max(end, current.startSeconds + *current.durationSeconds);
    }
    step.startSeconds += end;

    return append(step);
}

std::optional<MotionSample> Motion::sample(double seconds) const {
    return evaluate(seconds, false);
}

std::optional<MotionSample> Motion::execute(double seconds) const {
    // Snapshot permits callbacks to edit the original sequence without invalidating iteration.
    const Motion snapshot = *this;
    return snapshot.evaluate(seconds, true);
}

std::optional<MotionSample> Motion::evaluate(double seconds, bool actions) const {
    if (!std::isfinite(seconds) || seconds < 0)
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::sample",
                                "Rejected condition: !std::isfinite(seconds) || seconds < 0",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            std::nullopt);

    MotionSample result;

    for (const auto& step : steps_) {
        if (!step.enabled || seconds < step.startSeconds)
            continue;

        double time = seconds - step.startSeconds;

        if (step.durationSeconds)
            time = std::min(time, *step.durationSeconds);

        if (step.action) {
            if (actions && !step.action(time, result))
                return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::execute",
                                            "Action returned false; prior external effects are retained."), std::nullopt);
            continue;
        }
        const auto matrix = step.transform(time);

        if (!TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix))
            return (Diagnostics::failAt(
                        __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::sample",
                        "Rejected condition: !TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix)",
                        "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
                    std::nullopt);

        auto& target = step.space == TransformSpace::World ? result.parent : result.local;

        target = matrix * target; // Within each space, API order is application order.

        if (!TransformSystem::validAffine(target))
            return (Diagnostics::failAt(
                        __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::sample",
                        "Rejected condition: !TransformSystem::validAffine(target)",
                        "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
                    std::nullopt);
    }

    return result;
}

MotionStep Motion::fixed(const glm::mat4& matrix, TransformSpace space) {
    MotionStep step;

    if (TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix))
        step.transform = [matrix](double) {
            return matrix;
        };
    step.space = space;
    step.durationSeconds = 0;

    return step;
}

MotionStep Motion::rotation(const RotationMotion& config) {
    MotionStep step;

    if (!std::isfinite(config.degreesPerSecond) || !std::isfinite(config.initialDegrees) ||
        !TransformSystem::rotation(config.axis, config.initialDegrees))
        return step;
    step.space = config.space;
    step.transform = [config](double seconds) {
        const auto matrix =
            TransformSystem::rotation(config.axis, config.initialDegrees + config.degreesPerSecond * seconds);

        if (!matrix)
            throw std::domain_error("Rotation motion produced an invalid matrix.");

        return *matrix;
    };

    return step;
}

MotionStep Motion::followPath(const PathMotion& config) {
    MotionStep step;

    if (!config.path || !config.interpolation || !std::isfinite(config.durationSeconds) ||
        config.durationSeconds <= 0 || !std::isfinite(config.rate) || config.rate == 0 ||
        !std::isfinite(config.phase) || config.phase < 0 || config.phase > 1)
        return step;
    step.space = config.space;

    if (config.playback == Playback::Once) {
        step.durationSeconds =
            (config.rate > 0 ? 1 - config.phase : config.phase) * config.durationSeconds / std::abs(config.rate);

        if (!std::isfinite(*step.durationSeconds))
            return MotionStep{};
    }
    step.transform = [config](double seconds) {
        double progress = config.phase + seconds / config.durationSeconds * config.rate;

        if (!std::isfinite(progress))
            throw std::domain_error("Path time overflow.");

        if (config.playback == Playback::Once)
            progress = std::clamp(progress, 0.0, 1.0);
        else if (config.playback == Playback::Loop)
            progress -= std::floor(progress);
        else {
            progress -= std::floor(progress / 2) * 2;

            if (progress > 1)
                progress = 2 - progress;
        }

        const double u = config.interpolation(progress);

        if (!std::isfinite(u))
            throw std::domain_error("Interpolation returned non-finite progress.");

        glm::vec3 offset = config.path(u);

        if (config.relativeToStart) {
            const double initial = config.interpolation(config.phase);

            if (!std::isfinite(initial))
                throw std::domain_error("Invalid initial interpolation.");
            offset -= config.path(initial);
        }

        const auto matrix = TransformSystem::translation(offset);

        if (!matrix)
            throw std::domain_error("Path returned a non-finite position.");

        return *matrix;
    };

    return step;
}

MotionStep Motion::linear(const LinearMotion& config) {
    if (!detail::finiteVector(config.from) || !detail::finiteVector(config.to) ||
        !std::isfinite(config.durationSeconds) || config.durationSeconds <= 0 ||
        (config.playback != Playback::Once && config.playback != Playback::Loop &&
         config.playback != Playback::PingPong) ||
        (config.space != TransformSpace::World && config.space != TransformSpace::Local))
        return {};

    PathMotion path;

    path.path = [from = glm::dvec3(config.from), to = glm::dvec3(config.to)](double u) {
        return glm::vec3((1 - u) * from + u * to);
    };
    path.durationSeconds = config.durationSeconds;
    path.playback = config.playback;
    path.space = config.space;

    return followPath(path);
}

MotionStep Motion::pendulum(const PendulumMotion& config) {
    MotionStep step;

    if (!std::isfinite(config.amplitudeDegrees) || config.amplitudeDegrees < 0 ||
        !std::isfinite(config.periodSeconds) || config.periodSeconds <= 0 || !std::isfinite(config.phaseDegrees) ||
        !std::isfinite(config.offsetDegrees) ||
        !std::isfinite(std::abs(config.offsetDegrees) + config.amplitudeDegrees) ||
        (config.space != TransformSpace::World && config.space != TransformSpace::Local) ||
        !TransformSystem::rotation(config.axis, config.offsetDegrees))
        return step;
    step.space = config.space;
    step.transform = [config](double seconds) {
        constexpr double pi = 3.14159265358979323846;
        const double phase = std::remainder(seconds, config.periodSeconds) / config.periodSeconds * 2 * pi +
                             std::remainder(config.phaseDegrees, 360.0) * pi / 180;
        const auto matrix =
            TransformSystem::rotation(config.axis, config.offsetDegrees + config.amplitudeDegrees * std::sin(phase));

        if (!matrix)
            throw std::domain_error("Pendulum motion produced an invalid matrix.");

        return *matrix;
    };

    return step;
}

std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createMotion(const Motion& motion) {
    const auto sample = motion.sample(0);

    if (!sample || nextMotion_ == std::numeric_limits<MotionId>::max())
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createMotion",
                                "Rejected condition: !sample || nextMotion_ == std::numeric_limits<MotionId>::max()",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            std::nullopt);

    const auto id = nextMotion_;
    MotionState state;

    state.motion = motion;
    state.sampled = *sample;
    motions_.emplace(id, std::move(state));
    ++nextMotion_;

    return id;
}

const MotionState* MotionSystem::getMotion(MotionId id) const {
    const auto found = motions_.find(id);

    return found == motions_.end() ? nullptr : &found->second;
}

bool MotionSystem::setMotion(MotionId id, const Motion& motion) {
    const auto found = motions_.find(id);

    if (found == motions_.end())
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setMotion",
            "Rejected condition: found == motions_.end()",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);

    const auto sample = motion.sample(found->second.timeSeconds);

    if (!sample)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setMotion", "Rejected condition: !sample",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    found->second.motion = motion;
    found->second.sampled = *sample;

    return true;
}

bool MotionSystem::setMotionPlaying(MotionId id, bool playing) {
    const auto found = motions_.find(id);

    if (found == motions_.end())
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setMotionPlaying",
            "Rejected condition: found == motions_.end()",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    found->second.playing = playing;

    return true;
}

bool MotionSystem::setMotionEnabled(MotionId id, bool enabled) {
    const auto found = motions_.find(id);

    if (found == motions_.end())
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setMotionEnabled",
            "Rejected condition: found == motions_.end()",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    found->second.enabled = enabled;

    return true;
}

bool MotionSystem::seekMotion(MotionId id, double seconds) {
    const auto found = motions_.find(id);

    if (found == motions_.end())
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::seekMotion",
            "Rejected condition: found == motions_.end()",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);

    const auto sample = found->second.motion.sample(seconds);

    if (!sample)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::seekMotion", "Rejected condition: !sample",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    found->second.timeSeconds = seconds;
    found->second.sampled = *sample;

    return true;
}

bool MotionSystem::removeMotion(MotionId id) {
    if (!motions_.erase(id))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::removeMotion",
            "Rejected condition: !motions_.erase(id)",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
