    const auto reflection = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});

    if (!normalPath || !reflection) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig mirror;

    mirror.transform = *reflection;
    mirrorLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), mirror);

    if (!mirrorLayer_) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto mirroredPath = motions.pushLayer(*mirrorLayer_);

    normalSpinLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(*normalPath);

    if (!mirroredPath || !normalSpinLayer_) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto mirroredSpin = motions.pushLayer(*mirroredPath);

    if (!mirroredSpin || !motions.bindMotion(*normalPath, *pathId_) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredPath, *pathId_) ||
        !motions.bindMotion(*normalSpinLayer_, *spinId_) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredSpin, *spinId_) ||
        !scene.bindEntity(*first, *normalSpinLayer_) || !scene.bindEntity(*second, *mirroredSpin)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *first) ||
        !scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *second) ||
        !scene.addLine({0, -1.4f, 0}, {0, 1.4f, 0}, {.5f, .5f, .5f})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& camera = app_.getCamera();

    camera.position = {5, 3, 8};
    camera.target = {0, 0, 0};
    camera.up = {0, 1, 0};
    printHelp();

    return true;
}

/// 반사 변환을 순서대로 변경한다.
bool Console::changeReflection() {
    const int next = (reflection_ + 1) % 4;
    std::optional<glm::mat4> matrix = glm::mat4(1);

    if (next == 1) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 2) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 3) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectLine(graphics::AxisLine{});
    }

    if (!mirrorLayer_ || !matrix) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig config;

    config.transform = *matrix;

    if (!app_.getScene().getMotions().setLayer(*mirrorLayer_, config)) {
        return false;
    }
    reflection_ = next;

    const char* names[] = {"없음", "YZ평면 반사", "원점 점대칭", "Y축 직선대칭"};

    std::cout << "반사: " << names[reflection_] << '\n';

    return true;
}

/// 크기 레이어를 삽입하거나 제거한다.
bool Console::toggleInsertedLayer() {
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();

    if (insertedLayer_) {
        if (!motions.removeLayer(*insertedLayer_)) {
            return false;
        }
        insertedLayer_.reset();
        std::cout << "추가 크기 레이어 삭제\n";

        return true;
    }

    const auto scale = graphics::TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(1.5f));

    if (!normalSpinLayer_ || !scale) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig config;

    config.space = graphics::TransformSpace::Local;
    config.transform = *scale;
    insertedLayer_ = motions.insertLayer(*normalSpinLayer_, config);

    if (!insertedLayer_) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "첫 객체에 1.5배 Local 크기 레이어 삽입\n";

    return true;
}

/// 키 입력에 따라 동작을 변경한다.
void Console::onKey(int key, int action) {
    if (action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_7 || key == GLFW_KEY_8 || key == GLFW_KEY_9 || key == GLFW_KEY_0) {
        const bool ok = key == GLFW_KEY_7   ? initGrid()
                        : key == GLFW_KEY_8 ? initRoom()
                        : key == GLFW_KEY_9 ? initAnimation()
                                            : initScene();

        if (!ok)
            app_.requestClose();

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_V) {
        threeViews_ = !threeViews_;

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_D) {
        trace_ = !trace_;
        graphics::Diagnostics::configure({true, trace_});

        return;
    }

    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation && handleJointKey(key))
        return;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_F && demo_ == Demo::Room) {
        floorOpen_ = !floorOpen_;

        return;
    }

    if (demo_ != Demo::Motion) {
        if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE)
            clock_.playing = !clock_.playing;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE)
            app_.requestClose();
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H)
            printHelp();

        return;
    }

    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();
    bool ok = true;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
        app_.requestClose();
    } else if (key >= GLFW_KEY_1 && key <= GLFW_KEY_6) {
        ok = choosePath(key - GLFW_KEY_1 + 1);
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE) {
        const bool playing = paused_;

        ok = pathId_ && spinId_ && globalId_ && motions.setMotionPlaying(*pathId_, playing) &&
             motions.setMotionPlaying(*spinId_, playing) && motions.setMotionPlaying(*globalId_, playing);

        if (ok) {
            paused_ = !paused_;
            std::cout << (paused_ ? "시간 정지\n" : "시간 재개\n");
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_S) {
        ok = spinId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*spinId_, !spinEnabled_);

        if (ok) {
            spinEnabled_ = !spinEnabled_;
            std::cout << "자전 기여: " << spinEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_G) {
        ok = globalId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, !globalEnabled_);

        if (ok) {
            globalEnabled_ = !globalEnabled_;
            std::cout << "전체 공전 기여: " << globalEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_M) {
        ok = changeReflection();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_I) {
        ok = toggleInsertedLayer();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H) {
        printHelp();
    }

    if (!ok) {
        std::cerr << "샘플 명령 적용 실패. 장면을 종료합니다.\n";
        app_.requestClose();
    }
}

/// 마우스 버튼 입력을 처리한다.
void Console::onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {
    if (event.button != GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT || event.action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }

    std::optional<graphics::PickResult> hit;

    if (threeViews_) {
        const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

        if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
            return;

        const auto passes =
            graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions());

        for (const auto& pass : passes) {
            const auto& v = pass.viewport;
            const double top = size[1] - v.y - v.height;

            if (event.framebufferX >= v.x && event.framebufferX < v.x + v.width && event.framebufferY >= top &&
                event.framebufferY < top + v.height)
                hit = graphics::pick(app_.getScene(), pass.camera, event.framebufferX, event.framebufferY,
                                     {double(v.x), top, double(v.width), double(v.height)});
        }
    } else
        hit = app_.pickAt(event.windowX, event.windowY);

    if (hit) {
        std::cout << "entity=" << hit->entityId << " face=" << hit->faceIndex << '\n';
    }
}

/// 조작 방법을 출력한다.
void Console::printHelp() const {
    std::cout
        << "standard: 프레임워크 Motion·반사·레이어 샘플\n"
        << "1 타원 / 2 사각형 / 3 다각형 / 4 사용자 3D 경로+보간 / 5 원 / 6 왕복 꺾은선\n"
        << "Space 시간 정지·재개 / S 자전 기여 / G 전체 공전 기여\n"
        << "M 반사 변경 / I 크기 레이어 삽입·삭제 / H 도움말 / Esc 종료\n"
        << "초기 반사: YZ평면. M은 점대칭 -> Y축 직선대칭 -> 없음 -> YZ평면 순환\n"
        << "왼쪽 클릭: 물체의 entity/face ID 출력\n"
        << "7 Grid/거리 기반 배치 / 8 실내 미끄럼·낙하 / 0 기존 Motion 예제\n"
        << "9 선형보간·진자·사람형·그네→사람 계층 애니메이션\n"
        << "9번 관절: Tab/Backspace 선택, X/Y/Z 축, 좌우 회전, 상하 이동, PageUp/Down 피벗, +/- 균일 배율, R 복원\n"
        << "V 3뷰 전환 / D 단계 추적 로그 / F 실내 예제 바닥 개방 / Space 정지·재개\n";
}

/// 거리 기반 격자 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initGrid() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Grid;
    clock_.reset();
    gridBoxes_.clear();

    graphics::GridLayout grid;

    grid.origin = {-3, -.1f, -2};
    grid.extent = {6, .2f, 4};
    grid.cells = {6, 1, 4};

    for (int z = 0; z < 4; ++z)
        for (int x = 0; x < 6; ++x) {
            const auto center = grid.cellCenter({x, 0, z}), size = grid.relativeSize({.15f, 1, .22f});

            if (!center || !size ||
                !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, *size, *center,
                                                 (x + z) % 2 ? graphics::GlColor{.25f, .3f, .4f}
                                                             : graphics::GlColor{.4f, .45f, .55f}))
                return false;
        }

    if (!distancePath_.setPoints({{-2.5f, .35f, -1.5f}, {2.5f, .35f, -1.5f}, {2.5f, .35f, 1.5f}}))
        return false;

    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        const auto box =
            graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .5f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {.25f + .14f * i, .7f - .1f * i, .3f});

        if (!box || !graphics::PatternSystem::addNumber(scene, i + 1, {-.07f, -.1f, .205f}, .2f, {1, 1, 1}, *box))
            return false;
        gridBoxes_.push_back(*box);
    }
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {7, 6, 8};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 0, 0};
    app_.getCamera().orthographicHalfExtent = 4;

    return true;
}

/// 회전하는 방 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initRoom() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Room;
    clock_.reset();
    floorOpen_ = false;

    const auto room = graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene, {{-2, -1, -2}, {2, 2, 2}});
    const auto cube = graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.5f, .5f, .5f}, {0, -.75f, 0}, {1, .7f, .2f});

    if (!room || !cube)
        return false;
    room_ = *room;
    roomCube_ = *cube;
    roomBody_ = graphics::RoomBody{};
    roomBody_.localPosition = {0, -.75f, 0};
    roomBody_.halfExtent = {.25f, .25f, .25f};
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {5, 3, 8};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 0, 0};

    return true;
}

/// 선형보간과 그네·사람의 부모·자식 애니메이션을 구성한다.
bool Console::initAnimation() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Animation;
    clock_.reset();
    animation_.clear();
    jointControls_.clear();
    selectedJoint_ = 0;
    selectedAxis_ = 0;

    if (!graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {8, .15f, 6}, {0, -.15f, 0}, {.25f, .3f, .35f}))
        return false;

    graphics::SwingConfig swingConfig;

    swingConfig.pivot = {1, 3, 0};
    swingConfig.pendulum.amplitudeDegrees = 25;
    swingConfig.pendulum.periodSeconds = 3;

    const auto swing = graphics::AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider(scene, swingConfig);

    if (!swing)
        return false;
    animation_ = swing->animation;

    graphics::HumanoidConfig personConfig;

    personConfig.grid.frame = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), glm::vec3(-2, 0, 0));

    const auto person = graphics::AnimationSystem::addHumanoid(scene, personConfig);

    if (!person)
        return false;

    auto pose = graphics::HumanoidPose::seated();

    pose.hips = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};
    pose.knees = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};

    if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid(scene, *person, pose))
        return false;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        graphics::PendulumMotion sway;

        sway.amplitudeDegrees = 30;
        sway.periodSeconds = 2;
        sway.phaseDegrees = i == 0 ? 0 : 180;

        graphics::Motion motion;

        if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::pendulum(sway)))
            return false;

        const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, person->shoulders[i], motion);

        if (!track)
            return false;
        animation_.push_back(*track);
    }

    const auto marker = scene.addGroup();

    if (!marker || !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .4f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {1, .85f, .15f}, *marker))
        return false;

    graphics::LinearMotion move;

    move.from = {-2, .3f, 2};
    move.to = {2, .3f, 2};
    move.durationSeconds = 4;
    move.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;

    graphics::Motion motion;

    if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::linear(move)))
        return false;

    const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, *marker, motion);

    if (!track)
        return false;
    animation_.push_back(*track);

    const auto control = [&](graphics::EntityId id, const std::string& name) {
        std::size_t index = 0;

        while (index < animation_.size() && animation_[index].joint != id)
            ++index;

        if (index == animation_.size()) {
            auto fixed = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, id, graphics::Motion{});

            if (!fixed)
                return false;
            animation_.push_back(*fixed);
        }
        jointControls_.push_back({index, name, animation_[index].rest});

        return true;
    };

    const auto personControls = [&](const graphics::HumanoidRig& rig, const std::string& prefix) {
        if (!control(rig.root, prefix + " root") || !control(rig.pelvis, prefix + " pelvis") ||
            !control(rig.torso, prefix + " torso") || !control(rig.neck, prefix + " neck"))
            return false;

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
            const auto side = prefix + (i == 0 ? " left " : " right ");

            if (!control(rig.shoulders[i], side + "shoulder") || !control(rig.elbows[i], side + "elbow") ||
                !control(rig.hands[i], side + "hand") || !control(rig.hips[i], side + "hip") ||
                !control(rig.knees[i], side + "knee") || !control(rig.feet[i], side + "foot"))
                return false;
        }

        return true;
    };

    if (!control(swing->frame, "swing frame") || !control(swing->hinge, "swing hinge") ||
        !control(swing->seat, "swing seat") || !personControls(swing->person, "rider") ||
        !personControls(*person, "standing"))
        return false;
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {8, 5, 10};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 1.4f, 0};
    std::cout << "9: 노란 상자는 4초 편도 선형보간, 그네는 3초 주기 진자, 좌석의 사람은 계층 변환을 따릅니다.\n";
    printSelectedJoint();

    return graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, 0);
}

/// 선택한 관절의 기준 자세를 편집하고 같은 시각의 애니메이션에 반영한다.
bool Console::handleJointKey(int key) {
    if (jointControls_.empty())
        return false;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_TAB)
        selectedJoint_ = (selectedJoint_ + 1) % jointControls_.size();
    else if (key == GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE)
        selectedJoint_ = (selectedJoint_ + jointControls_.size() - 1) % jointControls_.size();
    else if (key == GLFW_KEY_X || key == GLFW_KEY_Y || key == GLFW_KEY_Z)
        selectedAxis_ = key == GLFW_KEY_X ? 0 : key == GLFW_KEY_Y ? 1 : 2;
    else {
        auto& track = animation_[jointControls_[selectedJoint_].track];
        const auto saved = track.rest;

        if (key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT || key == GLFW_KEY_RIGHT) {
            glm::vec3 axis(0);

            axis[selectedAxis_] = 1;
            track.rest.rotation =
                track.rest.rotation *
                glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), glm::radians(key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT ? -5.0f : 5.0f), axis));
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_UP || key == GLFW_KEY_DOWN)
            track.rest.basis[selectedAxis_] += key == GLFW_KEY_UP ? .05f : -.05f;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP || key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_DOWN)
            track.rest.localBasis[selectedAxis_] += key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP ? .05f : -.05f;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD || key == GLFW_KEY_MINUS ||
                 key == GLFW_KEY_KP_SUBTRACT) {
            const bool grow = key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD;

            track.rest.scale = glm::vec3(std::clamp(track.rest.scale.x * (grow ? 1.1f : 1 / 1.1f), .1f, 3.0f));
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_R)
            track.rest = jointControls_[selectedJoint_].initialRest;
        else
            return false;

        if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(app_.getScene(), animation_, clock_.seconds)) {
            track.rest = saved;
            std::cerr << "관절 편집 거절: 기준 자세를 복원했습니다.\n";
        }
    }
    printSelectedJoint();

    return true;
}

/// 선택 관절·축·이동·피벗·균일 배율을 출력한다.
void Console::printSelectedJoint() const {
    if (jointControls_.empty())
        return;

    const auto& control = jointControls_[selectedJoint_];
    const auto& rest = animation_[control.track].rest;

    std::cout << "joint " << selectedJoint_ + 1 << '/' << jointControls_.size() << ": " << control.name
              << " axis=" << "XYZ"[selectedAxis_] << " position=" << rest.basis.x << ',' << rest.basis.y << ','
              << rest.basis.z << " pivot=" << rest.localBasis.x << ',' << rest.localBasis.y << ',' << rest.localBasis.z
              << " scale=" << rest.scale.x << '\n';
}

/// 프레임 시간으로 상태와 장면을 갱신한다.
void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    if (demo_ == Demo::Motion || !clock_.playing)
        return;

    if (!clock_.advance(dt)) {
        app_.requestClose();

        return;
    }

    auto& scene = app_.getScene();
    bool ok = true;

    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation) {
        ok = graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, clock_.seconds);
    } else if (demo_ == Demo::Grid) {
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < gridBoxes_.size(); ++i)
            ok = graphics::PatternSystem::follow(scene, gridBoxes_[i], distancePath_,
                                                 clock_.seconds + distancePath_.length() * i / 5) &&
                 ok;
    } else {
        const float angle = .4f * float(std::sin(clock_.seconds));
        const auto matrix = glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), angle, glm::vec3(0, 0, 1));
        graphics::Transform3D roomTransform;

        roomTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix);
        roomTransform.basis = glm::vec3(matrix * glm::vec4(0, .5f, 0, 1));
        ok =
            scene.setTransform(room_, roomTransform) && scene.setFaceVisible(room_, 5, !floorOpen_) &&
            graphics::CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(roomBody_, {{-2, -1, -2}, {2, 2, 2}}, matrix, dt, floorOpen_, false,
                                                    {0, -9.8f, 0}, {0, 0, .4f * float(std::cos(clock_.seconds))});

        graphics::Transform3D cubeTransform;

        cubeTransform.basis = roomBody_.position(matrix);

        if (!roomBody_.released)
            cubeTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix);
        ok = scene.setTransform(roomCube_, cubeTransform) && ok;
    }

    if (!ok)
        app_.requestClose();
}

/// 현재 장면을 지정한 뷰로 렌더링한다.
bool Console::onRender() {
    if (!threeViews_)
        return app_.renderScene();

    const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

    if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
        return true;

    return app_.renderViews(
        graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions()));
}
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/main.cpp
#include "Console.h"

/// 콜백을 등록하고 애플리케이션을 실행한다.
int main() {
    graphics::Application app;
    Console sample(app);

    app.setInitHandler([&](graphics::Application&) {
        return sample.initScene();
    });
    app.setKeyHandler([&](graphics::Application&, int key, int action) {
        sample.onKey(key, action);
    });
    app.setMouseHandler([&](graphics::Application&, const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {
        sample.onMouse(event);
    });
    app.setUpdateHandler([&](graphics::Application&, float dt) {
        sample.onUpdate(dt);
    });
    app.setRenderHandler([&](graphics::Application&) {
        return sample.onRender();
    });

    graphics::ApplicationConfig config;

    config.title = "standard - Motion, Grid, diagnostics and render passes";

    return app.run(config);
}
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/detail/Animation.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include "Internal.h"
#include <set>

namespace graphics {
namespace detail {
bool validJointRest(const Transform3D& rest) {
    return DiagramSystem::validTransform(rest) && rest.scale.x > 1e-6f &&
           std::abs(rest.scale.x - rest.scale.y) < 1e-6f && std::abs(rest.scale.x - rest.scale.z) < 1e-6f;
}
}

HumanoidPose HumanoidPose::seated() {
    HumanoidPose pose;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        pose.hips[i].x = -90;
        pose.knees[i].x = 90;
        pose.shoulders[i].x = -20;
        pose.elbows[i].x = -40;
    }

    return pose;
}

std::optional<JointAnimation> AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(const Scene& scene, EntityId joint,
                                                                    const Motion& motion) {
    const auto* component = scene.getComponent(joint);
    const auto sample = motion.sample(0);

    if (!component || !sample || !detail::validJointRest(component->transform) || !detail::rigidRoom(sample->parent) ||
        !detail::rigidRoom(sample->local)) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation",
                            "Need a joint with positive uniform rest scale and a rigid motion sample.");

        return {};
    }

    return JointAnimation{joint, component->transform, motion};
}

bool AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(Scene& scene, const std::vector<JointAnimation>& animation, double seconds) {
    if (!std::isfinite(seconds) || seconds < 0)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy",
                                   "Need nonnegative finite seconds.");

    std::set<EntityId> seen;
    std::vector<std::pair<EntityId, Transform3D>> staged;

    staged.reserve(animation.size());

    for (const auto& track : animation) {
        const auto sample = track.motion.sample(seconds);
        const auto rest = DiagramSystem::modelMatrix(track.rest);

        if (!scene.getComponent(track.joint) || !seen.insert(track.joint).second || !sample ||
            !detail::validJointRest(track.rest) || !detail::rigidRoom(sample->parent) ||
            !detail::rigidRoom(sample->local))
            return Diagnostics::failAt(
                __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy",
                "Need unique existing joints, positive uniform rest scales and rigid motion samples.");
        // World contributions are in the joint parent's space, before its rest pose.
        const auto matrix = sample->parent * rest * sample->local;
        Transform3D transform;

        transform.basis = glm::vec3(matrix[3]);
        transform.scale = track.rest.scale;
        transform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix) / track.rest.scale.x;

        if (!DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform))
            return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy",
                                       "Joint composition overflow.");
        staged.emplace_back(track.joint, transform);
    }

    for (const auto& item : staged)
        if (!scene.setTransform(item.first, item.second))
            return false;

    return true;
}

std::optional<HumanoidRig> AnimationSystem::addHumanoid(Scene& scene, const HumanoidConfig& config,
                                                        std::optional<EntityId> parent) {
    if (!config.grid.valid() || !detail::rigidRoom(config.grid.frame) || !std::isfinite(config.upperArmFraction) ||
        config.upperArmFraction <= 0 || config.upperArmFraction >= 1 || !std::isfinite(config.upperLegFraction) ||
        config.upperLegFraction <= 0 || config.upperLegFraction >= 1 || !detail::validColor(config.torsoColor) ||
        !detail::validColor(config.headColor) || !detail::validColor(config.leftColor) ||
        !detail::validColor(config.rightColor) || (parent && !scene.getComponent(*parent))) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "AnimationSystem::addHumanoid",
                            "Invalid grid, limb fractions, colors or parent.");

        return {};
    }

    Scene staged = scene;
    HumanoidRig rig;
    const auto extent = config.grid.extent;
    Transform3D rootTransform;

    rootTransform.basis = glm::vec3(config.grid.frame[3]);
    rootTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(config.grid.frame);

    const auto root = staged.addGroup(rootTransform);

    if (!root || !staged.setParent(*root, parent))
        return {};
    rig.root = *root;

    const auto pelvis =
        PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.root, config.grid.origin + extent * glm::vec3(.5f, .4f, .5f));

    if (!pelvis)
        return {};
    rig.pelvis = *pelvis;

    const auto torso = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.pelvis, {0, extent.y * .18f, 0});

    if (!torso)
        return {};
    rig.torso = *torso;

    const auto torsoMesh =
        PatternSystem::addBox(staged, extent * glm::vec3(.45f, .35f, .7f), {0, 0, 0}, config.torsoColor, rig.torso);
    const auto neck = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.torso, {0, extent.y * .22f, 0});

    if (!torsoMesh || !neck)
        return {};
    rig.torsoMesh = *torsoMesh;
    rig.neck = *neck;

    const auto head = PatternSystem::addBox(staged, extent * glm::vec3(.3f, .2f, .6f), {0, extent.y * .1f, 0},
                                            config.headColor, rig.neck);
    const auto nose = PatternSystem::addBox(staged, extent * glm::vec3(.1f, .05f, .2f),
                                            {0, extent.y * .1f, extent.z * .4f}, {1, .2f, .2f}, rig.neck);

    if (!head || !nose)
        return {};
    rig.head = *head;
    rig.nose = *nose;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        const float side = i == 0 ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
        const auto color = i == 0 ? config.leftColor : config.rightColor;
        const float upperArm = extent.y * .32f * config.upperArmFraction;
        const float forearm = extent.y * .32f - upperArm;
        const float thigh = extent.y * .4f * config.upperLegFraction;
        const float shin = extent.y * .4f - thigh;
        const auto shoulder = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.torso, {side * extent.x * .3f, extent.y * .14f, 0});
        const auto hip = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.pelvis, {side * extent.x * .15f, 0, 0});

        if (!shoulder || !hip)
            return {};
        rig.shoulders[i] = *shoulder;
        rig.hips[i] = *hip;

        const auto elbow = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, *shoulder, {0, -upperArm, 0});
        const auto knee = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, *hip, {0, -thigh, 0});

        if (!elbow || !knee)
            return {};
        rig.elbows[i] = *elbow;
        rig.knees[i] = *knee;

        const auto hand = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, *elbow, {0, -forearm, 0});
        const auto foot = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, *knee, {0, -shin, 0});
        const auto upper = PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {extent.x * .15f, upperArm, extent.z * .3f},
                                                 {0, -upperArm / 2, 0}, color, *shoulder);
        const auto lower = PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {extent.x * .15f, forearm, extent.z * .3f},
                                                 {0, -forearm / 2, 0}, color, *elbow);
        const auto thighMesh =
            PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {extent.x * .18f, thigh, extent.z * .4f}, {0, -thigh / 2, 0}, color, *hip);
        const auto shinMesh =
            PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {extent.x * .18f, shin, extent.z * .4f}, {0, -shin / 2, 0}, color, *knee);

        if (!hand || !foot || !upper || !lower || !thighMesh || !shinMesh)
            return {};
        rig.hands[i] = *hand;
        rig.feet[i] = *foot;
        rig.upperArms[i] = *upper;
        rig.forearms[i] = *lower;
        rig.thighs[i] = *thighMesh;
        rig.shins[i] = *shinMesh;
    }
    scene = std::move(staged);

    return rig;
}

bool AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid(Scene& scene, const HumanoidRig& rig, const HumanoidPose& pose) {
    const std::array<EntityId, 11> joints{rig.pelvis,       rig.torso,     rig.neck,      rig.shoulders[0],
                                          rig.shoulders[1], rig.elbows[0], rig.elbows[1], rig.hips[0],
                                          rig.hips[1],      rig.knees[0],  rig.knees[1]};
    const std::array<glm::vec3, 11> angles{pose.pelvisDegrees, pose.torsoDegrees, pose.neckDegrees, pose.shoulders[0],
                                           pose.shoulders[1],  pose.elbows[0],    pose.elbows[1],   pose.hips[0],
                                           pose.hips[1],       pose.knees[0],     pose.knees[1]};
    std::set<EntityId> seen;
    std::array<Transform3D, 11> transforms;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < joints.size(); ++i) {
        const auto* component = scene.getComponent(joints[i]);

        if (!component || !seen.insert(joints[i]).second || !detail::finiteVector(angles[i]))
            return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid",
                                       "Invalid joint or Euler angles.");

        glm::mat4 rotation(1);

        for (int axis = 0; axis < 3; ++axis) {
            glm::vec3 direction(0);

            direction[axis] = 1;

            const auto turn = TransformSystem::rotation({{0, 0, 0}, direction}, angles[i][axis]);

            if (!turn)
                return false;
            rotation = *turn * rotation;
        }
        transforms[i] = component->transform;
        transforms[i].rotation = glm::mat3(rotation);

        if (!DiagramSystem::validTransform(transforms[i]))
            return false;
    }

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < joints.size(); ++i)
        if (!scene.setTransform(joints[i], transforms[i]))
            return false;

    return true;
}

std::optional<SwingRig> AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider(Scene& scene, const SwingConfig& config,
                                                           std::optional<EntityId> parent) {
    Motion swingMotion;
    auto pendulum = config.pendulum;

    pendulum.axis.point = {0, 0, 0};
    pendulum.space = TransformSpace::Local;

    if (!detail::finiteVector(config.pivot) || !detail::positiveLengths(config.seatSize) ||
        !std::isfinite(config.length) || config.length <= 0 || !std::isfinite(config.ropeThickness) ||
        config.ropeThickness <= 0 || !std::isfinite(config.riderSwayDegrees) || config.riderSwayDegrees < 0 ||
        (parent && !scene.getComponent(*parent)) || !swingMotion.append(Motion::pendulum(pendulum))) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider",
                            "Invalid swing configuration or parent.");

        return {};
    }

    Scene staged = scene;
    SwingRig rig;
    Transform3D transform;

    transform.basis = config.pivot;

    const auto frame = staged.addGroup(transform);

    if (!frame || !staged.setParent(*frame, parent))
        return {};
    rig.frame = *frame;

    const auto hinge = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.frame, {0, 0, 0});

    if (!hinge)
        return {};
    rig.hinge = *hinge;

    const auto seat =
        PatternSystem::addBox(staged, config.seatSize, {0, -config.length, 0}, {.55f, .3f, .1f}, rig.hinge);

    if (!seat)
        return {};
    rig.seat = *seat;

    if (!PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {config.seatSize.x + .6f, .12f, .12f}, {0, 0, 0}, {.3f, .35f, .4f}, rig.frame))
        return {};

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        const float side = i == 0 ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
        const auto rope = PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {config.ropeThickness, config.length, config.ropeThickness},
                                                {side * config.seatSize.x * .43f, -config.length / 2, 0},
                                                {.75f, .75f, .75f}, rig.hinge);

        if (!rope || !PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {.12f, config.length + 1, .12f},
                                            {side * (config.seatSize.x / 2 + .2f), -(config.length + 1) / 2, 0},
                                            {.3f, .35f, .4f}, rig.frame))
            return {};
        rig.ropes[i] = *rope;
    }

    const auto person = addHumanoid(staged, config.person, rig.seat);

    if (!person || !poseHumanoid(staged, *person, HumanoidPose::seated()))
        return {};
    rig.person = *person;

    const auto* root = staged.getComponent(rig.person.root);
    auto personTransform = root->transform;
    const auto hipCenter = config.person.grid.origin + config.person.grid.extent * glm::vec3(.5f, .4f, .5f);
    const float thighHalfThickness = config.person.grid.extent.z * .2f;

    personTransform.basis =
        glm::vec3(0, config.seatSize.y / 2 + thighHalfThickness, 0) - personTransform.rotation * hipCenter;

    if (!staged.setTransform(rig.person.root, personTransform))
        return {};

    const auto swingTrack = createJointAnimation(staged, rig.hinge, swingMotion);
    PendulumMotion rider;

    rider.periodSeconds = pendulum.periodSeconds;
    rider.amplitudeDegrees = config.riderSwayDegrees;
    rider.phaseDegrees = std::remainder(pendulum.phaseDegrees, 360.0) + 180;

    Motion riderMotion;

    if (!riderMotion.append(Motion::pendulum(rider)))
        return {};

    const auto riderTrack = createJointAnimation(staged, rig.person.torso, riderMotion);

    if (!swingTrack || !riderTrack)
        return {};
    rig.animation = {*swingTrack, *riderTrack};

    if (!poseHierarchy(staged, rig.animation, 0))
        return {};
    scene = std::move(staged);

    return rig;
}
} // namespace graphics

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

관절 선택·SRT·피벗 편집

그네를 멈추고 왼쪽 팔꿈치만 돌린 뒤 다시 재생한다. Scene 변환을 직접 덮어쓰면 다음 poseHierarchy가 원래 rest로 되돌리므로, 입력은 선택한 트랙의 rest를 수정한다.

주요 상태: selectedTrack은 animation 배열 인덱스이며 EntityId와 다르다. axis는 0/1/2의 X/Y/Z다. basis는 부모 공간 이동, localBasis는 모델 피벗, scale은 양수 균일 배율이다. rest.rotation에 오른쪽으로 회전을 곱하면 선택 관절의 로컬 축을 사용한다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

bool rotateSelected(graphics::Scene& scene,std::vector<graphics::JointAnimation>& animation,
                    std::size_t selectedTrack,int axis,double seconds) {
    if(selectedTrack>=animation.size()||axis<0||axis>2) return false;
    auto& track=animation[selectedTrack];
    const auto saved=track.rest;
    glm::vec3 direction(0);direction[axis]=1;
    track.rest.rotation=track.rest.rotation*glm::mat3(
        glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1),glm::radians(5.0f),direction));
    if(!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene,animation,seconds)) {
        track.rest=saved;return false;
    }
    return true;
}
// 이동 입력: track.rest.basis[axis] += .05f;
// 피벗 입력: track.rest.localBasis[axis] += .05f;
// 배율 입력: track.rest.scale = glm::vec3(1.1f * track.rest.scale.x);
// 실제 키 선택·배율 제한·복원은 아래 standard::Console 소스를 따른다.

시계를 정지해도 seconds는 유지되므로 같은 시각에서 편집 결과를 즉시 볼 수 있다. 입력 후 재생을 재개해도 rest가 남는다. standard의 handleJointKey는 실패 시 rest를 복원하고, R은 선택 관절의 최초 rest를 복원한다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/Console.h
#pragma once
#include "framework/Framework.h"

class Console {
    graphics::Application& app_;
    std::optional<graphics::MotionId> pathId_;
    std::optional<graphics::MotionId> spinId_;
    std::optional<graphics::MotionId> globalId_;
    std::optional<graphics::LayerId> mirrorLayer_;
    std::optional<graphics::LayerId> normalSpinLayer_;
    std::optional<graphics::LayerId> insertedLayer_;
    bool paused_ = false;
    bool spinEnabled_ = true;
    bool globalEnabled_ = false;
    int reflection_ = 1;

    enum class Demo { Motion, Grid, Room, Animation };

    Demo demo_ = Demo::Motion;
    graphics::AnimationClock clock_;
    graphics::DistancePath distancePath_;
    std::vector<graphics::EntityId> gridBoxes_;
    graphics::RoomBody roomBody_;
    graphics::EntityId room_ = 0, roomCube_ = 0;
    std::vector<graphics::JointAnimation> animation_;

    struct JointControl {
        std::size_t track = 0;
        std::string name;
        graphics::Transform3D initialRest;
    };

    std::vector<JointControl> jointControls_;
    std::size_t selectedJoint_ = 0;
    int selectedAxis_ = 0;


    /// 선택한 관절의 기준 SRT와 피벗을 키 입력으로 편집한다.
    bool handleJointKey(int key);


    /// 현재 선택 관절과 조작 축을 콘솔에 출력한다.
    void printSelectedJoint() const;


    bool threeViews_ = false, trace_ = false, floorOpen_ = false;


    /// 거리 기반 격자 예제를 구성한다.
    bool initGrid();


    /// 회전하는 방 예제를 구성한다.
    bool initRoom();


    /// 선형보간·그네·사람 관절의 계층 애니메이션 예제를 구성한다.
    bool initAnimation();


    /// 선택한 경로로 이동 모션을 바꾼다.
    bool choosePath(int kind);


    /// 반사 변환을 순서대로 변경한다.
    bool changeReflection();


    /// 크기 레이어를 삽입하거나 제거한다.
    bool toggleInsertedLayer();


    /// 조작 방법을 출력한다.
    void printHelp() const;


public:
    /// 애플리케이션 참조를 보관한다.
    explicit Console(graphics::Application& app) : app_(app) {
    }


    /// 장면 객체와 초기 상태를 구성한다.
    bool initScene();


    /// 키 입력에 따라 동작을 변경한다.
    void onKey(int key, int action);


    /// 마우스 버튼 입력을 처리한다.
    void onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event);


    /// 프레임 시간으로 상태와 장면을 갱신한다.
    void onUpdate(float dt);


    /// 현재 장면을 지정한 뷰로 렌더링한다.
    bool onRender();
};
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <iostream>

/// 선택한 경로로 이동 모션을 바꾼다.
bool Console::choosePath(int kind) {
    graphics::PathMotion path;

    path.durationSeconds = 6;

    const glm::vec3 center(1.7f, 0, 0);

    if (kind == 1) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::ellipse(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 2) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::rectangle(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 3) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polygon(
            {{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.4f, 0}, {2.1f, .6f, 0}, {1.4f, .8f, 0}, {.9f, .2f, 0}});
    } else if (kind == 4) {
        path.path = [center](double u) {
            const double angle = u * 6.283185307179586;

            return center +
                   glm::vec3(float(.7 * std::sin(2 * angle)), float(.6 * std::sin(angle)), float(.3 * std::cos(angle)));
        };
        path.interpolation = graphics::PathSystem::smoothStep;
    } else if (kind == 5) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::circle(center, .65f, {0, 0, 1});
    } else if (kind == 6) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polyline({{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.5f, 0}, {2, .6f, 0}});
        path.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;
    } else {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::Motion motion;
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();

    if (!pathId_ || !motion.append(graphics::Motion::followPath(path))) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!motions.setMotion(*pathId_, motion) || !motions.seekMotion(*pathId_, 0)) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "경로 " << kind << ": 시작 시각 0초로 변경\n";

    return true;
}

/// 장면 객체와 초기 상태를 구성한다.
bool Console::initScene() {
    demo_ = Demo::Motion;

    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    paused_ = false;
    spinEnabled_ = true;
    globalEnabled_ = false;
    reflection_ = 1;
    insertedLayer_.reset();

    const auto first = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
    const auto second = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});

    if (!first || !second) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!scene.setTint(*first, {1, .35f, .2f}) || !scene.setTint(*second, {.2f, .7f, 1})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& motions = scene.getMotions();

    pathId_ = motions.createMotion(graphics::Motion{});

    graphics::RotationMotion spin;

    spin.degreesPerSecond = 90;
    spinId_ = motions.createSpin(spin);

    graphics::RotationMotion global;

    global.degreesPerSecond = 15;
    globalId_ = motions.createOrbit(global);

    if (!pathId_ || !spinId_ || !globalId_ || !choosePath(1)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, false) ||
        !motions.bindMotion(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), *globalId_)) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto normalPath = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer());
    const auto reflection = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});

    if (!normalPath || !reflection) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig mirror;

    mirror.transform = *reflection;
    mirrorLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), mirror);

    if (!mirrorLayer_) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto mirroredPath = motions.pushLayer(*mirrorLayer_);

    normalSpinLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(*normalPath);

    if (!mirroredPath || !normalSpinLayer_) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto mirroredSpin = motions.pushLayer(*mirroredPath);

    if (!mirroredSpin || !motions.bindMotion(*normalPath, *pathId_) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredPath, *pathId_) ||
        !motions.bindMotion(*normalSpinLayer_, *spinId_) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredSpin, *spinId_) ||
        !scene.bindEntity(*first, *normalSpinLayer_) || !scene.bindEntity(*second, *mirroredSpin)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *first) ||
        !scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *second) ||
        !scene.addLine({0, -1.4f, 0}, {0, 1.4f, 0}, {.5f, .5f, .5f})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& camera = app_.getCamera();

    camera.position = {5, 3, 8};
    camera.target = {0, 0, 0};
    camera.up = {0, 1, 0};
    printHelp();

    return true;
}

/// 반사 변환을 순서대로 변경한다.
bool Console::changeReflection() {
    const int next = (reflection_ + 1) % 4;
    std::optional<glm::mat4> matrix = glm::mat4(1);

    if (next == 1) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 2) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 3) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectLine(graphics::AxisLine{});
    }

    if (!mirrorLayer_ || !matrix) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig config;

    config.transform = *matrix;

    if (!app_.getScene().getMotions().setLayer(*mirrorLayer_, config)) {
        return false;
    }
    reflection_ = next;

    const char* names[] = {"없음", "YZ평면 반사", "원점 점대칭", "Y축 직선대칭"};

    std::cout << "반사: " << names[reflection_] << '\n';

    return true;
}

/// 크기 레이어를 삽입하거나 제거한다.
bool Console::toggleInsertedLayer() {
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();

    if (insertedLayer_) {
        if (!motions.removeLayer(*insertedLayer_)) {
            return false;
        }
        insertedLayer_.reset();
        std::cout << "추가 크기 레이어 삭제\n";

        return true;
    }

    const auto scale = graphics::TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(1.5f));

    if (!normalSpinLayer_ || !scale) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig config;

    config.space = graphics::TransformSpace::Local;
    config.transform = *scale;
    insertedLayer_ = motions.insertLayer(*normalSpinLayer_, config);

    if (!insertedLayer_) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "첫 객체에 1.5배 Local 크기 레이어 삽입\n";

    return true;
}

/// 키 입력에 따라 동작을 변경한다.
void Console::onKey(int key, int action) {
    if (action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_7 || key == GLFW_KEY_8 || key == GLFW_KEY_9 || key == GLFW_KEY_0) {
        const bool ok = key == GLFW_KEY_7   ? initGrid()
                        : key == GLFW_KEY_8 ? initRoom()
                        : key == GLFW_KEY_9 ? initAnimation()
                                            : initScene();

        if (!ok)
            app_.requestClose();

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_V) {
        threeViews_ = !threeViews_;

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_D) {
        trace_ = !trace_;
        graphics::Diagnostics::configure({true, trace_});

        return;
    }

    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation && handleJointKey(key))
        return;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_F && demo_ == Demo::Room) {
        floorOpen_ = !floorOpen_;

        return;
    }

    if (demo_ != Demo::Motion) {
        if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE)
            clock_.playing = !clock_.playing;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE)
            app_.requestClose();
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H)
            printHelp();

        return;
    }

    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();
    bool ok = true;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
        app_.requestClose();
    } else if (key >= GLFW_KEY_1 && key <= GLFW_KEY_6) {
        ok = choosePath(key - GLFW_KEY_1 + 1);
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE) {
        const bool playing = paused_;

        ok = pathId_ && spinId_ && globalId_ && motions.setMotionPlaying(*pathId_, playing) &&
             motions.setMotionPlaying(*spinId_, playing) && motions.setMotionPlaying(*globalId_, playing);

        if (ok) {
            paused_ = !paused_;
            std::cout << (paused_ ? "시간 정지\n" : "시간 재개\n");
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_S) {
        ok = spinId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*spinId_, !spinEnabled_);

        if (ok) {
            spinEnabled_ = !spinEnabled_;
            std::cout << "자전 기여: " << spinEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_G) {
        ok = globalId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, !globalEnabled_);

        if (ok) {
            globalEnabled_ = !globalEnabled_;
            std::cout << "전체 공전 기여: " << globalEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_M) {
        ok = changeReflection();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_I) {
        ok = toggleInsertedLayer();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H) {
        printHelp();
    }

    if (!ok) {
        std::cerr << "샘플 명령 적용 실패. 장면을 종료합니다.\n";
        app_.requestClose();
    }
}

/// 마우스 버튼 입력을 처리한다.
void Console::onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {
    if (event.button != GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT || event.action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }

    std::optional<graphics::PickResult> hit;

    if (threeViews_) {
        const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

        if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
            return;

        const auto passes =
            graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions());

        for (const auto& pass : passes) {
            const auto& v = pass.viewport;
            const double top = size[1] - v.y - v.height;

            if (event.framebufferX >= v.x && event.framebufferX < v.x + v.width && event.framebufferY >= top &&
                event.framebufferY < top + v.height)
                hit = graphics::pick(app_.getScene(), pass.camera, event.framebufferX, event.framebufferY,
                                     {double(v.x), top, double(v.width), double(v.height)});
        }
    } else
        hit = app_.pickAt(event.windowX, event.windowY);

    if (hit) {
        std::cout << "entity=" << hit->entityId << " face=" << hit->faceIndex << '\n';
    }
}

/// 조작 방법을 출력한다.
void Console::printHelp() const {
    std::cout
        << "standard: 프레임워크 Motion·반사·레이어 샘플\n"
        << "1 타원 / 2 사각형 / 3 다각형 / 4 사용자 3D 경로+보간 / 5 원 / 6 왕복 꺾은선\n"
        << "Space 시간 정지·재개 / S 자전 기여 / G 전체 공전 기여\n"
        << "M 반사 변경 / I 크기 레이어 삽입·삭제 / H 도움말 / Esc 종료\n"
        << "초기 반사: YZ평면. M은 점대칭 -> Y축 직선대칭 -> 없음 -> YZ평면 순환\n"
        << "왼쪽 클릭: 물체의 entity/face ID 출력\n"
        << "7 Grid/거리 기반 배치 / 8 실내 미끄럼·낙하 / 0 기존 Motion 예제\n"
        << "9 선형보간·진자·사람형·그네→사람 계층 애니메이션\n"
        << "9번 관절: Tab/Backspace 선택, X/Y/Z 축, 좌우 회전, 상하 이동, PageUp/Down 피벗, +/- 균일 배율, R 복원\n"
        << "V 3뷰 전환 / D 단계 추적 로그 / F 실내 예제 바닥 개방 / Space 정지·재개\n";
}

/// 거리 기반 격자 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initGrid() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Grid;
    clock_.reset();
    gridBoxes_.clear();

    graphics::GridLayout grid;

    grid.origin = {-3, -.1f, -2};
    grid.extent = {6, .2f, 4};
    grid.cells = {6, 1, 4};

    for (int z = 0; z < 4; ++z)
        for (int x = 0; x < 6; ++x) {
            const auto center = grid.cellCenter({x, 0, z}), size = grid.relativeSize({.15f, 1, .22f});

            if (!center || !size ||
                !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, *size, *center,
                                                 (x + z) % 2 ? graphics::GlColor{.25f, .3f, .4f}
                                                             : graphics::GlColor{.4f, .45f, .55f}))
                return false;
        }

    if (!distancePath_.setPoints({{-2.5f, .35f, -1.5f}, {2.5f, .35f, -1.5f}, {2.5f, .35f, 1.5f}}))
        return false;

    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        const auto box =
            graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .5f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {.25f + .14f * i, .7f - .1f * i, .3f});

        if (!box || !graphics::PatternSystem::addNumber(scene, i + 1, {-.07f, -.1f, .205f}, .2f, {1, 1, 1}, *box))
            return false;
        gridBoxes_.push_back(*box);
    }
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {7, 6, 8};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 0, 0};
    app_.getCamera().orthographicHalfExtent = 4;

    return true;
}

/// 회전하는 방 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initRoom() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Room;
    clock_.reset();
    floorOpen_ = false;

    const auto room = graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene, {{-2, -1, -2}, {2, 2, 2}});
    const auto cube = graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.5f, .5f, .5f}, {0, -.75f, 0}, {1, .7f, .2f});

    if (!room || !cube)
        return false;
    room_ = *room;
    roomCube_ = *cube;
    roomBody_ = graphics::RoomBody{};
    roomBody_.localPosition = {0, -.75f, 0};
    roomBody_.halfExtent = {.25f, .25f, .25f};
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {5, 3, 8};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 0, 0};

    return true;
}

/// 선형보간과 그네·사람의 부모·자식 애니메이션을 구성한다.
bool Console::initAnimation() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Animation;
    clock_.reset();
    animation_.clear();
    jointControls_.clear();
    selectedJoint_ = 0;
    selectedAxis_ = 0;

    if (!graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {8, .15f, 6}, {0, -.15f, 0}, {.25f, .3f, .35f}))
        return false;

    graphics::SwingConfig swingConfig;

    swingConfig.pivot = {1, 3, 0};
    swingConfig.pendulum.amplitudeDegrees = 25;
    swingConfig.pendulum.periodSeconds = 3;

    const auto swing = graphics::AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider(scene, swingConfig);

    if (!swing)
        return false;
    animation_ = swing->animation;

    graphics::HumanoidConfig personConfig;

    personConfig.grid.frame = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), glm::vec3(-2, 0, 0));

    const auto person = graphics::AnimationSystem::addHumanoid(scene, personConfig);

    if (!person)
        return false;

    auto pose = graphics::HumanoidPose::seated();

    pose.hips = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};
    pose.knees = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};

    if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid(scene, *person, pose))
        return false;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        graphics::PendulumMotion sway;

        sway.amplitudeDegrees = 30;
        sway.periodSeconds = 2;
        sway.phaseDegrees = i == 0 ? 0 : 180;

        graphics::Motion motion;

        if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::pendulum(sway)))
            return false;

        const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, person->shoulders[i], motion);

        if (!track)
            return false;
        animation_.push_back(*track);
    }

    const auto marker = scene.addGroup();

    if (!marker || !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .4f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {1, .85f, .15f}, *marker))
        return false;

    graphics::LinearMotion move;

    move.from = {-2, .3f, 2};
    move.to = {2, .3f, 2};
    move.durationSeconds = 4;
    move.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;

    graphics::Motion motion;

    if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::linear(move)))
        return false;

    const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, *marker, motion);

    if (!track)
        return false;
    animation_.push_back(*track);

    const auto control = [&](graphics::EntityId id, const std::string& name) {
        std::size_t index = 0;

        while (index < animation_.size() && animation_[index].joint != id)
            ++index;

        if (index == animation_.size()) {
            auto fixed = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, id, graphics::Motion{});

            if (!fixed)
                return false;
            animation_.push_back(*fixed);
        }
        jointControls_.push_back({index, name, animation_[index].rest});

        return true;
    };

    const auto personControls = [&](const graphics::HumanoidRig& rig, const std::string& prefix) {
        if (!control(rig.root, prefix + " root") || !control(rig.pelvis, prefix + " pelvis") ||
            !control(rig.torso, prefix + " torso") || !control(rig.neck, prefix + " neck"))
            return false;

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
            const auto side = prefix + (i == 0 ? " left " : " right ");

            if (!control(rig.shoulders[i], side + "shoulder") || !control(rig.elbows[i], side + "elbow") ||
                !control(rig.hands[i], side + "hand") || !control(rig.hips[i], side + "hip") ||
                !control(rig.knees[i], side + "knee") || !control(rig.feet[i], side + "foot"))
                return false;
        }

        return true;
    };

    if (!control(swing->frame, "swing frame") || !control(swing->hinge, "swing hinge") ||
        !control(swing->seat, "swing seat") || !personControls(swing->person, "rider") ||
        !personControls(*person, "standing"))
        return false;
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {8, 5, 10};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 1.4f, 0};
    std::cout << "9: 노란 상자는 4초 편도 선형보간, 그네는 3초 주기 진자, 좌석의 사람은 계층 변환을 따릅니다.\n";
    printSelectedJoint();

    return graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, 0);
}

/// 선택한 관절의 기준 자세를 편집하고 같은 시각의 애니메이션에 반영한다.
bool Console::handleJointKey(int key) {
    if (jointControls_.empty())
        return false;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_TAB)
        selectedJoint_ = (selectedJoint_ + 1) % jointControls_.size();
    else if (key == GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE)
        selectedJoint_ = (selectedJoint_ + jointControls_.size() - 1) % jointControls_.size();
    else if (key == GLFW_KEY_X || key == GLFW_KEY_Y || key == GLFW_KEY_Z)
        selectedAxis_ = key == GLFW_KEY_X ? 0 : key == GLFW_KEY_Y ? 1 : 2;
    else {
        auto& track = animation_[jointControls_[selectedJoint_].track];
        const auto saved = track.rest;

        if (key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT || key == GLFW_KEY_RIGHT) {
            glm::vec3 axis(0);

            axis[selectedAxis_] = 1;
            track.rest.rotation =
                track.rest.rotation *
                glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), glm::radians(key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT ? -5.0f : 5.0f), axis));
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_UP || key == GLFW_KEY_DOWN)
            track.rest.basis[selectedAxis_] += key == GLFW_KEY_UP ? .05f : -.05f;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP || key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_DOWN)
            track.rest.localBasis[selectedAxis_] += key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP ? .05f : -.05f;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD || key == GLFW_KEY_MINUS ||
                 key == GLFW_KEY_KP_SUBTRACT) {
            const bool grow = key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD;

            track.rest.scale = glm::vec3(std::clamp(track.rest.scale.x * (grow ? 1.1f : 1 / 1.1f), .1f, 3.0f));
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_R)
            track.rest = jointControls_[selectedJoint_].initialRest;
        else
            return false;

        if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(app_.getScene(), animation_, clock_.seconds)) {
            track.rest = saved;
            std::cerr << "관절 편집 거절: 기준 자세를 복원했습니다.\n";
        }
    }
    printSelectedJoint();

    return true;
}

/// 선택 관절·축·이동·피벗·균일 배율을 출력한다.
void Console::printSelectedJoint() const {
    if (jointControls_.empty())
        return;

    const auto& control = jointControls_[selectedJoint_];
    const auto& rest = animation_[control.track].rest;

    std::cout << "joint " << selectedJoint_ + 1 << '/' << jointControls_.size() << ": " << control.name
              << " axis=" << "XYZ"[selectedAxis_] << " position=" << rest.basis.x << ',' << rest.basis.y << ','
              << rest.basis.z << " pivot=" << rest.localBasis.x << ',' << rest.localBasis.y << ',' << rest.localBasis.z
              << " scale=" << rest.scale.x << '\n';
}

/// 프레임 시간으로 상태와 장면을 갱신한다.
void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    if (demo_ == Demo::Motion || !clock_.playing)
        return;

    if (!clock_.advance(dt)) {
        app_.requestClose();

        return;
    }

    auto& scene = app_.getScene();
    bool ok = true;

    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation) {
        ok = graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, clock_.seconds);
    } else if (demo_ == Demo::Grid) {
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < gridBoxes_.size(); ++i)
            ok = graphics::PatternSystem::follow(scene, gridBoxes_[i], distancePath_,
                                                 clock_.seconds + distancePath_.length() * i / 5) &&
                 ok;
    } else {
        const float angle = .4f * float(std::sin(clock_.seconds));
        const auto matrix = glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), angle, glm::vec3(0, 0, 1));
        graphics::Transform3D roomTransform;

        roomTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix);
        roomTransform.basis = glm::vec3(matrix * glm::vec4(0, .5f, 0, 1));
        ok =
            scene.setTransform(room_, roomTransform) && scene.setFaceVisible(room_, 5, !floorOpen_) &&
            graphics::CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(roomBody_, {{-2, -1, -2}, {2, 2, 2}}, matrix, dt, floorOpen_, false,
                                                    {0, -9.8f, 0}, {0, 0, .4f * float(std::cos(clock_.seconds))});

        graphics::Transform3D cubeTransform;

        cubeTransform.basis = roomBody_.position(matrix);

        if (!roomBody_.released)
            cubeTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix);
        ok = scene.setTransform(roomCube_, cubeTransform) && ok;
    }

    if (!ok)
        app_.requestClose();
}

/// 현재 장면을 지정한 뷰로 렌더링한다.
bool Console::onRender() {
    if (!threeViews_)
        return app_.renderScene();

    const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

    if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
        return true;

    return app_.renderViews(
        graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions()));
}

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

그네(진자) → 좌석 → 사람

그네 좌석에 앉은 사람을 만들고 상체·팔꿈치를 따로 움직인다. 사람 루트를 좌석의 자식으로 연결하므로 그네 각도를 사람의 모든 관절에 다시 수동 계산할 필요가 없다.

주요 상태: HumanoidRig는 관절과 표시 메시의 ID 묶음이다. JointAnimation.rest는 생성 시 캡처한 기준 자세이며, 매 프레임 이 자세에서 다시 계산해 회전 누적 오차를 피한다. animation은 매 프레임 샘플링할 트랙 목록이다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

struct SwingExample {
    graphics::AnimationClock clock;
    std::vector<graphics::JointAnimation> animation;
    bool initialize(graphics::Scene& scene) {
        graphics::HumanoidConfig personConfig;
        personConfig.grid.frame=glm::translate(glm::mat4(1),glm::vec3(-2,0,0));
        auto person=graphics::AnimationSystem::addHumanoid(scene,personConfig); if(!person) return false;
        auto pose=graphics::HumanoidPose::seated();
        if(!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid(scene,*person,pose)) return false;
        graphics::SwingConfig config;config.pivot={1,3,0};config.pendulum.periodSeconds=3;
        auto swing=graphics::AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider(scene,config); if(!swing) return false;
        animation=swing->animation;
        graphics::PendulumMotion elbow;elbow.amplitudeDegrees=10;
        graphics::Motion motion;if(!motion.append(graphics::Motion::pendulum(elbow))) return false;
        auto track=graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene,swing->person.elbows[0],motion);
        if(!track) return false;
        animation.push_back(*track);clock.reset();return true;
    }
    bool update(graphics::Scene& scene,float dt) {
        return clock.advance(dt)&&graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene,animation,clock.seconds);
    }
};

initialize는 initHandler에서, update는 updateHandler에서 호출한다. 샘플은 강체 회전·이동이며 기준 rest는 양수 균일 배율과 피벗 편집을 허용한다. 비균일 배율·shear는 거부한다. standard 9번은 35개 관절/프레임을 선택해 rest를 편집하고, 매 프레임 같은 rest에서 계산하므로 입력이 덮어써지지 않는다. 전체 연결 코드는 아래에 있다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/Console.h
#pragma once
#include "framework/Framework.h"

class Console {
    graphics::Application& app_;
    std::optional<graphics::MotionId> pathId_;
    std::optional<graphics::MotionId> spinId_;
    std::optional<graphics::MotionId> globalId_;
    std::optional<graphics::LayerId> mirrorLayer_;
    std::optional<graphics::LayerId> normalSpinLayer_;
    std::optional<graphics::LayerId> insertedLayer_;
    bool paused_ = false;
    bool spinEnabled_ = true;
    bool globalEnabled_ = false;
    int reflection_ = 1;

    enum class Demo { Motion, Grid, Room, Animation };

    Demo demo_ = Demo::Motion;
    graphics::AnimationClock clock_;
    graphics::DistancePath distancePath_;
    std::vector<graphics::EntityId> gridBoxes_;
    graphics::RoomBody roomBody_;
    graphics::EntityId room_ = 0, roomCube_ = 0;
    std::vector<graphics::JointAnimation> animation_;

    struct JointControl {
        std::size_t track = 0;
        std::string name;
        graphics::Transform3D initialRest;
    };

    std::vector<JointControl> jointControls_;
    std::size_t selectedJoint_ = 0;
    int selectedAxis_ = 0;


    /// 선택한 관절의 기준 SRT와 피벗을 키 입력으로 편집한다.
    bool handleJointKey(int key);


    /// 현재 선택 관절과 조작 축을 콘솔에 출력한다.
    void printSelectedJoint() const;


    bool threeViews_ = false, trace_ = false, floorOpen_ = false;


    /// 거리 기반 격자 예제를 구성한다.
    bool initGrid();


    /// 회전하는 방 예제를 구성한다.
    bool initRoom();


    /// 선형보간·그네·사람 관절의 계층 애니메이션 예제를 구성한다.
    bool initAnimation();


    /// 선택한 경로로 이동 모션을 바꾼다.
    bool choosePath(int kind);


    /// 반사 변환을 순서대로 변경한다.
    bool changeReflection();


    /// 크기 레이어를 삽입하거나 제거한다.
    bool toggleInsertedLayer();


    /// 조작 방법을 출력한다.
    void printHelp() const;


public:
    /// 애플리케이션 참조를 보관한다.
    explicit Console(graphics::Application& app) : app_(app) {
    }


    /// 장면 객체와 초기 상태를 구성한다.
    bool initScene();


    /// 키 입력에 따라 동작을 변경한다.
    void onKey(int key, int action);


    /// 마우스 버튼 입력을 처리한다.
    void onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event);


    /// 프레임 시간으로 상태와 장면을 갱신한다.
    void onUpdate(float dt);


    /// 현재 장면을 지정한 뷰로 렌더링한다.
    bool onRender();
};
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <iostream>

/// 선택한 경로로 이동 모션을 바꾼다.
bool Console::choosePath(int kind) {
    graphics::PathMotion path;

    path.durationSeconds = 6;

    const glm::vec3 center(1.7f, 0, 0);

    if (kind == 1) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::ellipse(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 2) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::rectangle(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 3) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polygon(
            {{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.4f, 0}, {2.1f, .6f, 0}, {1.4f, .8f, 0}, {.9f, .2f, 0}});
    } else if (kind == 4) {
        path.path = [center](double u) {
            const double angle = u * 6.283185307179586;

            return center +
                   glm::vec3(float(.7 * std::sin(2 * angle)), float(.6 * std::sin(angle)), float(.3 * std::cos(angle)));
        };
        path.interpolation = graphics::PathSystem::smoothStep;
    } else if (kind == 5) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::circle(center, .65f, {0, 0, 1});
    } else if (kind == 6) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polyline({{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.5f, 0}, {2, .6f, 0}});
        path.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;
    } else {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::Motion motion;
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();

    if (!pathId_ || !motion.append(graphics::Motion::followPath(path))) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!motions.setMotion(*pathId_, motion) || !motions.seekMotion(*pathId_, 0)) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "경로 " << kind << ": 시작 시각 0초로 변경\n";

    return true;
}

/// 장면 객체와 초기 상태를 구성한다.
bool Console::initScene() {
    demo_ = Demo::Motion;

    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    paused_ = false;
    spinEnabled_ = true;
    globalEnabled_ = false;
    reflection_ = 1;
    insertedLayer_.reset();

    const auto first = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
    const auto second = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});

    if (!first || !second) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!scene.setTint(*first, {1, .35f, .2f}) || !scene.setTint(*second, {.2f, .7f, 1})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& motions = scene.getMotions();

    pathId_ = motions.createMotion(graphics::Motion{});

    graphics::RotationMotion spin;

    spin.degreesPerSecond = 90;
    spinId_ = motions.createSpin(spin);

    graphics::RotationMotion global;

    global.degreesPerSecond = 15;
    globalId_ = motions.createOrbit(global);

    if (!pathId_ || !spinId_ || !globalId_ || !choosePath(1)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, false) ||
        !motions.bindMotion(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), *globalId_)) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto normalPath = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer());
    const auto reflection = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});

    if (!normalPath || !reflection) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig mirror;

    mirror.transform = *reflection;
    mirrorLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), mirror);

    if (!mirrorLayer_) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto mirroredPath = motions.pushLayer(*mirrorLayer_);

    normalSpinLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(*normalPath);

    if (!mirroredPath || !normalSpinLayer_) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto mirroredSpin = motions.pushLayer(*mirroredPath);

    if (!mirroredSpin || !motions.bindMotion(*normalPath, *pathId_) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredPath, *pathId_) ||
        !motions.bindMotion(*normalSpinLayer_, *spinId_) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredSpin, *spinId_) ||
        !scene.bindEntity(*first, *normalSpinLayer_) || !scene.bindEntity(*second, *mirroredSpin)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *first) ||
        !scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *second) ||
        !scene.addLine({0, -1.4f, 0}, {0, 1.4f, 0}, {.5f, .5f, .5f})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& camera = app_.getCamera();

    camera.position = {5, 3, 8};
    camera.target = {0, 0, 0};
    camera.up = {0, 1, 0};
    printHelp();

    return true;
}

/// 반사 변환을 순서대로 변경한다.
bool Console::changeReflection() {
    const int next = (reflection_ + 1) % 4;
    std::optional<glm::mat4> matrix = glm::mat4(1);

    if (next == 1) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 2) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 3) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectLine(graphics::AxisLine{});
    }

    if (!mirrorLayer_ || !matrix) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig config;

    config.transform = *matrix;

    if (!app_.getScene().getMotions().setLayer(*mirrorLayer_, config)) {
        return false;
    }
    reflection_ = next;

    const char* names[] = {"없음", "YZ평면 반사", "원점 점대칭", "Y축 직선대칭"};

    std::cout << "반사: " << names[reflection_] << '\n';

    return true;
}

/// 크기 레이어를 삽입하거나 제거한다.
bool Console::toggleInsertedLayer() {
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();

    if (insertedLayer_) {
        if (!motions.removeLayer(*insertedLayer_)) {
            return false;
        }
        insertedLayer_.reset();
        std::cout << "추가 크기 레이어 삭제\n";

        return true;
    }

    const auto scale = graphics::TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(1.5f));

    if (!normalSpinLayer_ || !scale) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig config;

    config.space = graphics::TransformSpace::Local;
    config.transform = *scale;
    insertedLayer_ = motions.insertLayer(*normalSpinLayer_, config);

    if (!insertedLayer_) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "첫 객체에 1.5배 Local 크기 레이어 삽입\n";

    return true;
}

/// 키 입력에 따라 동작을 변경한다.
void Console::onKey(int key, int action) {
    if (action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_7 || key == GLFW_KEY_8 || key == GLFW_KEY_9 || key == GLFW_KEY_0) {
        const bool ok = key == GLFW_KEY_7   ? initGrid()
                        : key == GLFW_KEY_8 ? initRoom()
                        : key == GLFW_KEY_9 ? initAnimation()
                                            : initScene();

        if (!ok)
            app_.requestClose();

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_V) {
        threeViews_ = !threeViews_;

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_D) {
        trace_ = !trace_;
        graphics::Diagnostics::configure({true, trace_});

        return;
    }

    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation && handleJointKey(key))
        return;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_F && demo_ == Demo::Room) {
        floorOpen_ = !floorOpen_;

        return;
    }

    if (demo_ != Demo::Motion) {
        if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE)
            clock_.playing = !clock_.playing;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE)
            app_.requestClose();
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H)
            printHelp();

        return;
    }

    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();
    bool ok = true;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
        app_.requestClose();
    } else if (key >= GLFW_KEY_1 && key <= GLFW_KEY_6) {
        ok = choosePath(key - GLFW_KEY_1 + 1);
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE) {
        const bool playing = paused_;

        ok = pathId_ && spinId_ && globalId_ && motions.setMotionPlaying(*pathId_, playing) &&
             motions.setMotionPlaying(*spinId_, playing) && motions.setMotionPlaying(*globalId_, playing);

        if (ok) {
            paused_ = !paused_;
            std::cout << (paused_ ? "시간 정지\n" : "시간 재개\n");
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_S) {
        ok = spinId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*spinId_, !spinEnabled_);

        if (ok) {
            spinEnabled_ = !spinEnabled_;
            std::cout << "자전 기여: " << spinEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_G) {
        ok = globalId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, !globalEnabled_);

        if (ok) {
            globalEnabled_ = !globalEnabled_;
            std::cout << "전체 공전 기여: " << globalEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_M) {
        ok = changeReflection();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_I) {
        ok = toggleInsertedLayer();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H) {
        printHelp();
    }

    if (!ok) {
        std::cerr << "샘플 명령 적용 실패. 장면을 종료합니다.\n";
        app_.requestClose();
    }
}

/// 마우스 버튼 입력을 처리한다.
void Console::onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {
    if (event.button != GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT || event.action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }

    std::optional<graphics::PickResult> hit;

    if (threeViews_) {
        const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

        if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
            return;

        const auto passes =
            graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions());

        for (const auto& pass : passes) {
            const auto& v = pass.viewport;
            const double top = size[1] - v.y - v.height;

            if (event.framebufferX >= v.x && event.framebufferX < v.x + v.width && event.framebufferY >= top &&
                event.framebufferY < top + v.height)
                hit = graphics::pick(app_.getScene(), pass.camera, event.framebufferX, event.framebufferY,
                                     {double(v.x), top, double(v.width), double(v.height)});
        }
    } else
        hit = app_.pickAt(event.windowX, event.windowY);

    if (hit) {
        std::cout << "entity=" << hit->entityId << " face=" << hit->faceIndex << '\n';
    }
}

/// 조작 방법을 출력한다.
void Console::printHelp() const {
    std::cout
        << "standard: 프레임워크 Motion·반사·레이어 샘플\n"
        << "1 타원 / 2 사각형 / 3 다각형 / 4 사용자 3D 경로+보간 / 5 원 / 6 왕복 꺾은선\n"
        << "Space 시간 정지·재개 / S 자전 기여 / G 전체 공전 기여\n"
        << "M 반사 변경 / I 크기 레이어 삽입·삭제 / H 도움말 / Esc 종료\n"
        << "초기 반사: YZ평면. M은 점대칭 -> Y축 직선대칭 -> 없음 -> YZ평면 순환\n"
        << "왼쪽 클릭: 물체의 entity/face ID 출력\n"
        << "7 Grid/거리 기반 배치 / 8 실내 미끄럼·낙하 / 0 기존 Motion 예제\n"
        << "9 선형보간·진자·사람형·그네→사람 계층 애니메이션\n"
        << "9번 관절: Tab/Backspace 선택, X/Y/Z 축, 좌우 회전, 상하 이동, PageUp/Down 피벗, +/- 균일 배율, R 복원\n"
        << "V 3뷰 전환 / D 단계 추적 로그 / F 실내 예제 바닥 개방 / Space 정지·재개\n";
}

/// 거리 기반 격자 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initGrid() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Grid;
    clock_.reset();
    gridBoxes_.clear();

    graphics::GridLayout grid;

    grid.origin = {-3, -.1f, -2};
    grid.extent = {6, .2f, 4};
    grid.cells = {6, 1, 4};

    for (int z = 0; z < 4; ++z)
        for (int x = 0; x < 6; ++x) {
            const auto center = grid.cellCenter({x, 0, z}), size = grid.relativeSize({.15f, 1, .22f});

            if (!center || !size ||
                !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, *size, *center,
                                                 (x + z) % 2 ? graphics::GlColor{.25f, .3f, .4f}
                                                             : graphics::GlColor{.4f, .45f, .55f}))
                return false;
        }

    if (!distancePath_.setPoints({{-2.5f, .35f, -1.5f}, {2.5f, .35f, -1.5f}, {2.5f, .35f, 1.5f}}))
        return false;

    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        const auto box =
            graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .5f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {.25f + .14f * i, .7f - .1f * i, .3f});

        if (!box || !graphics::PatternSystem::addNumber(scene, i + 1, {-.07f, -.1f, .205f}, .2f, {1, 1, 1}, *box))
            return false;
        gridBoxes_.push_back(*box);
    }
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {7, 6, 8};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 0, 0};
    app_.getCamera().orthographicHalfExtent = 4;

    return true;
}

/// 회전하는 방 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initRoom() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Room;
    clock_.reset();
    floorOpen_ = false;

    const auto room = graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene, {{-2, -1, -2}, {2, 2, 2}});
    const auto cube = graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.5f, .5f, .5f}, {0, -.75f, 0}, {1, .7f, .2f});

    if (!room || !cube)
        return false;
    room_ = *room;
    roomCube_ = *cube;
    roomBody_ = graphics::RoomBody{};
    roomBody_.localPosition = {0, -.75f, 0};
    roomBody_.halfExtent = {.25f, .25f, .25f};
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {5, 3, 8};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 0, 0};

    return true;
}

/// 선형보간과 그네·사람의 부모·자식 애니메이션을 구성한다.
bool Console::initAnimation() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Animation;
    clock_.reset();
    animation_.clear();
    jointControls_.clear();
    selectedJoint_ = 0;
    selectedAxis_ = 0;

    if (!graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {8, .15f, 6}, {0, -.15f, 0}, {.25f, .3f, .35f}))
        return false;

    graphics::SwingConfig swingConfig;

    swingConfig.pivot = {1, 3, 0};
    swingConfig.pendulum.amplitudeDegrees = 25;
    swingConfig.pendulum.periodSeconds = 3;

    const auto swing = graphics::AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider(scene, swingConfig);

    if (!swing)
        return false;
    animation_ = swing->animation;

    graphics::HumanoidConfig personConfig;

    personConfig.grid.frame = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), glm::vec3(-2, 0, 0));

    const auto person = graphics::AnimationSystem::addHumanoid(scene, personConfig);

    if (!person)
        return false;

    auto pose = graphics::HumanoidPose::seated();

    pose.hips = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};
    pose.knees = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};

    if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid(scene, *person, pose))
        return false;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        graphics::PendulumMotion sway;

        sway.amplitudeDegrees = 30;
        sway.periodSeconds = 2;
        sway.phaseDegrees = i == 0 ? 0 : 180;

        graphics::Motion motion;

        if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::pendulum(sway)))
            return false;

        const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, person->shoulders[i], motion);

        if (!track)
            return false;
        animation_.push_back(*track);
    }

    const auto marker = scene.addGroup();

    if (!marker || !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .4f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {1, .85f, .15f}, *marker))
        return false;

    graphics::LinearMotion move;

    move.from = {-2, .3f, 2};
    move.to = {2, .3f, 2};
    move.durationSeconds = 4;
    move.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;

    graphics::Motion motion;

    if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::linear(move)))
        return false;

    const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, *marker, motion);

    if (!track)
        return false;
    animation_.push_back(*track);

    const auto control = [&](graphics::EntityId id, const std::string& name) {
        std::size_t index = 0;

        while (index < animation_.size() && animation_[index].joint != id)
            ++index;

        if (index == animation_.size()) {
            auto fixed = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, id, graphics::Motion{});

            if (!fixed)
                return false;
            animation_.push_back(*fixed);
        }
        jointControls_.push_back({index, name, animation_[index].rest});

        return true;
    };

    const auto personControls = [&](const graphics::HumanoidRig& rig, const std::string& prefix) {
        if (!control(rig.root, prefix + " root") || !control(rig.pelvis, prefix + " pelvis") ||
            !control(rig.torso, prefix + " torso") || !control(rig.neck, prefix + " neck"))
            return false;

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
            const auto side = prefix + (i == 0 ? " left " : " right ");

            if (!control(rig.shoulders[i], side + "shoulder") || !control(rig.elbows[i], side + "elbow") ||
                !control(rig.hands[i], side + "hand") || !control(rig.hips[i], side + "hip") ||
                !control(rig.knees[i], side + "knee") || !control(rig.feet[i], side + "foot"))
                return false;
        }

        return true;
    };

    if (!control(swing->frame, "swing frame") || !control(swing->hinge, "swing hinge") ||
        !control(swing->seat, "swing seat") || !personControls(swing->person, "rider") ||
        !personControls(*person, "standing"))
        return false;
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {8, 5, 10};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 1.4f, 0};
    std::cout << "9: 노란 상자는 4초 편도 선형보간, 그네는 3초 주기 진자, 좌석의 사람은 계층 변환을 따릅니다.\n";
    printSelectedJoint();

    return graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, 0);
}

/// 선택한 관절의 기준 자세를 편집하고 같은 시각의 애니메이션에 반영한다.
bool Console::handleJointKey(int key) {
    if (jointControls_.empty())
        return false;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_TAB)
