        selectedJoint_ = (selectedJoint_ + 1) % jointControls_.size();
    else if (key == GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE)
        selectedJoint_ = (selectedJoint_ + jointControls_.size() - 1) % jointControls_.size();
    else if (key == GLFW_KEY_X || key == GLFW_KEY_Y || key == GLFW_KEY_Z)
        selectedAxis_ = key == GLFW_KEY_X ? 0 : key == GLFW_KEY_Y ? 1 : 2;
    else {
        auto& track = animation_[jointControls_[selectedJoint_].track];
        const auto saved = track.rest;

        if (key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT || key == GLFW_KEY_RIGHT) {
            glm::vec3 axis(0);

            axis[selectedAxis_] = 1;
            track.rest.rotation =
                track.rest.rotation *
                glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), glm::radians(key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT ? -5.0f : 5.0f), axis));
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_UP || key == GLFW_KEY_DOWN)
            track.rest.basis[selectedAxis_] += key == GLFW_KEY_UP ? .05f : -.05f;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP || key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_DOWN)
            track.rest.localBasis[selectedAxis_] += key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP ? .05f : -.05f;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD || key == GLFW_KEY_MINUS ||
                 key == GLFW_KEY_KP_SUBTRACT) {
            const bool grow = key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD;

            track.rest.scale = glm::vec3(std::clamp(track.rest.scale.x * (grow ? 1.1f : 1 / 1.1f), .1f, 3.0f));
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_R)
            track.rest = jointControls_[selectedJoint_].initialRest;
        else
            return false;

        if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(app_.getScene(), animation_, clock_.seconds)) {
            track.rest = saved;
            std::cerr << "관절 편집 거절: 기준 자세를 복원했습니다.\n";
        }
    }
    printSelectedJoint();

    return true;
}

/// 선택 관절·축·이동·피벗·균일 배율을 출력한다.
void Console::printSelectedJoint() const {
    if (jointControls_.empty())
        return;

    const auto& control = jointControls_[selectedJoint_];
    const auto& rest = animation_[control.track].rest;

    std::cout << "joint " << selectedJoint_ + 1 << '/' << jointControls_.size() << ": " << control.name
              << " axis=" << "XYZ"[selectedAxis_] << " position=" << rest.basis.x << ',' << rest.basis.y << ','
              << rest.basis.z << " pivot=" << rest.localBasis.x << ',' << rest.localBasis.y << ',' << rest.localBasis.z
              << " scale=" << rest.scale.x << '\n';
}

/// 프레임 시간으로 상태와 장면을 갱신한다.
void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    if (demo_ == Demo::Motion || !clock_.playing)
        return;

    if (!clock_.advance(dt)) {
        app_.requestClose();

        return;
    }

    auto& scene = app_.getScene();
    bool ok = true;

    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation) {
        ok = graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, clock_.seconds);
    } else if (demo_ == Demo::Grid) {
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < gridBoxes_.size(); ++i)
            ok = graphics::PatternSystem::follow(scene, gridBoxes_[i], distancePath_,
                                                 clock_.seconds + distancePath_.length() * i / 5) &&
                 ok;
    } else {
        const float angle = .4f * float(std::sin(clock_.seconds));
        const auto matrix = glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), angle, glm::vec3(0, 0, 1));
        graphics::Transform3D roomTransform;

        roomTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix);
        roomTransform.basis = glm::vec3(matrix * glm::vec4(0, .5f, 0, 1));
        ok =
            scene.setTransform(room_, roomTransform) && scene.setFaceVisible(room_, 5, !floorOpen_) &&
            graphics::CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(roomBody_, {{-2, -1, -2}, {2, 2, 2}}, matrix, dt, floorOpen_, false,
                                                    {0, -9.8f, 0}, {0, 0, .4f * float(std::cos(clock_.seconds))});

        graphics::Transform3D cubeTransform;

        cubeTransform.basis = roomBody_.position(matrix);

        if (!roomBody_.released)
            cubeTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix);
        ok = scene.setTransform(roomCube_, cubeTransform) && ok;
    }

    if (!ok)
        app_.requestClose();
}

/// 현재 장면을 지정한 뷰로 렌더링한다.
bool Console::onRender() {
    if (!threeViews_)
        return app_.renderScene();

    const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

    if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
        return true;

    return app_.renderViews(
        graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions()));
}
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/main.cpp
#include "Console.h"

/// 콜백을 등록하고 애플리케이션을 실행한다.
int main() {
    graphics::Application app;
    Console sample(app);

    app.setInitHandler([&](graphics::Application&) {
        return sample.initScene();
    });
    app.setKeyHandler([&](graphics::Application&, int key, int action) {
        sample.onKey(key, action);
    });
    app.setMouseHandler([&](graphics::Application&, const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {
        sample.onMouse(event);
    });
    app.setUpdateHandler([&](graphics::Application&, float dt) {
        sample.onUpdate(dt);
    });
    app.setRenderHandler([&](graphics::Application&) {
        return sample.onRender();
    });

    graphics::ApplicationConfig config;

    config.title = "standard - Motion, Grid, diagnostics and render passes";

    return app.run(config);
}
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/detail/Animation.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include "Internal.h"
#include <set>

namespace graphics {
namespace detail {
bool validJointRest(const Transform3D& rest) {
    return DiagramSystem::validTransform(rest) && rest.scale.x > 1e-6f &&
           std::abs(rest.scale.x - rest.scale.y) < 1e-6f && std::abs(rest.scale.x - rest.scale.z) < 1e-6f;
}
}

HumanoidPose HumanoidPose::seated() {
    HumanoidPose pose;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        pose.hips[i].x = -90;
        pose.knees[i].x = 90;
        pose.shoulders[i].x = -20;
        pose.elbows[i].x = -40;
    }

    return pose;
}

std::optional<JointAnimation> AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(const Scene& scene, EntityId joint,
                                                                    const Motion& motion) {
    const auto* component = scene.getComponent(joint);
    const auto sample = motion.sample(0);

    if (!component || !sample || !detail::validJointRest(component->transform) || !detail::rigidRoom(sample->parent) ||
        !detail::rigidRoom(sample->local)) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation",
                            "Need a joint with positive uniform rest scale and a rigid motion sample.");

        return {};
    }

    return JointAnimation{joint, component->transform, motion};
}

bool AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(Scene& scene, const std::vector<JointAnimation>& animation, double seconds) {
    if (!std::isfinite(seconds) || seconds < 0)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy",
                                   "Need nonnegative finite seconds.");

    std::set<EntityId> seen;
    std::vector<std::pair<EntityId, Transform3D>> staged;

    staged.reserve(animation.size());

    for (const auto& track : animation) {
        const auto sample = track.motion.sample(seconds);
        const auto rest = DiagramSystem::modelMatrix(track.rest);

        if (!scene.getComponent(track.joint) || !seen.insert(track.joint).second || !sample ||
            !detail::validJointRest(track.rest) || !detail::rigidRoom(sample->parent) ||
            !detail::rigidRoom(sample->local))
            return Diagnostics::failAt(
                __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy",
                "Need unique existing joints, positive uniform rest scales and rigid motion samples.");
        // World contributions are in the joint parent's space, before its rest pose.
        const auto matrix = sample->parent * rest * sample->local;
        Transform3D transform;

        transform.basis = glm::vec3(matrix[3]);
        transform.scale = track.rest.scale;
        transform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix) / track.rest.scale.x;

        if (!DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform))
            return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy",
                                       "Joint composition overflow.");
        staged.emplace_back(track.joint, transform);
    }

    for (const auto& item : staged)
        if (!scene.setTransform(item.first, item.second))
            return false;

    return true;
}

std::optional<HumanoidRig> AnimationSystem::addHumanoid(Scene& scene, const HumanoidConfig& config,
                                                        std::optional<EntityId> parent) {
    if (!config.grid.valid() || !detail::rigidRoom(config.grid.frame) || !std::isfinite(config.upperArmFraction) ||
        config.upperArmFraction <= 0 || config.upperArmFraction >= 1 || !std::isfinite(config.upperLegFraction) ||
        config.upperLegFraction <= 0 || config.upperLegFraction >= 1 || !detail::validColor(config.torsoColor) ||
        !detail::validColor(config.headColor) || !detail::validColor(config.leftColor) ||
        !detail::validColor(config.rightColor) || (parent && !scene.getComponent(*parent))) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "AnimationSystem::addHumanoid",
                            "Invalid grid, limb fractions, colors or parent.");

        return {};
    }

    Scene staged = scene;
    HumanoidRig rig;
    const auto extent = config.grid.extent;
    Transform3D rootTransform;

    rootTransform.basis = glm::vec3(config.grid.frame[3]);
    rootTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(config.grid.frame);

    const auto root = staged.addGroup(rootTransform);

    if (!root || !staged.setParent(*root, parent))
        return {};
    rig.root = *root;

    const auto pelvis =
        PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.root, config.grid.origin + extent * glm::vec3(.5f, .4f, .5f));

    if (!pelvis)
        return {};
    rig.pelvis = *pelvis;

    const auto torso = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.pelvis, {0, extent.y * .18f, 0});

    if (!torso)
        return {};
    rig.torso = *torso;

    const auto torsoMesh =
        PatternSystem::addBox(staged, extent * glm::vec3(.45f, .35f, .7f), {0, 0, 0}, config.torsoColor, rig.torso);
    const auto neck = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.torso, {0, extent.y * .22f, 0});

    if (!torsoMesh || !neck)
        return {};
    rig.torsoMesh = *torsoMesh;
    rig.neck = *neck;

    const auto head = PatternSystem::addBox(staged, extent * glm::vec3(.3f, .2f, .6f), {0, extent.y * .1f, 0},
                                            config.headColor, rig.neck);
    const auto nose = PatternSystem::addBox(staged, extent * glm::vec3(.1f, .05f, .2f),
                                            {0, extent.y * .1f, extent.z * .4f}, {1, .2f, .2f}, rig.neck);

    if (!head || !nose)
        return {};
    rig.head = *head;
    rig.nose = *nose;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        const float side = i == 0 ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
        const auto color = i == 0 ? config.leftColor : config.rightColor;
        const float upperArm = extent.y * .32f * config.upperArmFraction;
        const float forearm = extent.y * .32f - upperArm;
        const float thigh = extent.y * .4f * config.upperLegFraction;
        const float shin = extent.y * .4f - thigh;
        const auto shoulder = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.torso, {side * extent.x * .3f, extent.y * .14f, 0});
        const auto hip = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.pelvis, {side * extent.x * .15f, 0, 0});

        if (!shoulder || !hip)
            return {};
        rig.shoulders[i] = *shoulder;
        rig.hips[i] = *hip;

        const auto elbow = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, *shoulder, {0, -upperArm, 0});
        const auto knee = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, *hip, {0, -thigh, 0});

        if (!elbow || !knee)
            return {};
        rig.elbows[i] = *elbow;
        rig.knees[i] = *knee;

        const auto hand = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, *elbow, {0, -forearm, 0});
        const auto foot = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, *knee, {0, -shin, 0});
        const auto upper = PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {extent.x * .15f, upperArm, extent.z * .3f},
                                                 {0, -upperArm / 2, 0}, color, *shoulder);
        const auto lower = PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {extent.x * .15f, forearm, extent.z * .3f},
                                                 {0, -forearm / 2, 0}, color, *elbow);
        const auto thighMesh =
            PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {extent.x * .18f, thigh, extent.z * .4f}, {0, -thigh / 2, 0}, color, *hip);
        const auto shinMesh =
            PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {extent.x * .18f, shin, extent.z * .4f}, {0, -shin / 2, 0}, color, *knee);

        if (!hand || !foot || !upper || !lower || !thighMesh || !shinMesh)
            return {};
        rig.hands[i] = *hand;
        rig.feet[i] = *foot;
        rig.upperArms[i] = *upper;
        rig.forearms[i] = *lower;
        rig.thighs[i] = *thighMesh;
        rig.shins[i] = *shinMesh;
    }
    scene = std::move(staged);

    return rig;
}

bool AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid(Scene& scene, const HumanoidRig& rig, const HumanoidPose& pose) {
    const std::array<EntityId, 11> joints{rig.pelvis,       rig.torso,     rig.neck,      rig.shoulders[0],
                                          rig.shoulders[1], rig.elbows[0], rig.elbows[1], rig.hips[0],
                                          rig.hips[1],      rig.knees[0],  rig.knees[1]};
    const std::array<glm::vec3, 11> angles{pose.pelvisDegrees, pose.torsoDegrees, pose.neckDegrees, pose.shoulders[0],
                                           pose.shoulders[1],  pose.elbows[0],    pose.elbows[1],   pose.hips[0],
                                           pose.hips[1],       pose.knees[0],     pose.knees[1]};
    std::set<EntityId> seen;
    std::array<Transform3D, 11> transforms;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < joints.size(); ++i) {
        const auto* component = scene.getComponent(joints[i]);

        if (!component || !seen.insert(joints[i]).second || !detail::finiteVector(angles[i]))
            return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid",
                                       "Invalid joint or Euler angles.");

        glm::mat4 rotation(1);

        for (int axis = 0; axis < 3; ++axis) {
            glm::vec3 direction(0);

            direction[axis] = 1;

            const auto turn = TransformSystem::rotation({{0, 0, 0}, direction}, angles[i][axis]);

            if (!turn)
                return false;
            rotation = *turn * rotation;
        }
        transforms[i] = component->transform;
        transforms[i].rotation = glm::mat3(rotation);

        if (!DiagramSystem::validTransform(transforms[i]))
            return false;
    }

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < joints.size(); ++i)
        if (!scene.setTransform(joints[i], transforms[i]))
            return false;

    return true;
}

std::optional<SwingRig> AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider(Scene& scene, const SwingConfig& config,
                                                           std::optional<EntityId> parent) {
    Motion swingMotion;
    auto pendulum = config.pendulum;

    pendulum.axis.point = {0, 0, 0};
    pendulum.space = TransformSpace::Local;

    if (!detail::finiteVector(config.pivot) || !detail::positiveLengths(config.seatSize) ||
        !std::isfinite(config.length) || config.length <= 0 || !std::isfinite(config.ropeThickness) ||
        config.ropeThickness <= 0 || !std::isfinite(config.riderSwayDegrees) || config.riderSwayDegrees < 0 ||
        (parent && !scene.getComponent(*parent)) || !swingMotion.append(Motion::pendulum(pendulum))) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider",
                            "Invalid swing configuration or parent.");

        return {};
    }

    Scene staged = scene;
    SwingRig rig;
    Transform3D transform;

    transform.basis = config.pivot;

    const auto frame = staged.addGroup(transform);

    if (!frame || !staged.setParent(*frame, parent))
        return {};
    rig.frame = *frame;

    const auto hinge = PatternSystem::addJoint(staged, rig.frame, {0, 0, 0});

    if (!hinge)
        return {};
    rig.hinge = *hinge;

    const auto seat =
        PatternSystem::addBox(staged, config.seatSize, {0, -config.length, 0}, {.55f, .3f, .1f}, rig.hinge);

    if (!seat)
        return {};
    rig.seat = *seat;

    if (!PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {config.seatSize.x + .6f, .12f, .12f}, {0, 0, 0}, {.3f, .35f, .4f}, rig.frame))
        return {};

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        const float side = i == 0 ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
        const auto rope = PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {config.ropeThickness, config.length, config.ropeThickness},
                                                {side * config.seatSize.x * .43f, -config.length / 2, 0},
                                                {.75f, .75f, .75f}, rig.hinge);

        if (!rope || !PatternSystem::addBox(staged, {.12f, config.length + 1, .12f},
                                            {side * (config.seatSize.x / 2 + .2f), -(config.length + 1) / 2, 0},
                                            {.3f, .35f, .4f}, rig.frame))
            return {};
        rig.ropes[i] = *rope;
    }

    const auto person = addHumanoid(staged, config.person, rig.seat);

    if (!person || !poseHumanoid(staged, *person, HumanoidPose::seated()))
        return {};
    rig.person = *person;

    const auto* root = staged.getComponent(rig.person.root);
    auto personTransform = root->transform;
    const auto hipCenter = config.person.grid.origin + config.person.grid.extent * glm::vec3(.5f, .4f, .5f);
    const float thighHalfThickness = config.person.grid.extent.z * .2f;

    personTransform.basis =
        glm::vec3(0, config.seatSize.y / 2 + thighHalfThickness, 0) - personTransform.rotation * hipCenter;

    if (!staged.setTransform(rig.person.root, personTransform))
        return {};

    const auto swingTrack = createJointAnimation(staged, rig.hinge, swingMotion);
    PendulumMotion rider;

    rider.periodSeconds = pendulum.periodSeconds;
    rider.amplitudeDegrees = config.riderSwayDegrees;
    rider.phaseDegrees = std::remainder(pendulum.phaseDegrees, 360.0) + 180;

    Motion riderMotion;

    if (!riderMotion.append(Motion::pendulum(rider)))
        return {};

    const auto riderTrack = createJointAnimation(staged, rig.person.torso, riderMotion);

    if (!swingTrack || !riderTrack)
        return {};
    rig.animation = {*swingTrack, *riderTrack};

    if (!poseHierarchy(staged, rig.animation, 0))
        return {};
    scene = std::move(staged);

    return rig;
}
} // namespace graphics

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

여러 카메라로 같은 Scene 표시

몸체의 자식 관절을 만들고 문을 열거나 카메라를 돌린다. 이 함수들은 응용의 키 입력이나 누적 진행률을 Scene·Camera 상태로 바꾸는 작은 재사용 도구다.

주요 상태: anchor는 부모 공간의 관절 위치다. progress는 0~1 개방률이며 dt가 아니다. cameraFacingWall의 결과는 선택한 옆벽 번호다. selected가 비면 벽을 임의로 열지 않는다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

bool editParts(graphics::Scene& scene,graphics::EntityId body,graphics::EntityId room,graphics::Camera& camera) {
    auto joint=graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,body,{0,.5f,0});
    if(!joint||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,*joint,{1,0,0},30)) return false;
    auto polygon=graphics::PatternSystem::addPolygon(scene,{{0,0,0},{1,0,0},{0,1,0}},{1,1,0},body);
    if(!polygon||!graphics::PatternSystem::addNumber(scene,3,{0,1,0},.2f,{1,1,1},body)) return false;
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::foldFace(scene,body,0,{{0,.5f,0},{1,0,0}},45)) return false;
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::openFace(scene,body,0,.5f,{0,1,0},{0,.5f,0},true)) return false;
    auto selected=graphics::PatternSystem::cameraFacingWall(scene,room,camera);
    if(selected&&!graphics::PatternSystem::openRoomWall(scene,room,*selected,.5f)) return false;
    auto other=scene.addCuboid({1,1,1},{2,0,0}); if(!other) return false;
    auto staged=scene;
    const auto firstModel=scene.getComponent(body)->polyhedron;
    const auto secondModel=scene.getComponent(*other)->polyhedron;
    if(!staged.setModel(body,secondModel)||!staged.setModel(*other,firstModel)) return false;
    scene=std::move(staged);
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::moveCamera(camera,{.2f,0,0})
       ||!graphics::PatternSystem::yawCamera(camera,5)
       ||!graphics::PatternSystem::orbitCamera(camera,{{0,0,0},{0,1,0}},5)
       ||!graphics::PatternSystem::zoomCamera(camera,.9f)) return false;
    auto views=graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(camera,1100,800); return !views.empty();
}

이 단편의 각 조작은 별도의 입력 명령 사례다. 실제 앱에서는 같은 면을 foldFace와 openFace가 동시에 덮어쓰지 않게 모드를 정한다. 벽 표시 개방은 충돌 경계 제거와 별개다.

실제 저장소 소스 · openGL_3dAssign_22/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace {
graphics::EntityId requireEntity(std::optional<graphics::EntityId> id, const char* reason) {
    if (!id)
        throw std::runtime_error(reason);

    return *id;
}
}

graphics::EntityId Console::box(glm::vec3 size, glm::vec3 position, graphics::EntityId parent) {
    const auto id = requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(app_.getScene(), size, position, {1, 1, 1}, parent),
                                  "탱크 상자 생성 실패: 크기·위치·부모 ID를 확인하세요.");

    colored_.push_back(id);

    return id;
}

bool Console::initScene() {
    reset();
    std::cout << "실습 22: 같은 장면을 원근 / XZ 평면도 / XY 정면도로 표시\n"
              << "방향키 이동; d 하부 높이; t 중앙 자전; l 상부 교환\n"
              << "g 포신 반대 회전; p 깃대 반대 기울기; x/X,z/Z 카메라 이동\n"
              << "y/Y 카메라 시선 회전; r/R 공전(재입력 정지); O/o 색 변경; c 초기화; q 종료\n";

    return true;
}

void Console::reset() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    colored_.clear();
    pressed_.fill(false);
    position_ = glm::vec3(0);
    lowerTime_ = centralAngle_ = gunAngle_ = poleTime_ = swapProgress_ = 0;
    swapDirection_ = 1;
    cameraX_ = cameraZ_ = cameraYaw_ = cameraOrbit_ = 0;
    lowerRunning_ = centralRunning_ = gunRunning_ = poleRunning_ = swapRunning_ = swapStarted_ = false;
    colorVersion_ = 0;
    root_ = requireEntity(scene.addGroup(), "탱크 루트 생성 실패.");
    central_ = requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene, root_, {0, .6f, 0}), "중앙 관절 생성 실패.");

    const auto ground = requireEntity(scene.addGroup(), "바닥 그룹 생성 실패.");

    box({12, .1f, 12}, {0, -.05f, 0}, ground);

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i)
        lower_[i] = box({.65f, .6f, 1.8f}, {i == 0 ? -.8f : .8f, .3f, 0}, root_);
    box({2.3f, .25f, 1.4f}, {0, .125f, 0}, central_);

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        const float sign = i == 0 ? -1.0f : 1.0f;

        upper_[i] = requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene, central_, {sign * .65f, .25f, 0}),
                                  "상부 관절 생성 실패.");
        guns_[i] = requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene, upper_[i], {0, .3f, 0}),
                                 "포신 중심 피벗 생성 실패.");
        poles_[i] = requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene, upper_[i], {0, .6f, 0}),
                                  "깃대 밑점 피벗 생성 실패.");
        box({.65f, .6f, .8f}, {0, .3f, 0}, upper_[i]);

        box({.14f, .14f, 1.1f}, {0, -.05f, .95f}, guns_[i]);
        box({.08f, .85f, .08f}, {0, .425f, 0}, poles_[i]);
    }

    auto& camera = app_.getCamera();

    camera = graphics::Camera{};
    camera.position = {-5, 4.5f, 7};
    camera.target = {0, .7f, 0};
    camera.orthographic = false;
    camera.orthographicHalfExtent = 6.5f;
    app_.getRenderOptions() = graphics::RenderOptions{};
    app_.getRenderOptions().lighting = true;
    recolor();
    apply();
}

void Console::recolor() {
    const std::array<graphics::GlColor, 10> palette{graphics::GlColor{.3f, .35f, .4f},
                                                    {.25f, .6f, .3f},
                                                    {.55f, .35f, .2f},
                                                    {.2f, .55f, .75f},
                                                    {.7f, .75f, .2f},
                                                    {.9f, .4f, .15f},
                                                    {1, .8f, .2f},
                                                    {.4f, .7f, .8f},
                                                    {.7f, .25f, .6f},
                                                    {.6f, .9f, .4f}};

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < colored_.size(); ++i)
        if (!app_.getScene().setTint(colored_[i], palette[(i + colorVersion_) % palette.size()]))
            throw std::runtime_error("탱크 부품 색상 적용 실패.");
}

void Console::apply() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    if (!scene.setPosition(root_, position_))
        throw std::runtime_error("탱크 위치 적용 실패.");

    const float factor = 1 + .25f * std::sin(lowerTime_), height = .6f * factor;

    if (!scene.setPosition(central_, {0, height, 0}) ||
        !graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene, central_, {0, 1, 0}, centralAngle_))
        throw std::runtime_error("중앙 관절 변환 적용 실패.");

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        const float sign = i == 0 ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
        graphics::Transform3D low;

        low.basis = {sign * .8f, height * .5f, 0};
        low.scale = {1, factor, 1};

        const float a = 3.14159265f * swapProgress_;
        const glm::vec3 upperPosition{sign * .65f * std::cos(a), .25f, sign * .65f * std::sin(a)};

        if (!scene.setTransform(lower_[i], low) || !scene.setPosition(upper_[i], upperPosition) ||
            !graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene, guns_[i], {0, 1, 0}, sign * gunAngle_) ||
            !graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene, poles_[i], {1, 0, 0}, sign * 60 * std::sin(poleTime_)))
            throw std::runtime_error("탱크 부품 변환 적용 실패.");
    }
}

void Console::onKey(int key, int action, int mods) {
    const bool upper = ((mods & GLFW_MOD_SHIFT) != 0) != ((mods & GLFW_MOD_CAPS_LOCK) != 0);

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_X || key == GLFW_KEY_Z) {
        if (action == GLFW_PRESS || action == GLFW_RELEASE) {
            float& direction = key == GLFW_KEY_X ? cameraX_ : cameraZ_;

            direction = action == GLFW_RELEASE ? 0 : (upper ? -1.0f : 1.0f);
        }

        return;
    }

    if (key >= 0 && key <= GLFW_KEY_LAST) {
        if (action == GLFW_PRESS)
            pressed_[key] = true;
        else if (action == GLFW_RELEASE)
            pressed_[key] = false;
    }

    if (action != GLFW_PRESS)
        return;

    const float direction = upper ? 1.0f : -1.0f;

    switch (key) {
    case GLFW_KEY_D:
        lowerRunning_ = !lowerRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_T:
        centralRunning_ = !centralRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_G:
        gunRunning_ = !gunRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_P:
        poleRunning_ = !poleRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_L:
        if (!swapStarted_) {
            swapStarted_ = true;
            swapRunning_ = true;
        } else if (!swapRunning_ && (swapProgress_ == 0 || swapProgress_ == 1)) {
            swapDirection_ = -swapDirection_;
            swapRunning_ = true;
        } else
            swapRunning_ = !swapRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_Y:
        cameraYaw_ = cameraYaw_ == direction ? 0 : direction;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_R:
        cameraOrbit_ = cameraOrbit_ == direction ? 0 : direction;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_O:
        colorVersion_ = (colorVersion_ + 1) % 10;
        recolor();
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_C:
        reset();
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_Q:
    case GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE:
        app_.requestClose();
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}

void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    glm::vec3 movement{float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_RIGHT]) - float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_LEFT]), 0,
                       float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_DOWN]) - float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_UP])};

    if (glm::length(movement) > 0)
        position_ += glm::normalize(movement) * dt * 1.3f;

    position_.x = std::clamp(position_.x, -3.5f, 3.5f);
    position_.z = std::clamp(position_.z, -3.5f, 3.5f);

    if (lowerRunning_)
        lowerTime_ = std::fmod(lowerTime_ + dt, 6.283185307f);

    if (centralRunning_)
        centralAngle_ = std::fmod(centralAngle_ + 45 * dt, 360.0f);

    if (gunRunning_)
        gunAngle_ = std::fmod(gunAngle_ + 60 * dt, -360.0f);

    if (poleRunning_)
        poleTime_ = std::fmod(poleTime_ + dt, 6.283185307f);

    if (swapRunning_) {
        swapProgress_ = std::clamp(swapProgress_ + swapDirection_ * dt * .5f, 0.0f, 1.0f);

        if (swapProgress_ == 0 || swapProgress_ == 1)
            swapRunning_ = false;
    }

    auto camera = app_.getCamera();
    const glm::vec3 delta{cameraX_ * 2 * dt, 0, cameraZ_ * 2 * dt};

    if (!graphics::PatternSystem::moveCamera(camera, delta) ||
        (cameraYaw_ != 0 && !graphics::PatternSystem::yawCamera(camera, cameraYaw_ * 35 * dt)) ||
        (cameraOrbit_ != 0 &&
         !graphics::PatternSystem::orbitCamera(camera, {{0, 0, 0}, {0, 1, 0}}, cameraOrbit_ * 25 * dt)))
        throw std::runtime_error("카메라 갱신 실패: 위치·시선·회전축을 확인하세요.");
    app_.getCamera() = camera;
    apply();
}

void Console::onFocus(bool focused) {
    if (!focused) {
        pressed_.fill(false);
        cameraX_ = cameraZ_ = 0;
    }
}

bool Console::onRender() {
    const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

    if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
        return true;

    const auto passes =
        graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions());

    if (passes.size() != 3)
        throw std::runtime_error("세 카메라 뷰 생성 실패: 프레임버퍼 크기와 카메라를 확인하세요.");

    return app_.renderViews(passes);
}
실제 저장소 소스 · openGL_3dAssign_23/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <iostream>

namespace {
void require(bool ok, const char* message) {
    if (!ok)
        throw std::runtime_error(message);
}
}

bool Console::initScene() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    obstacles_.clear();
    body_ = graphics::CharacterBody{};
    body_.feet = {-3, 0, -3};
    body_.speed = 1;
    body_.height = 1.8f;
    body_.halfWidth = {.5f, .5f};
    doors_.fill(0);
    open_.fill(false);
    activeWall_.reset();
    walking_ = true;

    const auto stage = graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene, bounds_, false);

    if (!stage)
        return false;
    stage_ = *stage;

    for (std::size_t face = 0; face < 4; ++face) {
        if (!graphics::PatternSystem::openRoomWall(scene, stage_, face, 0))
            return false;
    }

    graphics::GridLayout grid;

    grid.origin = {-.5f, 0, -.3f};
    grid.extent = {1, 1.8f, .6f};

    const auto rig = graphics::PatternSystem::addWalkingRig(scene, grid);

    if (!rig)
        return false;
    rig_ = *rig;
    std::cout << "실습 23 난수 시드=" << randomSeed_ << "; 초기화 시 기존 난수 엔진의 다음 수열을 사용합니다.\n";

    std::vector<graphics::Bounds3> occupied{{{-3.8f, 0, -3.8f}, {-2.2f, 2, -2.2f}}};

    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        const glm::vec3 size{.9f + .2f * i, .6f + .25f * i, .9f + .15f * i};
        const auto box =
            graphics::CollisionSystem::randomFreeBox({{-4, 0, -4}, {4, size.y, 4}}, size, occupied, random_);

        if (!box)
            return false;

        if (!graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, size, (box->minimum + box->maximum) * .5f,
                                             {.3f + .2f * i, .5f, .8f - .2f * i}))
            return false;
        occupied.push_back(*box);
        obstacles_.push_back(*box);
    }
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {8, 5, 11};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 1, 0};
    app_.getRenderOptions() = graphics::RenderOptions{};
    app_.getRenderOptions().depthTest = true;
    app_.getRenderOptions().lighting = true;

    return graphics::PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig(scene, rig_, body_.feet, 0, 0, body_.speed);
}

void Console::onKey(int key, int action, int mods) {
    if (action != GLFW_PRESS && action != GLFW_REPEAT)
        return;

    const bool upper = ((mods & GLFW_MOD_SHIFT) != 0) != ((mods & GLFW_MOD_CAPS_LOCK) != 0);
    auto& camera = app_.getCamera();

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_X || key == GLFW_KEY_Z) {
        glm::vec3 delta(0);

        delta[key == GLFW_KEY_X ? 0 : 2] = upper ? -.35f : .35f;
        require(graphics::PatternSystem::moveCamera(camera, delta), "실습 23 카메라 이동 실패.");

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_Y) {
        require(graphics::PatternSystem::orbitCamera(camera, {camera.target, {0, 1, 0}}, upper ? 4.0f : -4.0f),
                "실습 23 카메라 공전 실패.");

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD)
        body_.speed = std::min(3.0f, body_.speed + .1f);

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_MINUS || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_SUBTRACT)
        body_.speed = std::max(0.0f, body_.speed - .1f);

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_W) {
        body_.direction = {0, 0, 1};
        walking_ = true;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_S) {
        body_.direction = {0, 0, -1};
        walking_ = true;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_A) {
        body_.direction = {-1, 0, 0};
        walking_ = true;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_D) {
        body_.direction = {1, 0, 0};
        walking_ = true;
    }

    if (action != GLFW_PRESS)
        return;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_O) {
        if (activeWall_)
            open_[*activeWall_] = !open_[*activeWall_];
        else {
            activeWall_ = graphics::PatternSystem::cameraFacingWall(app_.getScene(), stage_, camera);

            if (activeWall_)
                open_[*activeWall_] = true;
        }

        if (activeWall_)
            std::cout << "선택한 벽=" << *activeWall_ << (open_[*activeWall_] ? " 열기\n" : " 원상복귀\n");
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE)
        walking_ = !walking_;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_J)
        graphics::CollisionSystem::jump(body_, 5.5f);

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_I)
        require(initScene(), "실습 23 초기화 실패: 앞선 API 오류를 확인하세요.");

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_Q || key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE)
        app_.requestClose();
}

void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    const float requested = body_.speed;

    if (!walking_)
        body_.speed = 0;

    const auto result = graphics::CollisionSystem::stepCharacter(body_, bounds_, obstacles_, dt);

    body_.speed = requested;
    require(result.has_value(), "실습 23 캐릭터 충돌 계산 실패.");

    const float yaw = glm::degrees(std::atan2(body_.direction.x, body_.direction.z));

    require(graphics::PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig(app_.getScene(), rig_, body_.feet, yaw, body_.walkDistance,
                                                    body_.speed),
            "실습 23 로봇 관절 자세 적용 실패.");

    for (std::size_t face = 0; face < doors_.size(); ++face) {
        doors_[face] = std::clamp(doors_[face] + (open_[face] ? 1.0f : -1.0f) * dt, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        require(graphics::PatternSystem::openRoomWall(app_.getScene(), stage_, face, doors_[face]),
                "실습 23 벽 개방·복원 실패.");
    }

    if (activeWall_ && !open_[*activeWall_] && doors_[*activeWall_] == 0)
        activeWall_.reset();
}

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

의미 동작·육면체 모드·정점 입력 편집

설정과 작은 핸들러를 편집하여 기존 Scene/ECS에 동작을 연결한다. 세부 계약과 내부 코드 수정 순서는 MODE_ACTIONS.md, RENDER_CONFIGURATION.md, PATTERN_COMPOSITION.md를 읽는다.

주요 상태: mode는 설정과 이름 바인딩을 보관한다. Scene은 컴포넌트 데이터, MotionSystem은 시간, Renderer는 GPU 자원을 소유한다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

bool configureEditable(graphics::Application& app) {
    auto mode=std::make_shared<graphics::CuboidMode>();
    graphics::CuboidConfig box; box.name="box"; box.size={1,2,1};
    if(!mode->setCuboid(box)) return false;
    graphics::CuboidCommand command;
    command.operation=graphics::CuboidOperation::Translate; command.value={.1f,0,0};
    if(!mode->bindKey({GLFW_KEY_RIGHT,GLFW_PRESS,0,graphics::CuboidMode::action("box",command)})) return false;
    mode->setSetupHandler([](graphics::Application& app,graphics::CuboidMode& mode) {
        const auto id=mode.entity("box"); if(!id) return false;
        graphics::Motion motion;
        graphics::RotationMotion spin;
        if(!motion.append(graphics::Motion::rotation(spin))) return false;
        if(!motion.insertAction(1,graphics::SceneActions::visible(app.getScene(),*id,true))) return false;
        const auto track=app.getScene().getMotions().createMotion(motion); if(!track) return false;
        graphics::LayerConfig layer; layer.motion=*track;
        const auto binding=app.getScene().getMotions().pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(),layer);
        return binding && app.getScene().bindEntity(*id,*binding);
    });
    return graphics::bindCuboidMode(app,mode);
}
// Run before app.run(config), not inside an existing init callback.
// config.renderConfig.bindUniforms = [](const graphics::DrawUniformContext& context) {
//     return context.setUniform("uAmplitude", .1f);
// };

sample/seek는 의미 동작을 실행하지 않는다. 모션 갱신은 활성 동작을 실행한다. mode.apply는 Scene 전체를 교체한다. 사용자 셰이더 입력은 실제 바이트와 맞춰야 한다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/include/RenderConfig.h
#pragma once
#include "Model.h"
#include "../detail/Native.h"
#include <functional>

namespace graphics {
class Scene;

// Canonical CPU input for packing; independent of GL attribute locations.
struct RenderVertex {
    float position[3], color[3], uv[2], normal[3];
};

struct VertexAttribute {
    GLuint location = 0;
    GLint components = 3;
    GLenum type = GL_FLOAT;
    bool normalized = false;
    std::size_t offset = 0;
    bool integer = false; // ivec/uvec inputs use VertexAttribIPointer.
};

struct VertexLayout {
    std::size_t stride = sizeof(RenderVertex);
    std::vector<VertexAttribute> attributes{
        {0, 3, GL_FLOAT, false, offsetof(RenderVertex, position)},
        {1, 3, GL_FLOAT, false, offsetof(RenderVertex, color)},
        {2, 2, GL_FLOAT, false, offsetof(RenderVertex, uv)},
        {3, 3, GL_FLOAT, false, offsetof(RenderVertex, normal)}};
};

struct UniformBindings {
    std::string mvp = "uMVP", model = "uModel", normal = "uNormalMatrix";
    std::string texture = "uTexture", hasTexture = "uHasTexture", lighting = "uLighting";
    std::string lightDirection = "uLightDirection", lightColor = "uLightColor";
    std::string overrideColor = "uOverrideColor", outlineColor = "uOutlineColor", tint = "uTint";
};

struct DrawUniformContext {
    GLuint program;
    const Scene& scene;
    std::optional<EntityId> entity;
    std::optional<LineId> line;
    std::size_t face;
    glm::mat4 model, view, projection;
    bool depthOnly = false;


    /// [API] 현재 program의 추가 float uniform을 설정한다. 없는 이름이나 비유한 값은 거부한다.
    bool setUniform(const std::string& name, float value) const {
        const auto location = detail::gl().GetUniformLocation(program, name.c_str());
        if (location < 0 || !std::isfinite(value))
            return false;
        detail::gl().Uniform1f(location, value);
        return true;
    }


    /// [API] 현재 program의 추가 int/bool/sampler uniform을 설정한다.
    bool setUniform(const std::string& name, int value) const {
        const auto location = detail::gl().GetUniformLocation(program, name.c_str());
        if (location < 0)
            return false;
        detail::gl().Uniform1i(location, value);
        return true;
    }


    /// [API] 현재 program의 추가 vec3 uniform을 설정한다.
    bool setUniform(const std::string& name, const glm::vec3& value) const {
        const auto location = detail::gl().GetUniformLocation(program, name.c_str());
        if (location < 0 || !detail::finiteVector(value))
            return false;
        detail::gl().Uniform3f(location, value.x, value.y, value.z);
        return true;
    }


    /// [API] 현재 program의 추가 mat4 uniform을 설정한다.
    bool setUniform(const std::string& name, const glm::mat4& value) const {
        const auto location = detail::gl().GetUniformLocation(program, name.c_str());
        if (location < 0 || !detail::finiteMatrix(value))
            return false;
        detail::gl().UniformMatrix4fv(location, 1, GL_FALSE, &value[0][0]);
        return true;
    }
};

struct RenderConfig {
    VertexLayout vertexLayout;
    UniformBindings uniforms;
    // Must output exactly input.size()*stride bytes, preserving vertex order/count.
    std::function<bool(const std::vector<RenderVertex>&, std::vector<std::uint8_t>&)> packVertices;
    // Runs with program bound, after built-in uniforms, before each range/pass.
    std::function<bool(const DrawUniformContext&)> bindUniforms;
};
} // namespace graphics
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/include/Actions.h
#pragma once
#include "Patterns.h"

namespace graphics {
// Value-configured operations. Scene and camera references are non-owning.
class SceneActions {
public:
    static MotionAction transform(Scene& scene, EntityId id, Transform3D value) {
        return [&scene, id, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return scene.setTransform(id, value);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction position(Scene& scene, EntityId id, glm::vec3 value) {
        return [&scene, id, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return scene.setPosition(id, value);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction scale(Scene& scene, EntityId id, glm::vec3 value) {
        return [&scene, id, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            const auto* component = scene.getComponent(id);
            if (!component)
                return false;
            auto transform = component->transform;
            transform.scale = value;
            return scene.setTransform(id, transform);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction rotation(Scene& scene, EntityId id, glm::mat3 value) {
        return [&scene, id, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            const auto* component = scene.getComponent(id);
            if (!component)
                return false;
            auto transform = component->transform;
            transform.rotation = value;
            return scene.setTransform(id, transform);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction tint(Scene& scene, EntityId id, GlColor value) {
        return [&scene, id, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return scene.setTint(id, value);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction pivot(Scene& scene, EntityId id, glm::vec3 value) {
        return [&scene, id, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return scene.setLocalBasis(id, PivotMode::Custom, value);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction visible(Scene& scene, EntityId id, bool value) {
        return [&scene, id, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return scene.setVisible(id, value);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction faceVisible(Scene& scene, EntityId id, std::size_t face, bool value) {
        return [&scene, id, face, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return scene.setFaceVisible(id, face, value);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction faceRotation(Scene& scene, EntityId id, std::size_t face, FaceRotationConfig config) {
        return [&scene, id, face, config](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return PatternSystem::rotateFace(scene, id, face, config);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction faceMode(Scene& scene, EntityId id, std::size_t face, FaceTransformMode mode,
                                 bool preserveWorld = true) {
        return [&scene, id, face, mode, preserveWorld](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return scene.setFaceTransformMode(id, face, mode, preserveWorld);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction layer(Scene& scene, EntityId id, std::optional<LayerId> value) {
        return [&scene, id, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return value ? scene.bindEntity(id, *value) : scene.unbindEntity(id);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction motion(Scene& scene, LayerId layer, std::optional<MotionId> value) {
        return [&scene, layer, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return scene.getMotions().bindMotion(layer, value);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction parent(Scene& scene, EntityId id, std::optional<EntityId> value) {
        return [&scene, id, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            return scene.setParent(id, value);
        };
    }


    static MotionAction camera(Camera& camera, Camera value) {
        return [&camera, value](double, const MotionSample&) {
            if (!CameraSystem::validCamera(value))
                return false;
            camera = value;
            return true;
        };
    }
};
} // namespace graphics
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/include/CuboidMode.h
#pragma once
#include "Actions.h"

namespace graphics {
struct CuboidFaceConfig {
    Transform3D transform;
    GlColor color;
    bool visible = true;
    CullMode cull = CullMode::Inherit;
    FaceTransformMode mode = FaceTransformMode::Hierarchy;
    std::optional<FaceRotationConfig> rotation;
};
struct CuboidConfig {
    std::string name;
    glm::vec3 size{1};
    Transform3D transform;
    GlColor color;
    bool visible = true;
    std::array<CuboidFaceConfig, 6> faces;
    std::optional<Motion> motion;
};
struct CuboidModeConfig {
    std::vector<CuboidConfig> cuboids;
    Camera camera;
    RenderOptions renderOptions;
};

enum class CuboidOperation {
    Position, Translate, Rotate, Scale, Transform, Pivot, Visible, ToggleVisible, Layer, Motion, Playing
};
struct CuboidCommand {
    CuboidOperation operation = CuboidOperation::Position;
    glm::vec3 value{0}; // Rotate: local axis; Scale: absolute scale.
    float degrees = 0;
    Transform3D transform;
    bool enabled = true;
    std::optional<LayerId> layer;
    std::optional<MotionId> motion;
};
class CuboidMode;
using CuboidHandler = std::function<bool(Application&, CuboidMode&)>;
struct CuboidKeyBinding {
    int key = 0, action = GLFW_PRESS, mods = 0;
    CuboidHandler handler;
};

// Configuration and bindings are data. Scene stores ECS components; this class coordinates systems.
class CuboidMode {
    CuboidModeConfig config_;
    std::vector<CuboidKeyBinding> bindings_;
    std::map<std::string, EntityId> entities_;
    std::map<std::string, LayerId> layers_;
    std::map<std::string, MotionId> motions_;
    CuboidHandler setup_;
    std::function<bool(Application&, CuboidMode&, float)> update_;
public:
    /// [API] 초기 설정만 교체한다. 현재 Scene은 apply를 호출할 때 갱신된다.
    bool setConfig(const CuboidModeConfig& config);


    const CuboidModeConfig& getConfig() const {
        return config_;
    }


    /// [API] 같은 이름의 육면체 초기 설정을 교체하거나 새로 등록한다.
    bool setCuboid(const CuboidConfig& config);


    /// [API] 같은 key/action/mods 바인딩을 교체하거나 추가한다.
    bool bindKey(const CuboidKeyBinding& binding);


    bool unbindKey(int key, int action = GLFW_PRESS, int mods = 0);


    const std::vector<CuboidKeyBinding>& getBindings() const {
        return bindings_;
    }


    void setSetupHandler(CuboidHandler handler) {
        setup_ = std::move(handler);
    }


    void setUpdateHandler(std::function<bool(Application&, CuboidMode&, float)> handler) {
        update_ = std::move(handler);
    }


    /// [API] 별도 CPU Scene에 육면체와 모션을 구성하고 성공하면 장면 전체를 교체한다.
    bool apply(Application& app);


    bool update(Application& app, float dt);


    bool dispatch(Application& app, int key, int action, int mods);


    std::optional<EntityId> entity(const std::string& name) const;


    std::optional<LayerId> layer(const std::string& name) const;


    std::optional<MotionId> motion(const std::string& name) const;


    /// [API] 실행 시 이름을 해석하므로 초기 설정 재적용 뒤에도 같은 바인딩을 사용한다.
    static CuboidHandler action(std::string name, CuboidCommand command);
};
/// [API] 육면체 모드의 init/update/수정키 콜백을 설치한다. 기존 해당 핸들러를 교체한다.
bool bindCuboidMode(Application& app, std::shared_ptr<CuboidMode> mode);
} // namespace graphics
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/detail/CuboidMode.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include <algorithm>

namespace graphics {
namespace {
bool validFaceRotation(const std::optional<FaceRotationConfig>& rotation) {
    if (!rotation) return true;
    if (!std::isfinite(rotation->degrees)) return false;
    if (rotation->kind == FaceRotationKind::EdgeHinge) return rotation->edge < 4;
    return (rotation->kind == FaceRotationKind::SelfSpin || rotation->kind == FaceRotationKind::ModelOrbit) &&
           detail::finiteVector(rotation->axis) && glm::length(glm::dvec3(rotation->axis)) > 1e-12;
}
bool validCuboid(const CuboidConfig& c) {
    if (c.name.empty() || !detail::finiteVector(c.size) || c.size.x <= 0 || c.size.y <= 0 || c.size.z <= 0 ||
        !detail::validColor(c.color) || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(c.transform))
        return false;
    for (const auto& face : c.faces)
        if (!DiagramSystem::validTransform(face.transform) || !detail::validColor(face.color) || !validFaceRotation(face.rotation) ||
            (face.mode != FaceTransformMode::Hierarchy && face.mode != FaceTransformMode::Independent) ||
            (face.cull != CullMode::Inherit && face.cull != CullMode::None &&
             face.cull != CullMode::Back && face.cull != CullMode::Front))
            return false;
    return !c.motion || bool(c.motion->sample(0));
}
template<class Id> std::optional<Id> lookup(const std::map<std::string, Id>& map, const std::string& name) {
    const auto found = map.find(name);
    return found == map.end() ? std::nullopt : std::optional<Id>(found->second);
}
}

bool CuboidMode::setConfig(const CuboidModeConfig& config) {
    const auto& options = config.renderOptions;
    if (!CameraSystem::validCamera(config.camera) || !detail::finiteVector(options.lightDirection) ||
        glm::length(options.lightDirection) < 1e-12f || !detail::validColor(options.lightColor) ||
        !detail::validColor(options.outlineColor) || !std::isfinite(options.background.x) ||
        !std::isfinite(options.background.y) || !std::isfinite(options.background.z) ||
        !std::isfinite(options.background.w) || (options.cull != CullMode::None &&
        options.cull != CullMode::Back && options.cull != CullMode::Front))
        return false;
    std::vector<std::string> names;
    for (const auto& cuboid : config.cuboids) {
        if (!validCuboid(cuboid) || std::find(names.begin(), names.end(), cuboid.name) != names.end())
            return false;
        names.push_back(cuboid.name);
    }
    config_ = config;
    return true;
}
bool CuboidMode::setCuboid(const CuboidConfig& cuboid) {
    auto next = config_;
    const auto found = std::find_if(next.cuboids.begin(), next.cuboids.end(),
                                    [&](const auto& c) { return c.name == cuboid.name; });
    if (found == next.cuboids.end()) next.cuboids.push_back(cuboid);
    else *found = cuboid;
    return setConfig(next);
}
bool CuboidMode::bindKey(const CuboidKeyBinding& binding) {
    if (!binding.handler || (binding.action != GLFW_PRESS && binding.action != GLFW_RELEASE && binding.action != GLFW_REPEAT))
        return false;
    const auto found = std::find_if(bindings_.begin(), bindings_.end(), [&](const auto& b) {
        return b.key == binding.key && b.action == binding.action && b.mods == binding.mods;
    });
    if (found == bindings_.end()) bindings_.push_back(binding);
    else *found = binding;
    return true;
}
bool CuboidMode::unbindKey(int key, int action, int mods) {
    const auto found = std::find_if(bindings_.begin(), bindings_.end(), [&](const auto& b) {
        return b.key == key && b.action == action && b.mods == mods;
    });
    if (found == bindings_.end()) return false;
    bindings_.erase(found);
    return true;
}
std::optional<EntityId> CuboidMode::entity(const std::string& name) const { return lookup(entities_, name); }
std::optional<LayerId> CuboidMode::layer(const std::string& name) const { return lookup(layers_, name); }
std::optional<MotionId> CuboidMode::motion(const std::string& name) const { return lookup(motions_, name); }

bool CuboidMode::apply(Application& app) {
    Scene staged;
    std::map<std::string, EntityId> entities;
    std::map<std::string, LayerId> layers;
    std::map<std::string, MotionId> motions;
    for (const auto& c : config_.cuboids) {
        if (!validCuboid(c)) return false;
        auto model = DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Cube, c.size);
        for (std::size_t face = 0; face < c.faces.size(); ++face)
            model.faces[face].color = c.faces[face].color;
        const auto id = staged.addModel(model, c.transform, PivotMode::Preserve);
        if (!id || !staged.setTransform(*id, c.transform) || !staged.setTint(*id, c.color) ||
            !staged.setVisible(*id, c.visible)) return false;
        for (std::size_t face = 0; face < c.faces.size(); ++face)
            if (!staged.setFaceTransform(*id, face, c.faces[face].transform, true) ||
                !staged.setFaceVisible(*id, face, c.faces[face].visible) ||
                !staged.setFaceCullMode(*id, face, c.faces[face].cull) ||
                (c.faces[face].rotation && !PatternSystem::rotateFace(staged, *id, face, *c.faces[face].rotation))) return false;
        entities.emplace(c.name, *id);
        if (c.motion) {
            const auto motion = staged.getMotions().createMotion(*c.motion);
            if (!motion) return false;
            LayerConfig layer;
            layer.motion = *motion;
            const auto idLayer = staged.getMotions().pushLayer(MotionSystem::rootLayer(), layer);
            if (!idLayer || !staged.bindEntity(*id, *idLayer)) return false;
            layers.emplace(c.name, *idLayer);
            motions.emplace(c.name, *motion);
        }
        // Freeze independent faces only after the initial motion/layer binding exists.
        for (std::size_t face = 0; face < c.faces.size(); ++face)
            if (!staged.setFaceTransformMode(*id, face, c.faces[face].mode)) return false;
    }
    app.getScene() = std::move(staged);
    app.getCamera() = config_.camera;
    app.getRenderOptions() = config_.renderOptions;
    entities_ = std::move(entities);
    layers_ = std::move(layers);
    motions_ = std::move(motions);
    const auto setup = setup_;
    return !setup || setup(app, *this);
}
bool CuboidMode::update(Application& app, float dt) {
    const auto handler = update_;
    return !handler || handler(app, *this, dt);
}
bool CuboidMode::dispatch(Application& app, int key, int action, int mods) {
    for (const auto& binding : bindings_)
        if (binding.key == key && binding.action == action && binding.mods == mods) {
            const auto handler = binding.handler;
            return handler(app, *this);
        }
    return true; // Unbound keys are normal.
}
CuboidHandler CuboidMode::action(std::string name, CuboidCommand command) {
    return [name = std::move(name), command](Application& app, CuboidMode& mode) {
        const auto id = mode.entity(name);
        auto& scene = app.getScene();
        if (!id) return false;
        const auto* component = scene.getComponent(*id);
        if (!component) return false;
        auto value = component->transform;
        MotionAction action;
        switch (command.operation) {
        case CuboidOperation::Position: action = SceneActions::position(scene, *id, command.value); break;
        case CuboidOperation::Translate:
            action = SceneActions::position(scene, *id, value.basis + command.value); break;
        case CuboidOperation::Rotate: {
            const auto rotation = TransformSystem::rotation({glm::vec3(0), command.value}, command.degrees);
            if (!rotation) return false;
            value.rotation = value.rotation * glm::mat3(*rotation);
            action = SceneActions::transform(scene, *id, value); break;
        }
        case CuboidOperation::Scale: action = SceneActions::scale(scene, *id, command.value); break;
        case CuboidOperation::Transform: action = SceneActions::transform(scene, *id, command.transform); break;
        case CuboidOperation::Pivot: action = SceneActions::pivot(scene, *id, command.value); break;
        case CuboidOperation::Visible: action = SceneActions::visible(scene, *id, command.enabled); break;
        case CuboidOperation::ToggleVisible: action = SceneActions::visible(scene, *id, !component->visible); break;
        case CuboidOperation::Layer: action = SceneActions::layer(scene, *id, command.layer); break;
        case CuboidOperation::Motion: {
            const auto layer = scene.getEntityLayer(*id);
            return layer && scene.getMotions().bindMotion(*layer, command.motion);
        }
        case CuboidOperation::Playing: {
            const auto layer = scene.getEntityLayer(*id);
            const auto* state = layer ? scene.getMotions().getLayer(*layer) : nullptr;
            return state && state->config.motion && scene.getMotions().setMotionPlaying(*state->config.motion, command.enabled);
        }
        default: return false;
        }
        return action(0, MotionSample{});
    };
}
bool bindCuboidMode(Application& app, std::shared_ptr<CuboidMode> mode) {
    if (!mode) return false;
    app.setInitHandler([mode](Application& app) { return mode->apply(app); });
    app.setUpdateHandler([mode](Application& app, float dt) {
        if (!mode->update(app, dt)) app.requestClose();
    });
    app.setModifiedKeyHandler([mode](Application& app, int key, int action, int mods) {
        if (!mode->dispatch(app, key, action, mods)) app.requestClose();
    });
    return true;
}
} // namespace graphics

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

면의 독립·계층과 힌지·자전·공전

육면체의 변환 상속과 면 자체의 회전 중심을 별도 설정으로 조합한다.

주요 상태: FaceTransformMode는 상속, FaceRotationKind는 축의 위치/방향, FaceAnimationSpace는 면 SRT와 추가 행렬의 적용 순서다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

bool faceMotion(graphics::Scene& scene,graphics::EntityId box,std::size_t face) {
    graphics::Transform3D pose; pose.basis={0,.2f,0};
    if(!scene.setFaceSRT(box,face,pose)) return false;
    if(!scene.setFaceTransformMode(box,face,graphics::FaceTransformMode::Independent)) return false;
    graphics::FaceRotationConfig rotation;
    rotation.kind=graphics::FaceRotationKind::EdgeHinge;
    rotation.edge=0; rotation.degrees=90;
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateFace(scene,box,face,rotation)) return false;
    rotation.kind=graphics::FaceRotationKind::SelfSpin; rotation.axis={0,1,0};
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateFace(scene,box,face,rotation)) return false;
    rotation.kind=graphics::FaceRotationKind::ModelOrbit;
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateFace(scene,box,face,rotation)) return false;
    graphics::Motion actions;
    if(!actions.append(graphics::Motion::action(graphics::SceneActions::faceRotation(scene,box,face,rotation)))) return false;
    return actions.insertAction(1,graphics::SceneActions::faceMode(scene,box,face,graphics::FaceTransformMode::Hierarchy));
}

독립 면은 전환 순간의 기준을 고정하며 기본 계층 모드는 본체 변환을 따른다. 위 회전 호출은 각 종류의 시연이며 마지막 행렬이 최종 값이다. 자세 보존과 적용 순서는 FACE_TRANSFORMS.md를 읽는다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/include/Model.h
#pragma once

#include "Diagnostics.h"
#include "../dependencies/glm/glm/glm.hpp"
#include "../dependencies/glm/glm/gtc/matrix_transform.hpp"
#include <array>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <limits>
#include <memory>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <utility>

namespace graphics {

constexpr int WINDOW_WIDTH = 800;
constexpr int WINDOW_HEIGHT = 600;
// Initial allocation hints, not scene capacity limits.
constexpr std::size_t MAX_ENTITIES = 50;
constexpr std::size_t MAX_LINES = 500;

using EntityId = std::size_t;
using LineId = std::size_t;

struct GlColor {
    float red = 1.0f, green = 1.0f, blue = 1.0f;
};
enum class GLShape { None, Cube, Tetra, Pyramid, Custom, Imported };
enum class GLFace { None, Rect, Triangle, Mesh };
enum class PivotMode { Preserve, BottomCenter, Custom };
enum class OutlineMode { None, FeatureEdges, AllTriangleEdges, Explicit };
enum class CullMode { Inherit, None, Back, Front };
enum class FaceTransformMode { Hierarchy, Independent };
enum class FaceAnimationSpace { Local, Model };

struct Transform3D {
    glm::vec3 basis{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 localBasis{0.0f};
    glm::mat3 rotation{1.0f};
    glm::vec3 scale{1.0f};
};

struct TextureImage {
    std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> rgba;


    // Row zero maps to UV v=0. File decoding flips top-down images by default.
    /// [API] 이미지 너비·높이와 RGBA 바이트 수가 맞는지 검사한다.
    bool valid() const {
        return width && height && width <= 16384 && height <= 16384 && rgba.size() == std::size_t(width) * height * 4;
    }
};

struct Material {
    GlColor color;
    std::shared_ptr<const TextureImage> texture;
    bool useTexture = false;
    bool repeatTexture = true;
};

struct Vertex {
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 normal{0.0f};
    glm::vec2 uv{0.0f};
    GlColor color;
};

struct PlaneFigure {
    GLFace shape = GLFace::Mesh;
    std::vector<glm::vec3> localVertices;
    GlColor color;
    glm::vec3 normal{0.0f};
    // Compatibility flag: true enables child-local SRT, never world detachment.
    bool independence = false;
    Transform3D transform;
    bool visible = true;
    glm::mat4 localAnimationMatrix{1.0f};
    std::vector<GlColor> vertexColors;
    std::vector<glm::vec3> vertexNormals;
    std::vector<glm::vec2> texcoords;
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> triangleIndices;
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> outlineIndices; // GL_LINES pairs, mesh-local indices.
    Material material;
    OutlineMode outline = OutlineMode::None;
    float creaseAngleDegrees = 1.0f;
    std::string name;
    CullMode cull = CullMode::Inherit;
    FaceTransformMode transformMode = FaceTransformMode::Hierarchy;
    glm::mat4 transformFrame{1.0f}; // Parent-local correction, or a frozen world frame.
    FaceAnimationSpace animationSpace = FaceAnimationSpace::Local;


    /// [API] 정점 목록에서 하나의 면 데이터를 만든다. 모델/Scene 등록은 수행하지 않는다.
    static PlaneFigure fromVertices(const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices, const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& triangles) {
        PlaneFigure mesh;

        mesh.triangleIndices = triangles;

        for (const auto& vertex : vertices) {
            mesh.localVertices.push_back(vertex.position);
            mesh.vertexNormals.push_back(vertex.normal);
            mesh.texcoords.push_back(vertex.uv);
            mesh.vertexColors.push_back(vertex.color);
        }

        return mesh;
    }
};

using Mesh = PlaneFigure;

struct Polyhedron {
    GLShape shape = GLShape::Custom;
    std::vector<PlaneFigure> faces;
    std::string name;


    /// [API] 모델이 보관한 면/메시 배열의 참조를 반환한다. const 오버로드는 읽기 전용이다.
    std::vector<Mesh>& meshes() {
        return faces;
    }


    /// [API] 모델이 보관한 면/메시 배열의 참조를 반환한다. const 오버로드는 읽기 전용이다.
    const std::vector<Mesh>& meshes() const {
        return faces;
    }
};

using Model = Polyhedron;

struct Component_Poly {
    Polyhedron polyhedron;
    Transform3D transform;
    glm::vec3 sideLengths{1.0f};
    bool visible = true;
    PivotMode pivotMode = PivotMode::Preserve;
    std::optional<EntityId> parent; // Local SRT is relative to this entity.
    GlColor tint;                   // Per-entity multiplier; does not propagate to children.
    bool transformOnly = false;
};

struct FaceId {
    EntityId entityId = 0;
    std::size_t faceIndex = 0;
};

struct Line3D {
    glm::vec3 start{0.0f}, end{0.0f};
    GlColor color;
    std::optional<EntityId> owner;
};

namespace detail {
inline bool finiteVector(const glm::vec3& v) {
    return std::isfinite(v.x) && std::isfinite(v.y) && std::isfinite(v.z);
}


inline bool finiteMatrix(const glm::mat4& m) {
    for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
        for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)
            if (!std::isfinite(m[c][r]))
                return false;

    return true;
}


inline bool validColor(GlColor c) {
    return std::isfinite(c.red) && std::isfinite(c.green) && std::isfinite(c.blue);
}


inline std::vector<std::uint32_t> triangles(const Mesh& mesh) {
    if (!mesh.triangleIndices.empty())
        return mesh.triangleIndices;

    if (mesh.shape == GLFace::Triangle)
        return {0, 1, 2};

    if (mesh.shape == GLFace::Rect)
        return {0, 1, 2, 0, 2, 3};

    return {};
}


inline bool validMesh(const Mesh& m) {
    if (m.cull != CullMode::Inherit && m.cull != CullMode::None && m.cull != CullMode::Back &&
        m.cull != CullMode::Front)
        return false;

    if ((m.transformMode != FaceTransformMode::Hierarchy && m.transformMode != FaceTransformMode::Independent) ||
        !finiteMatrix(m.transformFrame) ||
        (m.animationSpace != FaceAnimationSpace::Local && m.animationSpace != FaceAnimationSpace::Model))
        return false;

    if (m.localVertices.size() < 3 || !validColor(m.color) || !validColor(m.material.color) ||
        !finiteVector(m.normal) || !finiteMatrix(m.localAnimationMatrix) || !std::isfinite(m.creaseAngleDegrees) ||
        m.creaseAngleDegrees < 0 || m.creaseAngleDegrees > 180)
        return false;

    const auto count = m.localVertices.size();

    if (count > std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max())
        return false;

    if ((!m.vertexColors.empty() && m.vertexColors.size() != count) ||
        (!m.vertexNormals.empty() && m.vertexNormals.size() != count) ||
        (!m.texcoords.empty() && m.texcoords.size() != count) || (m.material.texture && !m.material.texture->valid()) ||
        (m.material.useTexture && (!m.material.texture || m.texcoords.empty())))
        return false;

    for (const auto& v : m.localVertices)
        if (!finiteVector(v))
            return false;

    for (const auto& v : m.vertexNormals)
        if (!finiteVector(v))
            return false;

    for (const auto& v : m.texcoords)
        if (!std::isfinite(v.x) || !std::isfinite(v.y))
            return false;

    for (auto c : m.vertexColors)
        if (!validColor(c))
            return false;

    const auto indices = triangles(m);

    if (indices.empty() || indices.size() % 3 || m.outlineIndices.size() % 2)
        return false;

    for (auto i : indices)
        if (i >= count)
            return false;

    for (auto i : m.outlineIndices)
        if (i >= count)
            return false;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < indices.size(); i += 3) {
        const auto a = glm::dvec3(m.localVertices[indices[i]]);
        const auto b = glm::dvec3(m.localVertices[indices[i + 1]]);
        const auto c = glm::dvec3(m.localVertices[indices[i + 2]]);
        const auto normal = glm::cross(b - a, c - a);
        const double area = glm::dot(normal, normal);

        if (!std::isfinite(area) || area <= 0)
            return false;
    }

    return true;
}


inline std::pair<glm::vec3, glm::vec3> bounds(const Model& model) {
    glm::vec3 low(std::numeric_limits<float>::max()), high(-std::numeric_limits<float>::max());

    for (const auto& face : model.faces)
        for (const auto& p : face.localVertices) {
            low = glm::min(low, p);
            high = glm::max(high, p);
        }

    return {low, high};
}


inline glm::vec3 bottomCenter(const Model& model) {
    const auto box = bounds(model);

    return {box.first.x * .5f + box.second.x * .5f, box.first.y, box.first.z * .5f + box.second.z * .5f};
}


inline glm::mat4 transformMatrix(const Transform3D& t) {
    glm::mat4 m = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), t.basis);

    m *= glm::mat4(t.rotation);

    return glm::translate(glm::scale(m, t.scale), -t.localBasis);
}


inline glm::mat4 faceSrtMatrix(const Mesh& face) {
    return face.independence
        ? glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), face.transform.localBasis) * transformMatrix(face.transform)
        : glm::mat4(1);
}


inline glm::mat4 faceLocalMatrix(const Mesh& face) {
    const auto child = faceSrtMatrix(face);
    return face.animationSpace == FaceAnimationSpace::Model
        ? face.localAnimationMatrix * child : child * face.localAnimationMatrix;
}


inline glm::mat4 faceMatrix(const Component_Poly& c, const Mesh& face) {
    const auto parent = face.transformMode == FaceTransformMode::Hierarchy ? transformMatrix(c.transform) : glm::mat4(1);
    return parent * face.transformFrame * faceLocalMatrix(face);
}

} // namespace detail
} // namespace graphics
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/detail/Scene.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include "Internal.h"
#include <fstream>
#include <initializer_list>
#include <iostream>
#include <sstream>
#include <utility>

namespace graphics {
namespace detail {

bool positiveLengths(const glm::vec3& value) {
    return finiteVector(value) && value.x > 0.0f && value.y > 0.0f && value.z > 0.0f;
}

std::uint64_t nextGeometryRevision() {
    // 장면 복사·교체 시에도 다른 형상에 같은 revision을 부여하지 않는다.
    // Scene과 Renderer는 한 렌더링 스레드에서 사용한다.
    static std::uint64_t revision = 0;

    return ++revision;
}

void appendFace(Polyhedron& polyhedron, GLFace shape, std::initializer_list<glm::vec3> vertices, GlColor color) {
    PlaneFigure face;

    face.shape = shape;
    face.localVertices = vertices;
    face.color = color;
    face.normal = glm::normalize(
        glm::cross(face.localVertices[1] - face.localVertices[0], face.localVertices[2] - face.localVertices[0]));
    polyhedron.faces.push_back(face);
}

} // namespace detail

using detail::finiteVector;
using detail::finiteMatrix;
using detail::validColor;
using detail::positiveLengths;
using detail::nextGeometryRevision;
using detail::appendFace;

Scene::Scene() {
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    polyId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES);
    components_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES);
    tetraFaceId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES * 4);
    cubeFaceId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES * 6);
    pyramidFaceId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES * 5);
    lines_.reserve(MAX_LINES);
}

std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addGroup(const Transform3D& transform) {
    if (!DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform))
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addGroup",
                                "Rejected condition: !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            std::nullopt);

    const auto id = components_.size();
    Component_Poly group;

    group.polyhedron.shape = GLShape::None;
    group.transform = transform;
    group.sideLengths = glm::vec3(0);
    group.transformOnly = true;
    components_.push_back(group);
    polyId_.push_back(id);
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();

    return id;
}

bool Scene::setTint(EntityId id, GlColor color) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || !validColor(color))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTint",
            "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size() || !validColor(color)",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    components_[id].tint = color;

    return true;
}

std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addSphere(float radius, const glm::vec3& position, unsigned slices, unsigned stacks) {
    Transform3D transform;

    transform.basis = position;

    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makeSphere(radius, slices, stacks), transform);
}

std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addCone(float radius, float height, const glm::vec3& position, unsigned slices) {
    Transform3D transform;

    transform.basis = position;

    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makeCone(radius, height, slices), transform);
}

std::optional<EntityId> Scene::push_entity(const Polyhedron& polyhedron, const Transform3D& transform) {
    if (!DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(polyhedron) || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)) {
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(
                __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::push_entity",
                "Rejected condition: !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(polyhedron) || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)",
                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            std::nullopt);
    }

    glm::vec3 minimum = polyhedron.faces[0].localVertices[0];
    glm::vec3 maximum = minimum;

    for (const PlaneFigure& face : polyhedron.faces) {
        for (const glm::vec3& vertex : face.localVertices) {
            minimum = glm::min(minimum, vertex);
            maximum = glm::max(maximum, vertex);
        }
    }

    if (!finiteVector(maximum - minimum))
        return (
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::push_entity",
                                "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(maximum - minimum)",
                                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."),
            std::nullopt);

    const EntityId id = components_.size();

    components_.push_back({polyhedron, transform, maximum - minimum});
    polyId_.push_back(id);

    std::vector<FaceId>* faceIds = nullptr;

    if (polyhedron.shape == GLShape::Cube)
        faceIds = &cubeFaceId_;

    if (polyhedron.shape == GLShape::Tetra)
        faceIds = &tetraFaceId_;

    if (polyhedron.shape == GLShape::Pyramid)
        faceIds = &pyramidFaceId_;

    if (faceIds)
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < polyhedron.faces.size(); ++i)
            faceIds->push_back({id, i});
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();

    return id;
}

std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addCuboid(const glm::vec3& sideLengths, const glm::vec3& worldPosition) {
    Transform3D transform;

    transform.basis = worldPosition;

    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Cube, sideLengths), transform);
}

std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addRightTetrahedron(const glm::vec3& edgeLengths, const glm::vec3& worldPosition) {
    Transform3D transform;

    transform.basis = worldPosition;

    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Tetra, edgeLengths), transform);
}

std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addPyramid(const glm::vec3& sideLengths, const glm::vec3& worldPosition) {
    Transform3D transform;

    transform.basis = worldPosition;

    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Pyramid, sideLengths), transform);
}

bool Scene::setFaceVisible(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex, bool visible) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVisible",
            "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex].visible = visible;

    return true;
}

bool Scene::setFaceCullMode(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex, CullMode mode) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size() ||
        (mode != CullMode::Inherit && mode != CullMode::None && mode != CullMode::Back && mode != CullMode::Front))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Raster, "Scene::setFaceCullMode",
                                   "Invalid entity, face index or culling mode.",
                                   "Check the model's face list and CullMode enum.", id);
    components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex].cull = mode;

    return true;
}

bool Scene::setFaceLocalMatrix(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex, const glm::mat4& matrix, FaceAnimationSpace space) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size() || !finiteMatrix(matrix) ||
        (space != FaceAnimationSpace::Local && space != FaceAnimationSpace::Model)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceLocalMatrix",
            "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size() || !finiteMatrix(matrix)",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex].localAnimationMatrix = matrix;
    components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex].animationSpace = space;

    return true;
}

bool Scene::setFaceVertexColors(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex, const std::vector<GlColor>& colors) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVertexColors",
            "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }

    PlaneFigure& face = components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];

    if (!colors.empty() && colors.size() != face.localVertices.size()) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVertexColors",
            "Rejected condition: !colors.empty() && colors.size() != face.localVertices.size()",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }

    for (GlColor color : colors) {
        if (!validColor(color)) {
            return Diagnostics::failAt(
                __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVertexColors",
                "Rejected condition: !validColor(color)",
                "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
        }
    }
    face.vertexColors = colors;
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();

    return true;
}

const Component_Poly* Scene::getComponent(EntityId id) const {
    return id < components_.size() ? &components_[id] : nullptr;
}

bool Scene::setPosition(EntityId id, const glm::vec3& worldPosition) {
    // For a parented entity this position is in parent coordinates.
    if (id >= components_.size() || !finiteVector(worldPosition)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setPosition",
            "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || !finiteVector(worldPosition)",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }

    auto candidate = components_[id].transform;

    candidate.basis = worldPosition;

    return setTransform(id, candidate);
}

bool Scene::setSideLengths(EntityId id, const glm::vec3& sideLengths) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || !positiveLengths(sideLengths))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setSideLengths",
            "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size()||!positiveLengths(sideLengths)",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);

    const auto& c = components_[id];

    if (c.transformOnly)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setSideLengths", "Rejected condition: c.transformOnly",
            "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);

    auto replacement = c.polyhedron;
    const auto box = detail::bounds(replacement);
