#pragma once
#include "Application.h"
#include "GridLayout.h"
#include <random>

namespace graphics {
struct Bounds3 {
    glm::vec3 minimum{-1}, maximum{1};


    /// [API] 해당 데이터가 계산에 필요한 유효 범위와 유한성 조건을 만족하는지 반환한다.
    bool valid() const;


    /// [API] 점이 margin만큼 안쪽으로 줄인 Bounds3 안에 있는지 검사한다.
    bool contains(const glm::vec3& point, const glm::vec3& margin = glm::vec3(0)) const;


    /// [API] 두 축 정렬 경계 상자가 겹치는지 검사한다.
    bool overlaps(const Bounds3& other) const;
};

struct PathPose {
    glm::vec3 position{0}, tangent{0, 0, 1};
    float yawDegrees = 0;
};

class DistancePath {
    std::vector<glm::vec3> points_;
    std::vector<double> distances_;

public:
    /// [API] 연속 점들의 누적 거리 경로를 구성한다. closed면 마지막에서 처음으로 연결한다.
    bool setPoints(const std::vector<glm::vec3>& points, bool closed = false);


    /// [API] 경로 전체 누적 길이를 반환한다. 경로가 비어 있으면 0이다.
    double length() const {
        return distances_.empty() ? 0 : distances_.back();
    }


    /// [API] 누적 거리와 Once/Loop/PingPong 정책에서 위치·접선·yaw를 샘플링한다.
    std::optional<PathPose> sample(double distance, Playback playback = Playback::Loop) const;
};

struct AnimationClock {
    double seconds = 0;
    bool playing = true;


    /// [API] playing일 때만 누적 시간을 늘린다. dt와 누적 값의 유한성·범위를 검사한다.
    bool advance(double dt);


    /// [API] 누적 시간을 0으로 되돌리고 재생 상태를 켠다.
    void reset() {
        seconds = 0;
        playing = true;
    }
};

struct RoomBody {
    glm::vec3 localPosition{0}, localVelocity{0}, halfExtent{.2f};
    glm::vec3 worldPosition{0}, worldVelocity{0};
    bool released = false;


    /// [API] 이탈 전에는 roomWorld로 로컬 중심을 월드로 변환하고 이탈 후에는 worldPosition을 반환한다.
    glm::vec3 position(const glm::mat4& roomWorld) const {
        return released ? worldPosition : glm::vec3(roomWorld * glm::vec4(localPosition, 1));
    }
};

struct CharacterBody {
    glm::vec3 feet{0}, direction{0, 0, 1};
    glm::vec2 halfWidth{.25f};
    float height = 1.6f, speed = 1, verticalSpeed = 0;
    double walkDistance = 0;
    bool grounded = true;
};

struct CharacterStep {
    bool blocked = false, turned = false, landed = false;
};

class CollisionSystem {
public:
    // Rigid, kinematic room. Boxes stay aligned to its axes until release.
    /// [API] 방 내부에서는 로컬 반사/중력을 계산하고 released 뒤에는 월드 중력으로 적분한다.
    static bool stepRoomBody(RoomBody& body, const Bounds3& room, const glm::mat4& roomWorld, float dt, bool floorOpen,
                             bool elastic, const glm::vec3& gravity = glm::vec3(0, -9.8f, 0),
                             const glm::vec3& angularVelocity = glm::vec3(0));


    /// [API] 접지 상태에서만 위쪽 속도를 설정한다. 공중 입력 거절은 정상적인 false다.
    static bool jump(CharacterBody& body, float speed);


    /// [API] 수평 이동·장애물/경계 반전·중력·윗면 착지·지지면 이탈을 하위 단계로 계산한다.
    static std::optional<CharacterStep> stepCharacter(CharacterBody& body, const Bounds3& stage,
                                                      const std::vector<Bounds3>& obstacles, float dt,
                                                      float gravity = 9.8f);


    /// [API] 제한 횟수 안에서 occupied와 겹치지 않는 배치를 찾는다. 난수 엔진의 시드·수명은 호출자가 관리한다.
    static std::optional<Bounds3> randomFreeBox(const Bounds3& area, const glm::vec3& size,
                                                const std::vector<Bounds3>& occupied, std::mt19937& random,
                                                unsigned attempts = 128);
};

struct JointRig {
    EntityId root = 0, head = 0;
    std::array<EntityId, 2> shoulders{}, hips{};
};

enum class FaceRotationKind { EdgeHinge, SelfSpin, ModelOrbit };
struct FaceRotationConfig {
    FaceRotationKind kind = FaceRotationKind::EdgeHinge;
    std::size_t edge = 0; // Cyclic boundary edge of the original polygon.
    glm::vec3 axis{0, 1, 0}; // Model-local direction for self spin / model orbit.
    double degrees = 0; // Absolute angle, not a frame increment.
};

class PatternSystem {
public:
    /// [API] 직선 → 안쪽 코너 중심 원호 → 직선의 거리에서 위치·접선·yaw를 계산한다. 끝에서 처음으로 순환한다.
    static std::optional<PathPose> conveyorCornerPose(double distance, const glm::vec3& start, const glm::vec3& pivot,
                                                      float radius, float endZ);


    /// [API] 양수 크기의 중심 기준 모델을 만들고 부모 로컬 위치·색·부모 관계를 설정한다.
    static std::optional<EntityId> addBox(Scene& scene, const glm::vec3& size, const glm::vec3& position, GlColor color,
                                          std::optional<EntityId> parent = std::nullopt);


    /// [API] 점 목록으로 단일 다각형 면을 만들고 색·부모 관계와 함께 Scene에 등록한다. 오목 다각형의 자동 분할을 가정하지 않는다.
    static std::optional<EntityId> addPolygon(Scene& scene, const std::vector<glm::vec3>& points, GlColor color,
                                              std::optional<EntityId> parent = std::nullopt);


    /// [API] 여섯 면의 방을 만들고 바깥 winding에 Front cull, 안쪽 조명 법선을 적용한다. face 0..3은 옆벽, 4 천장, 5 바닥이다.
    static std::optional<EntityId> addRoom(Scene& scene, const Bounds3& bounds, bool frontOpen = true);


    /// [API] 부모에 부착된 transform-only 그룹을 만든다. 표시 메시 크기와 회전 연결 위치를 분리한다.
    static std::optional<EntityId> addJoint(Scene& scene, EntityId parent, const glm::vec3& anchor);


    /// [API] 연결 위치와 크기를 유지하면서 회전 기저를 변경한다.
    static bool rotateJoint(Scene& scene, EntityId joint, const glm::vec3& axis, float degrees);


    /// [API] DistancePath 샘플을 엔티티 위치와 +Z 기준 Y 회전에 반영한다.
    static bool follow(Scene& scene, EntityId entity, const DistancePath& path, double distance,
                       const glm::vec3& offset = glm::vec3(0), bool orient = true);


    /// [API] 0~9를 선분으로 구성한다. 부모 로컬 XY 평면의 표시이며 일반 폰트/텍스트 렌더러는 아니다.
    static bool addNumber(Scene& scene, unsigned digit, const glm::vec3& position, float height, GlColor color,
                          std::optional<EntityId> parent = std::nullopt);


    /// [API] 지정 회전축을 중심으로 면 로컬 애니메이션 행렬을 계산한다.
    static bool foldFace(Scene& scene, EntityId entity, std::size_t face, const AxisLine& hinge, float degrees);


    /// [API] 원래 모서리 힌지·면 중심 자전·원본 모델 중심 공전을 구분하여 면 회전 행렬을 설정한다.
    static bool rotateFace(Scene& scene, EntityId entity, std::size_t face, const FaceRotationConfig& config);


    /// [API] 진행률 [0,1]로 이동 또는 상단 피벗 Y축 축소를 적용한다. 완전 개방은 숨기고 0 배율 특이행렬을 피한다.
    static bool openFace(Scene& scene, EntityId entity, std::size_t face, float progress, const glm::vec3& displacement,
                         const glm::vec3& topPivot, bool scaleOpen);


    /// [API] 시선 target-position과 옆벽의 월드 바깥 법선 내적이 가장 큰 양수인 면을 선택한다. 외부 카메라에서 보이는 맞은편 내부 벽이며 수직 시선에는 선택이 없을 수 있다.
    static std::optional<std::size_t> cameraFacingWall(const Scene& scene, EntityId room, const Camera& camera);


    /// [API] 선택 옆벽의 원래 중심을 기준으로 모든 축을 균등 축소/복원하며 완전 개방 시 숨긴다.
    static bool openRoomWall(Scene& scene, EntityId room, std::size_t face, float progress);


    /// [API] 셀 격자를 지그재그 순서로 방문하는 정수 인덱스 목록을 만든다.
    static std::vector<glm::ivec3> snakeCells(const glm::ivec3& cells);


    /// [API] 두 값의 차이가 허용 오차 이하인지 검사한다.
    static bool aligned(float first, float second, float tolerance);


    /// [API] 좌표를 양수 간격으로 반올림한다. 실패 시 입력 점을 그대로 유지한다.
    static glm::vec3 snap(const glm::vec3& point, float spacing);


    /// [API] 화면 픽셀을 ray로 바꾸어 평면 교차 위치를 얻는다. 평행/뒤쪽 교차는 빈 결과다.
    static std::optional<glm::vec3> screenOnPlane(const Camera& camera, double x, double y,
                                                  const PickViewport& viewport, const glm::vec3& planePoint,
                                                  const glm::vec3& normal);


    /// [API] 시점과 주목점을 동일하게 이동해 시선 방향을 보존한다.
    static bool moveCamera(Camera& camera, const glm::vec3& offset);


    /// [API] 시점은 고정하고 카메라 로컬 up축을 기준으로 시선을 회전한다.
    static bool yawCamera(Camera& camera, float degrees);


    /// [API] 월드 축에 대해 시점·주목점·up을 함께 변환한다. 축을 target에 두면 target은 고정된다.
    static bool orbitCamera(Camera& camera, const AxisLine& axis, float degrees);


    /// [API] 원근은 target까지 거리, 직각은 orthographicHalfExtent를 배율로 바꾼다.
    static bool zoomCamera(Camera& camera, float factor);


    /// [API] 원근·XZ 평면도·XY 정면도 카메라와 픽셀 영역을 구성한다. 상단 뷰의 up은 -Z다.
    static std::vector<RenderPass> threeViews(const Camera& perspective, int width, int height,
                                              const RenderOptions& options = RenderOptions{});


    /// [API] 그리드 비율로 머리·코·몸통·어깨·엉덩이·팔·다리 계층을 조립하고 ID 묶음을 반환한다.
    static std::optional<JointRig> addWalkingRig(Scene& scene, const GridLayout& bodyGrid);


    /// [API] 루트 이동/yaw와 실제 이동 거리 기반 팔다리 반대 위상을 적용한다. 속도는 보행 진폭에 반영한다.
    static bool poseWalkingRig(Scene& scene, const JointRig& rig, const glm::vec3& feet, float yawDegrees,
                               double walkDistance, float speed);
};
} // namespace graphics
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/framework/detail/Patterns.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include "Internal.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

namespace graphics {
std::optional<PathPose> PatternSystem::conveyorCornerPose(double distance, const glm::vec3& start,
                                                          const glm::vec3& pivot, float radius, float endZ) {
    const double straight = double(pivot.x) - start.x, arc = 1.5707963267948966 * radius, last = double(endZ) - pivot.z;

    if (!detail::finiteVector(start) || !detail::finiteVector(pivot) || !std::isfinite(distance) ||
        !std::isfinite(radius) || radius <= 0 || !std::isfinite(endZ) || straight <= 0 || last <= 0 ||
        std::abs(start.z - (pivot.z - radius)) > 1e-4f) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "conveyorCornerPose",
                            "Invalid XZ conveyor geometry.");

        return {};
    }

    double d = std::fmod(distance, straight + arc + last);

    if (d < 0)
        d += straight + arc + last;

    PathPose p;

    if (d < straight) {
        p.position = start + glm::vec3(float(d), 0, 0);
        p.tangent = {1, 0, 0};
    } else if (d < straight + arc) {
        const float a = float((d - straight) / radius);

        p.position = {pivot.x + radius * std::sin(a), start.y, pivot.z - radius * std::cos(a)};
        p.tangent = {std::cos(a), 0, std::sin(a)};
    } else {
        p.position = {pivot.x + radius, start.y, pivot.z + float(d - straight - arc)};
        p.tangent = {0, 0, 1};
    }
    p.yawDegrees = glm::degrees(std::atan2(p.tangent.x, p.tangent.z));

    return p;
}

bool Bounds3::valid() const {
    return detail::finiteVector(minimum) && detail::finiteVector(maximum) && maximum.x > minimum.x &&
           maximum.y > minimum.y && maximum.z > minimum.z;
}

bool Bounds3::contains(const glm::vec3& p, const glm::vec3& margin) const {
    return valid() && detail::finiteVector(p) && detail::finiteVector(margin) && margin.x >= 0 && margin.y >= 0 &&
           margin.z >= 0 && p.x >= minimum.x + margin.x && p.x <= maximum.x - margin.x && p.y >= minimum.y + margin.y &&
           p.y <= maximum.y - margin.y && p.z >= minimum.z + margin.z && p.z <= maximum.z - margin.z;
}

bool Bounds3::overlaps(const Bounds3& b) const {
    return valid() && b.valid() && minimum.x < b.maximum.x && maximum.x > b.minimum.x && minimum.y < b.maximum.y &&
           maximum.y > b.minimum.y && minimum.z < b.maximum.z && maximum.z > b.minimum.z;
}

bool DistancePath::setPoints(const std::vector<glm::vec3>& points, bool closed) {
    if (points.size() < 2)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "DistancePath::setPoints",
                                   "Need at least two points.");

    auto candidate = points;

    for (const auto& p : candidate)
        if (!detail::finiteVector(p))
            return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "DistancePath::setPoints",
                                       "Path contains NaN/Inf.");

    if (closed && glm::length(glm::dvec3(candidate.front()) - glm::dvec3(candidate.back())) > 1e-10)
        candidate.push_back(candidate.front());

    std::vector<double> distances{0};

    for (std::size_t i = 1; i < candidate.size(); ++i) {
        const double segment = glm::length(glm::dvec3(candidate[i]) - glm::dvec3(candidate[i - 1]));

        if (!std::isfinite(segment) || segment <= 1e-10 || !std::isfinite(distances.back() + segment))
            return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "DistancePath::setPoints",
                                       "Zero-length, repeated or overflowing path segment.");
        distances.push_back(distances.back() + segment);
    }
    points_ = std::move(candidate);
    distances_ = std::move(distances);

    return true;
}

std::optional<PathPose> DistancePath::sample(double distance, Playback playback) const {
    const double total = length();

    if (total <= 0 || !std::isfinite(distance)) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "DistancePath::sample",
                            "Configure a nonempty path and finite distance.");

        return {};
    }

    double sign = 1;

    if (playback == Playback::Once)
        distance = std::clamp(distance, 0.0, total);
    else if (playback == Playback::Loop) {
        distance = std::fmod(distance, total);

        if (distance < 0)
            distance += total;
    } else if (playback == Playback::PingPong) {
        distance = std::fmod(distance, 2 * total);

        if (distance < 0)
            distance += 2 * total;

        if (distance > total) {
            distance = 2 * total - distance;
            sign = -1;
        }
    } else {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "DistancePath::sample",
                            "Invalid Playback value.");

        return {};
    }

    const auto end = std::upper_bound(distances_.begin(), distances_.end(), distance);
    const std::size_t i = end == distances_.end() ? distances_.size() - 1 : std::size_t(end - distances_.begin());
    const float weight = float((distance - distances_[i - 1]) / (distances_[i] - distances_[i - 1]));
    PathPose pose;

    pose.position = glm::mix(points_[i - 1], points_[i], weight);
    pose.tangent = glm::vec3(glm::normalize(glm::dvec3(points_[i]) - glm::dvec3(points_[i - 1])) * sign);
    pose.yawDegrees = glm::degrees(std::atan2(pose.tangent.x, pose.tangent.z));

    return pose;
}

bool AnimationClock::advance(double dt) {
    if (!std::isfinite(dt) || dt < 0 || !std::isfinite(seconds) || seconds < 0 || !std::isfinite(seconds + dt))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "AnimationClock::advance",
                                   "Time must be finite, nonnegative and within range.");

    if (playing)
        seconds += dt;

    return true;
}

namespace detail {
bool rigidRoom(const glm::mat4& matrix) {
    const auto r = glm::mat3(matrix), identity = glm::transpose(r) * r;

    return TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix) && std::abs(glm::determinant(r) - 1) < .001f &&
           glm::length(identity[0] - glm::vec3(1, 0, 0)) < .001f &&
           glm::length(identity[1] - glm::vec3(0, 1, 0)) < .001f &&
           glm::length(identity[2] - glm::vec3(0, 0, 1)) < .001f;
}

bool horizontalOverlap(const CharacterBody& b, const glm::vec3& feet, const Bounds3& box) {
    return feet.x + b.halfWidth.x > box.minimum.x && feet.x - b.halfWidth.x < box.maximum.x &&
           feet.z + b.halfWidth.y > box.minimum.z && feet.z - b.halfWidth.y < box.maximum.z;
}
}

bool CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(RoomBody& body, const Bounds3& room, const glm::mat4& roomWorld, float dt,
                                   bool floorOpen, bool elastic, const glm::vec3& gravity,
                                   const glm::vec3& angularVelocity) {
    if (!room.valid() || !detail::rigidRoom(roomWorld) || !detail::finiteVector(gravity) ||
        !detail::finiteVector(angularVelocity) || !detail::finiteVector(body.localPosition) ||
        !detail::finiteVector(body.localVelocity) || !detail::positiveLengths(body.halfExtent) ||
        !detail::finiteVector(body.worldPosition) || !detail::finiteVector(body.worldVelocity) || !std::isfinite(dt) ||
        dt < 0 || dt > .25f)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(
            __FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody",
            "Need a rigid room transform, finite body state, positive half extents and dt in [0,.25].");

    const auto low = room.minimum + body.halfExtent, high = room.maximum - body.halfExtent;

    if (low.x >= high.x || low.y >= high.y || low.z >= high.z)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody",
                                   "Body does not fit inside the room.");

    auto next = body;
    const int steps = std::max(1, int(std::ceil(dt * 120)));
    const float h = dt / steps;
    const auto rotation = glm::mat3(roomWorld);
    const auto localGravity = glm::transpose(rotation) * gravity;

    for (int s = 0; s < steps; ++s) {
        if (next.released) {
            next.worldVelocity += gravity * h;
            next.worldPosition += next.worldVelocity * h;
            continue;
        }

        if (!elastic)
            next.localVelocity += localGravity * h;
        next.localPosition += next.localVelocity * h;

        if (floorOpen && next.localPosition.y < low.y) {
            next.released = true;
            next.worldPosition = glm::vec3(roomWorld * glm::vec4(next.localPosition, 1));
            next.worldVelocity = rotation * next.localVelocity +
                                 glm::cross(angularVelocity, next.worldPosition - glm::vec3(roomWorld[3]));
            continue;
        }

        for (int axis = 0; axis < 3; ++axis) {
            if (elastic && !(floorOpen && axis == 1)) {
                const double width = double(high[axis]) - low[axis];
                const double p = double(next.localPosition[axis]) - low[axis];
                double folded = std::fmod(p, 2 * width);

                if (folded < 0)
                    folded += 2 * width;

                const bool reflected = folded > width;

                next.localPosition[axis] = float(low[axis] + (reflected ? 2 * width - folded : folded));

                if (reflected)
                    next.localVelocity[axis] = -next.localVelocity[axis];

                if (next.localPosition[axis] <= low[axis])
                    next.localVelocity[axis] = std::abs(next.localVelocity[axis]);

                if (next.localPosition[axis] >= high[axis])
                    next.localVelocity[axis] = -std::abs(next.localVelocity[axis]);
            } else {
                if (next.localPosition[axis] < low[axis] && !(floorOpen && axis == 1)) {
                    next.localPosition[axis] = low[axis];
                    next.localVelocity[axis] =
                        elastic ? std::abs(next.localVelocity[axis]) : std::max(0.0f, next.localVelocity[axis]);
                }

                if (next.localPosition[axis] > high[axis]) {
                    next.localPosition[axis] = high[axis];
                    next.localVelocity[axis] =
                        elastic ? -std::abs(next.localVelocity[axis]) : std::min(0.0f, next.localVelocity[axis]);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (!detail::finiteVector(next.localPosition) || !detail::finiteVector(next.localVelocity) ||
        !detail::finiteVector(next.worldPosition) || !detail::finiteVector(next.worldVelocity))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody",
                                   "Simulation overflow; original state was preserved.");
    body = next;

    return true;
}

bool CollisionSystem::jump(CharacterBody& body, float speed) {
    if (!std::isfinite(speed) || speed <= 0)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "CollisionSystem::jump",
                                   "Jump speed must be positive and finite.");

    if (!body.grounded)
        return false; // Normal input rejection, not an API error.
    body.verticalSpeed = speed;
    body.grounded = false;

    return true;
}

std::optional<CharacterStep> CollisionSystem::stepCharacter(CharacterBody& body, const Bounds3& stage,
                                                            const std::vector<Bounds3>& obstacles, float dt,
                                                            float gravity) {
    if (!stage.valid() || !detail::finiteVector(body.feet) || !detail::finiteVector(body.direction) ||
        !std::isfinite(body.speed) || body.speed < 0 || !std::isfinite(body.height) || body.height <= 0 ||
        !std::isfinite(body.halfWidth.x) || !std::isfinite(body.halfWidth.y) || body.halfWidth.x <= 0 ||
        body.halfWidth.y <= 0 || !std::isfinite(body.verticalSpeed) || !std::isfinite(body.walkDistance) ||
        !std::isfinite(dt) || dt < 0 || dt > .25f || !std::isfinite(gravity) || gravity <= 0) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "CollisionSystem::stepCharacter",
                            "Invalid bounds, body dimensions, speed, gravity or dt.");

        return {};
    }

    if (stage.maximum.x - stage.minimum.x <= 2 * body.halfWidth.x ||
        stage.maximum.z - stage.minimum.z <= 2 * body.halfWidth.y) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "CollisionSystem::stepCharacter",
                            "Character does not fit on the stage.");

        return {};
    }

    float spacing = std::min(body.halfWidth.x, body.halfWidth.y) * .5f;

    for (const auto& box : obstacles) {
        if (!box.valid()) {
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "CollisionSystem::stepCharacter",
                                "Invalid obstacle bounds.");

            return {};
        }
        spacing = std::min(spacing, std::min(box.maximum.x - box.minimum.x, box.maximum.z - box.minimum.z) * .5f);
    }

    const double count =
        std::ceil(std::max(double(dt) * 120, double(body.speed) * dt / std::max(double(spacing), 1e-9)));

    if (count > 4096) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "CollisionSystem::stepCharacter",
                            "Motion exceeds the substep budget; reduce speed or dt.");

        return {};
    }

    auto next = body;
    CharacterStep event;

    next.direction.y = 0;

    const double directionLength = glm::length(glm::dvec3(next.direction));

    next.direction = directionLength > 1e-10 ? glm::vec3(glm::dvec3(next.direction) / directionLength) : glm::vec3(0);

    const int steps = std::max(1, int(count));
    const float h = dt / steps;

    for (int i = 0; i < steps; ++i) {
        const auto previous = next.feet;
        auto candidate = previous + next.direction * (next.speed * h);
        bool blocked =
            candidate.x - next.halfWidth.x < stage.minimum.x || candidate.x + next.halfWidth.x > stage.maximum.x ||
            candidate.z - next.halfWidth.y < stage.minimum.z || candidate.z + next.halfWidth.y > stage.maximum.z;

        for (const auto& box : obstacles)
            if (previous.y < box.maximum.y - 1e-4f && previous.y + next.height > box.minimum.y + 1e-4f &&
                detail::horizontalOverlap(next, candidate, box))
                blocked = true;

        if (blocked) {
            next.direction = -next.direction;
            candidate = previous;
            event.blocked = event.turned = true;
        }
        next.feet = candidate;
        next.walkDistance += glm::length(glm::dvec2(candidate.x - previous.x, candidate.z - previous.z));

        bool supported = std::abs(next.feet.y - stage.minimum.y) < 1e-4f;

        for (const auto& box : obstacles)
            if (detail::horizontalOverlap(next, next.feet, box) && std::abs(next.feet.y - box.maximum.y) < 1e-4f)
                supported = true;

        if (next.grounded && !supported)
            next.grounded = false;

        if (!next.grounded) {
            const float oldY = next.feet.y;

            next.verticalSpeed -= gravity * h;

            float newY = oldY + next.verticalSpeed * h;
            float landing = stage.minimum.y;

            for (const auto& box : obstacles)
                if (detail::horizontalOverlap(next, next.feet, box)) {
                    if (next.verticalSpeed <= 0 && oldY >= box.maximum.y - 1e-4f && newY <= box.maximum.y)
                        landing = std::max(landing, box.maximum.y);

                    if (next.verticalSpeed > 0 && oldY + next.height <= box.minimum.y &&
                        newY + next.height >= box.minimum.y) {
                        newY = box.minimum.y - next.height;
                        next.verticalSpeed = 0;
                    }
                }

            if (next.verticalSpeed <= 0 && newY <= landing && oldY >= landing - 1e-4f) {
                newY = landing;
                next.verticalSpeed = 0;
                next.grounded = true;
                event.landed = true;
            }
            next.feet.y = newY;
        }
    }

    if (!detail::finiteVector(next.feet) || !std::isfinite(next.walkDistance) || !std::isfinite(next.verticalSpeed)) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "CollisionSystem::stepCharacter",
                            "Simulation overflow; original state was preserved.");

        return {};
    }
    body = next;

    return event;
}

std::optional<Bounds3> CollisionSystem::randomFreeBox(const Bounds3& area, const glm::vec3& size,
                                                      const std::vector<Bounds3>& occupied, std::mt19937& random,
                                                      unsigned attempts) {
    if (!area.valid() || !detail::positiveLengths(size) || attempts == 0 || attempts > 100000 ||
        size.x > area.maximum.x - area.minimum.x || size.y > area.maximum.y - area.minimum.y ||
        size.z > area.maximum.z - area.minimum.z) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "CollisionSystem::randomFreeBox",
                            "Box must fit in the area; attempts must be in [1,100000].");

        return {};
    }

    for (const auto& box : occupied)
        if (!box.valid()) {
            Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "CollisionSystem::randomFreeBox",
                                "Invalid occupied bounds.");

            return {};
        }

    for (unsigned i = 0; i < attempts; ++i) {
        Bounds3 box;

        for (int axis = 0; axis < 3; ++axis)
            box.minimum[axis] =
                std::uniform_real_distribution<float>(area.minimum[axis], area.maximum[axis] - size[axis])(random);
        box.maximum = box.minimum + size;

        if (std::none_of(occupied.begin(), occupied.end(), [&](const Bounds3& other) {
                return box.overlaps(other);
            }))
            return box;
    }
    Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "CollisionSystem::randomFreeBox",
                        "No free placement within the attempt budget.",
                        "Use smaller objects, fewer occupied regions or a larger placement area.");

    return {};
}

std::optional<EntityId> PatternSystem::addBox(Scene& scene, const glm::vec3& size, const glm::vec3& position,
                                              GlColor color, std::optional<EntityId> parent) {
    if (!detail::positiveLengths(size) || !detail::finiteVector(position) || !detail::validColor(color) ||
        (parent && !scene.getComponent(*parent))) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "PatternSystem::addBox",
                            "Need positive dimensions, finite position/color and an existing parent.");

        return {};
    }

    auto model = DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Cube, size);

    for (auto& face : model.faces)
        face.color = color;

    Transform3D transform;

    transform.basis = position;

    const auto entity = scene.addModel(model, transform, PivotMode::Preserve);

    if (!entity || !scene.setParent(*entity, parent))
        return {};

    return entity;
}

std::optional<EntityId> PatternSystem::addPolygon(Scene& scene, const std::vector<glm::vec3>& points, GlColor color,
                                                  std::optional<EntityId> parent) {
    if (points.size() < 3 || !detail::validColor(color) || (parent && !scene.getComponent(*parent))) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "PatternSystem::addPolygon",
                            "Need a convex polygon (>=3 points), valid color and parent.");

        return {};
    }

    Mesh face;

    face.localVertices = points;
    face.color = color;

    for (std::size_t i = 1; i + 1 < points.size(); ++i)
        face.triangleIndices.insert(face.triangleIndices.end(), {0, std::uint32_t(i), std::uint32_t(i + 1)});
    recalculateNormals(face);

    Model model;

    model.faces.push_back(face);

    const auto entity = scene.addModel(model, Transform3D{}, PivotMode::Preserve);

    if (!entity || !scene.setParent(*entity, parent))
        return {};

    return entity;
}

std::optional<EntityId> PatternSystem::addRoom(Scene& scene, const Bounds3& bounds, bool frontOpen) {
    if (!bounds.valid()) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "PatternSystem::addRoom",
                            "Room bounds must have positive finite extents.");

        return {};
    }

    auto model = DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Cube, bounds.maximum - bounds.minimum);

    for (auto& face : model.faces) {
        face.cull = CullMode::Front;
        recalculateNormals(face);

        for (auto& normal : face.vertexNormals)
            normal = -normal;
    }
    model.faces[0].visible = !frontOpen;

    Transform3D transform;

    transform.basis = (bounds.minimum + bounds.maximum) * .5f;

    return scene.addModel(model, transform, PivotMode::Preserve);
}

std::optional<EntityId> PatternSystem::addJoint(Scene& scene, EntityId parent, const glm::vec3& anchor) {
    if (!scene.getComponent(parent) || !detail::finiteVector(anchor)) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "PatternSystem::addJoint",
                            "Missing parent or non-finite anchor.");

        return {};
    }

    Transform3D transform;

    transform.basis = anchor;

    const auto joint = scene.addGroup(transform);

    if (!joint || !scene.setParent(*joint, parent))
        return {};

    return joint;
}

bool PatternSystem::rotateJoint(Scene& scene, EntityId joint, const glm::vec3& axis, float degrees) {
    const auto* component = scene.getComponent(joint);
    const auto rotation = TransformSystem::rotation({{0, 0, 0}, axis}, degrees);

    if (!component || !rotation)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PatternSystem::rotateJoint",
                                   "Missing joint or invalid rotation.");

    auto transform = component->transform;

    transform.rotation = glm::mat3(*rotation);

    return scene.setTransform(joint, transform);
}

bool PatternSystem::follow(Scene& scene, EntityId entity, const DistancePath& path, double distance,
                           const glm::vec3& offset, bool orient) {
    const auto pose = path.sample(distance);
    const auto* component = scene.getComponent(entity);

    if (!pose || !component || !detail::finiteVector(offset))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "PatternSystem::follow",
                                   "Invalid path sample, entity or offset.");

    auto transform = component->transform;

    transform.basis = pose->position + offset;

    if (orient)
        transform.rotation = glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), glm::radians(pose->yawDegrees), glm::vec3(0, 1, 0)));

    return scene.setTransform(entity, transform);
}

bool PatternSystem::addNumber(Scene& scene, unsigned digit, const glm::vec3& position, float height, GlColor color,
                              std::optional<EntityId> parent) {
    if (digit > 9 || !detail::finiteVector(position) || !std::isfinite(height) || height <= 0 ||
        !detail::validColor(color) || (parent && !scene.getComponent(*parent)))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "PatternSystem::addNumber",
                                   "Need digit 0-9, positive height, finite values and a valid parent.");
    constexpr unsigned masks[10] = {0x3f, 0x06, 0x5b, 0x4f, 0x66, 0x6d, 0x7d, 0x07, 0x7f, 0x6f};

    const glm::vec2 endpoints[7][2] = {{{0, 1}, {.5f, 1}},    {{.5f, 1}, {.5f, .5f}}, {{.5f, .5f}, {.5f, 0}},
                                       {{0, 0}, {.5f, 0}},    {{0, .5f}, {0, 0}},     {{0, 1}, {0, .5f}},
                                       {{0, .5f}, {.5f, .5f}}};

    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 7; ++i)
        if (masks[digit] & (1u << i)) {
            const auto a = position + glm::vec3(endpoints[i][0] * height, 0),
                       b = position + glm::vec3(endpoints[i][1] * height, 0);

            if (!scene.addLine(a, b, color, parent))
                return false;
        }

    return true;
}

bool PatternSystem::rotateFace(Scene& scene, EntityId entity, std::size_t face, const FaceRotationConfig& config) {
    const auto* component = scene.getComponent(entity);
    const auto* mesh = scene.getMesh(entity, face);
    if (!component || !mesh || mesh->localVertices.size() < 3 || !std::isfinite(config.degrees))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "rotateFace",
                                   "Need a valid polygon face and finite angle.");
    const auto srt = detail::faceSrtMatrix(*mesh);
    auto point = [&](const glm::vec3& vertex) { return glm::vec3(srt * glm::vec4(vertex, 1)); };
    AxisLine axis;
    switch (config.kind) {
    case FaceRotationKind::EdgeHinge:
        if (config.edge >= mesh->localVertices.size()) return false;
        axis.point = point(mesh->localVertices[config.edge]);
        axis.direction = point(mesh->localVertices[(config.edge + 1) % mesh->localVertices.size()]) - axis.point;
        break;
    case FaceRotationKind::SelfSpin:
        for (const auto& vertex : mesh->localVertices) axis.point += point(vertex);
        axis.point /= float(mesh->localVertices.size());
        axis.direction = config.axis;
        break;
    case FaceRotationKind::ModelOrbit: {
        const auto bounds = detail::bounds(component->polyhedron);
        axis.point = bounds.first*.5f + bounds.second*.5f;
        axis.direction = config.axis;
        break;
    }
    default: return false;
    }
    const auto matrix = TransformSystem::rotation(axis, config.degrees);
    return matrix && scene.setFaceLocalMatrix(entity, face, *matrix, FaceAnimationSpace::Model);
}

bool PatternSystem::foldFace(Scene& scene, EntityId entity, std::size_t face, const AxisLine& hinge, float degrees) {
    const auto matrix = TransformSystem::rotation(hinge, degrees);

    return matrix && scene.setFaceLocalMatrix(entity, face, *matrix);
}

bool PatternSystem::openFace(Scene& scene, EntityId entity, std::size_t face, float progress,
                             const glm::vec3& displacement, const glm::vec3& topPivot, bool scaleOpen) {
    if (!std::isfinite(progress) || progress < 0 || progress > 1 || !detail::finiteVector(displacement) ||
        !detail::finiteVector(topPivot))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PatternSystem::openFace",
                                   "Need progress in [0,1] and finite vectors.");

    if (scaleOpen) {
        // Fully open faces are hidden; retain a nonsingular matrix for normals/picking.
        const auto scale = TransformSystem::scaling({1, std::max(1e-4f, 1 - progress), 1}, topPivot);

        return scale && scene.setFaceLocalMatrix(entity, face, *scale) &&
               scene.setFaceVisible(entity, face, progress < 1);
    }

    return scene.setFaceLocalMatrix(entity, face, glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), displacement * progress)) &&
           scene.setFaceVisible(entity, face, true);
}

std::optional<std::size_t> PatternSystem::cameraFacingWall(const Scene& scene, EntityId room, const Camera& camera) {
    const auto* component = scene.getComponent(room);
    const auto world = scene.worldMatrix(room);

    if (!component || component->polyhedron.faces.size() != 6 || !world || !CameraSystem::validCamera(camera) ||
        std::abs(glm::determinant(glm::mat3(*world))) < 1e-8f) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "cameraFacingWall",
                            "Need a six-face room, invertible world transform and valid camera.");

        return {};
    }

    const auto view = glm::normalize(camera.target - camera.position);
    const auto normals = glm::transpose(glm::inverse(glm::mat3(*world)));
    float best = 0;
    std::optional<std::size_t> selected;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        const auto& vertices = component->polyhedron.faces[i].localVertices;

        if (vertices.size() < 3)
            return {};

        const auto n = glm::normalize(normals * glm::cross(vertices[1] - vertices[0], vertices[2] - vertices[0]));
        const float score = glm::dot(n, view);

        if (score > best + 1e-6f) {
            best = score;
            selected = i;
        }
    }

    return selected; // Select the far interior wall visible along the viewing direction.
}

bool PatternSystem::openRoomWall(Scene& scene, EntityId room, std::size_t face, float progress) {
    const auto* mesh = scene.getMesh(room, face);

    if (!mesh || face >= 4 || mesh->localVertices.empty())
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "openRoomWall",
                                   "Select one of the four side walls of addRoom().");

    if (!std::isfinite(progress) || progress < 0 || progress > 1)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "openRoomWall",
                                   "Need progress in [0,1].");

    glm::vec3 pivot(0);

    for (const auto& vertex : mesh->localVertices)
        pivot += vertex;
    pivot /= float(mesh->localVertices.size());
    // Fully open faces are hidden; retain a nonsingular matrix for normals/picking.
    const float factor = std::max(1e-3f, 1 - progress);
    const auto scale = TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(factor), pivot);

    return scale && scene.setFaceLocalMatrix(room, face, *scale) && scene.setFaceVisible(room, face, progress < 1);
}

std::vector<glm::ivec3> PatternSystem::snakeCells(const glm::ivec3& cells) {
    std::vector<glm::ivec3> result;

    if (cells.x <= 0 || cells.y != 1 || cells.z <= 0 || std::uint64_t(cells.x) * cells.z > 1000000) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "PatternSystem::snakeCells",
                            "Need an XZ grid with y=1 and at most 1,000,000 cells.");

        return result;
    }

    for (int z = 0; z < cells.z; ++z)
        for (int x = 0; x < cells.x; ++x)
            result.push_back({z % 2 ? cells.x - 1 - x : x, 0, z});

    return result;
}

bool PatternSystem::aligned(float first, float second, float tolerance) {
    if (!std::isfinite(first) || !std::isfinite(second) || !std::isfinite(tolerance) || tolerance < 0)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "PatternSystem::aligned",
                                   "Need finite heights and nonnegative tolerance.");

    return std::abs(double(first) - second) <= tolerance;
}

glm::vec3 PatternSystem::snap(const glm::vec3& point, float spacing) {
    if (!detail::finiteVector(point) || !std::isfinite(spacing) || spacing <= 0) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PatternSystem::snap",
                            "Need finite point and positive spacing.");

        return point;
    }

    const auto result = glm::vec3(glm::round(glm::dvec3(point) / double(spacing)) * double(spacing));

    if (!detail::finiteVector(result)) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PatternSystem::snap", "Snap overflow.");

        return point;
    }

    return result;
}

std::optional<glm::vec3> PatternSystem::screenOnPlane(const Camera& camera, double x, double y,
                                                      const PickViewport& viewport, const glm::vec3& planePoint,
                                                      const glm::vec3& normal) {
    const auto ray = screenRay(camera, x, y, viewport);
    const auto n = detail::motionUnitVector(normal);

    if (!ray || !n || !detail::finiteVector(planePoint)) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Input, "PatternSystem::screenOnPlane",
                            "Invalid camera, viewport, pointer or plane.");

        return {};
    }

    const double denominator = glm::dot(glm::dvec3(ray->direction), glm::dvec3(*n));

    if (std::abs(denominator) < 1e-10)
        return {};

    const double t = glm::dot(glm::dvec3(planePoint) - glm::dvec3(ray->origin), glm::dvec3(*n)) / denominator;

    if (t < 0 || t > ray->maxDistance)
        return {};

    return glm::vec3(glm::dvec3(ray->origin) + glm::dvec3(ray->direction) * t);
}

bool PatternSystem::moveCamera(Camera& camera, const glm::vec3& offset) {
    auto next = camera;

    next.position += offset;
    next.target += offset;

    if (!CameraSystem::validCamera(next))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PatternSystem::moveCamera",
                                   "Camera translation creates an invalid camera.");
    camera = next;

    return true;
}

bool PatternSystem::yawCamera(Camera& camera, float degrees) {
    if (!CameraSystem::validCamera(camera))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PatternSystem::yawCamera",
                                   "Invalid camera.");

    const auto forward = glm::normalize(camera.target - camera.position),
               right = glm::normalize(glm::cross(forward, camera.up));
    const auto up = glm::normalize(glm::cross(right, forward));
    const auto rotation = TransformSystem::rotation({camera.position, up}, degrees);

    if (!rotation)
        return false;

    auto next = camera;

    next.target = glm::vec3(*rotation * glm::vec4(camera.target, 1));
    next.up = glm::mat3(*rotation) * up;

    if (!CameraSystem::validCamera(next))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PatternSystem::yawCamera",
                                   "Rotation creates an invalid camera.");
    camera = next;

    return true;
}

bool PatternSystem::orbitCamera(Camera& camera, const AxisLine& axis, float degrees) {
    const auto rotation = TransformSystem::rotation(axis, degrees);

    if (!rotation)
        return false;

    auto next = camera;

    next.position = glm::vec3(*rotation * glm::vec4(camera.position, 1));
    next.target = glm::vec3(*rotation * glm::vec4(camera.target, 1));
    next.up = glm::mat3(*rotation) * camera.up;

    if (!CameraSystem::validCamera(next))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PatternSystem::orbitCamera",
                                   "Orbit creates an invalid camera.");
    camera = next;

    return true;
}

bool PatternSystem::zoomCamera(Camera& camera, float factor) {
    if (!std::isfinite(factor) || factor <= 0)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PatternSystem::zoomCamera",
                                   "Zoom factor must be positive and finite.");

    auto next = camera;

    if (next.orthographic)
        next.orthographicHalfExtent *= factor;
    else
        next.position = next.target + (next.position - next.target) * factor;

    if (!CameraSystem::validCamera(next))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Transform, "PatternSystem::zoomCamera",
                                   "Zoom creates an invalid camera.");
    camera = next;

    return true;
}

std::vector<RenderPass> PatternSystem::threeViews(const Camera& perspective, int width, int height,
                                                  const RenderOptions& options) {
    if (width < 3 || height < 2 || !CameraSystem::validCamera(perspective)) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Framebuffer, "PatternSystem::threeViews",
                            "Need a valid camera and framebuffer of at least 3x2 pixels.");

        return {};
    }

    const int left = width * 2 / 3, right = width - left, bottom = height / 2;
    RenderPass main;

    main.camera = perspective;
    main.camera.orthographic = false;
    main.options = options;
    main.viewport = {0, 0, left, height};

    RenderPass top = main;

    top.viewport = {left, bottom, right, height - bottom};
    top.camera.orthographic = true;
    top.camera.position = perspective.target + glm::vec3(0, 20, 0);
    top.camera.target = perspective.target;
    top.camera.up = {0, 0, -1};
    top.camera.orthographicHalfExtent = std::max(6.5f, perspective.orthographicHalfExtent);

    RenderPass front = top;

    front.viewport = {left, 0, right, bottom};
    front.camera.position = perspective.target + glm::vec3(0, 0, 20);
    front.camera.up = {0, 1, 0};

    return {main, top, front};
}

std::optional<JointRig> PatternSystem::addWalkingRig(Scene& scene, const GridLayout& grid) {
    if (!grid.valid() || !detail::rigidRoom(grid.frame)) {
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "PatternSystem::addWalkingRig",
                            "Need a valid grid with a rigid frame.");

        return {};
    }

    JointRig rig;
    const auto root = scene.addGroup();

    if (!root)
        return {};
    rig.root = *root;

    auto point = [&](glm::vec3 ratio) {
        return grid.origin + grid.extent * ratio;
    };

    const auto torso =
        addBox(scene, grid.extent * glm::vec3(.45f, .35f, .7f), point({.5f, .58f, .5f}), {.65f, .65f, .7f}, rig.root);
    const auto head = addJoint(scene, rig.root, point({.5f, .8f, .5f}));

    if (!torso || !head)
        return {};
    rig.head = *head;

    if (!addBox(scene, grid.extent * glm::vec3(.3f, .2f, .6f), {0, grid.extent.y * .1f, 0}, {1, .8f, .5f}, rig.head) ||
        !addBox(scene, grid.extent * glm::vec3(.1f, .05f, .2f), {0, grid.extent.y * .1f, grid.extent.z * .4f},
                {1, .2f, .2f}, rig.head))
        return {};

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        const float x = i == 0 ? .2f : .8f;
        const GlColor color = i == 0 ? GlColor{.2f, .6f, 1} : GlColor{1, .4f, .2f};
        const auto shoulder = addJoint(scene, rig.root, point({x, .72f, .5f})),
                   hip = addJoint(scene, rig.root, point({i == 0 ? .35f : .65f, .4f, .5f}));

        if (!shoulder || !hip)
            return {};
        rig.shoulders[i] = *shoulder;
        rig.hips[i] = *hip;

        if (!addBox(scene, grid.extent * glm::vec3(.15f, .32f, .3f), {0, -grid.extent.y * .16f, 0}, color, *shoulder) ||
            !addBox(scene, grid.extent * glm::vec3(.18f, .4f, .4f), {0, -grid.extent.y * .2f, 0}, color, *hip))
            return {};
    }

    Transform3D transform;

    transform.rotation = glm::mat3(grid.frame);
    transform.basis = glm::vec3(grid.frame[3]);

    if (!scene.setTransform(rig.root, transform))
        return {};

    return rig;
}

bool PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig(Scene& scene, const JointRig& rig, const glm::vec3& feet, float yawDegrees,
                                   double distance, float speed) {
    if (!detail::finiteVector(feet) || !std::isfinite(yawDegrees) || !std::isfinite(distance) ||
        !std::isfinite(speed) || speed < 0)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Motion, "PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig",
                                   "Need finite pose values and nonnegative speed.");

    const auto* root = scene.getComponent(rig.root);

    if (!root || !scene.getComponent(rig.head))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig",
                                   "Rig IDs are missing or stale.");

    for (auto id : rig.shoulders)
        if (!scene.getComponent(id))
            return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig",
                                       "Shoulder joint is missing.");

    for (auto id : rig.hips)
        if (!scene.getComponent(id))
            return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__, __LINE__, PipelineStage::Scene, "PatternSystem::poseWalkingRig",
                                       "Hip joint is missing.");

    auto transform = root->transform;

    transform.basis = feet;
    transform.rotation = glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), glm::radians(yawDegrees), glm::vec3(0, 1, 0)));

    if (!scene.setTransform(rig.root, transform))
        return false;

    const float angle =
        std::min(50.0f, 15 + speed * 12) * float(std::sin(std::remainder(distance, 1.0) * 6.283185307179586));

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        const float sign = i == 0 ? 1.0f : -1.0f;

        if (!rotateJoint(scene, rig.hips[i], {1, 0, 0}, sign * angle) ||
            !rotateJoint(scene, rig.shoulders[i], {1, 0, 0}, -sign * angle))
            return false;
    }

    return true;
}
} // namespace graphics

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

standard 전체 연결: main → Console 상태 → 콜백 구현

9번이 그네·사람형·선형보간의 실제 실행 경로다. clock과 animation_은 Console 멤버이며 초기화 때 만들고 프레임 update에서 재사용한다. 아래 세 파일은 서로 연결되는 실제 소스다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/main.cpp
#include "Console.h"

/// 콜백을 등록하고 애플리케이션을 실행한다.
int main() {
    graphics::Application app;
    Console sample(app);

    app.setInitHandler([&](graphics::Application&) {
        return sample.initScene();
    });
    app.setKeyHandler([&](graphics::Application&, int key, int action) {
        sample.onKey(key, action);
    });
    app.setMouseHandler([&](graphics::Application&, const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {
        sample.onMouse(event);
    });
    app.setUpdateHandler([&](graphics::Application&, float dt) {
        sample.onUpdate(dt);
    });
    app.setRenderHandler([&](graphics::Application&) {
        return sample.onRender();
    });

    graphics::ApplicationConfig config;

    config.title = "standard - Motion, Grid, diagnostics and render passes";

    return app.run(config);
}
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/Console.h
#pragma once
#include "framework/Framework.h"

class Console {
    graphics::Application& app_;
    std::optional<graphics::MotionId> pathId_;
    std::optional<graphics::MotionId> spinId_;
    std::optional<graphics::MotionId> globalId_;
    std::optional<graphics::LayerId> mirrorLayer_;
    std::optional<graphics::LayerId> normalSpinLayer_;
    std::optional<graphics::LayerId> insertedLayer_;
    bool paused_ = false;
    bool spinEnabled_ = true;
    bool globalEnabled_ = false;
    int reflection_ = 1;

    enum class Demo { Motion, Grid, Room, Animation };

    Demo demo_ = Demo::Motion;
    graphics::AnimationClock clock_;
    graphics::DistancePath distancePath_;
    std::vector<graphics::EntityId> gridBoxes_;
    graphics::RoomBody roomBody_;
    graphics::EntityId room_ = 0, roomCube_ = 0;
    std::vector<graphics::JointAnimation> animation_;

    struct JointControl {
        std::size_t track = 0;
        std::string name;
        graphics::Transform3D initialRest;
    };

    std::vector<JointControl> jointControls_;
    std::size_t selectedJoint_ = 0;
    int selectedAxis_ = 0;


    /// 선택한 관절의 기준 SRT와 피벗을 키 입력으로 편집한다.
    bool handleJointKey(int key);


    /// 현재 선택 관절과 조작 축을 콘솔에 출력한다.
    void printSelectedJoint() const;


    bool threeViews_ = false, trace_ = false, floorOpen_ = false;


    /// 거리 기반 격자 예제를 구성한다.
    bool initGrid();


    /// 회전하는 방 예제를 구성한다.
    bool initRoom();


    /// 선형보간·그네·사람 관절의 계층 애니메이션 예제를 구성한다.
    bool initAnimation();


    /// 선택한 경로로 이동 모션을 바꾼다.
    bool choosePath(int kind);


    /// 반사 변환을 순서대로 변경한다.
    bool changeReflection();


    /// 크기 레이어를 삽입하거나 제거한다.
    bool toggleInsertedLayer();


    /// 조작 방법을 출력한다.
    void printHelp() const;


public:
    /// 애플리케이션 참조를 보관한다.
    explicit Console(graphics::Application& app) : app_(app) {
    }


    /// 장면 객체와 초기 상태를 구성한다.
    bool initScene();


    /// 키 입력에 따라 동작을 변경한다.
    void onKey(int key, int action);


    /// 마우스 버튼 입력을 처리한다.
    void onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event);


    /// 프레임 시간으로 상태와 장면을 갱신한다.
    void onUpdate(float dt);


    /// 현재 장면을 지정한 뷰로 렌더링한다.
    bool onRender();
};
실제 저장소 소스 · standard/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <iostream>

/// 선택한 경로로 이동 모션을 바꾼다.
bool Console::choosePath(int kind) {
    graphics::PathMotion path;

    path.durationSeconds = 6;

    const glm::vec3 center(1.7f, 0, 0);

    if (kind == 1) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::ellipse(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 2) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::rectangle(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 3) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polygon(
            {{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.4f, 0}, {2.1f, .6f, 0}, {1.4f, .8f, 0}, {.9f, .2f, 0}});
    } else if (kind == 4) {
        path.path = [center](double u) {
            const double angle = u * 6.283185307179586;

            return center +
                   glm::vec3(float(.7 * std::sin(2 * angle)), float(.6 * std::sin(angle)), float(.3 * std::cos(angle)));
        };
        path.interpolation = graphics::PathSystem::smoothStep;
    } else if (kind == 5) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::circle(center, .65f, {0, 0, 1});
    } else if (kind == 6) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polyline({{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.5f, 0}, {2, .6f, 0}});
        path.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;
    } else {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::Motion motion;
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();

    if (!pathId_ || !motion.append(graphics::Motion::followPath(path))) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!motions.setMotion(*pathId_, motion) || !motions.seekMotion(*pathId_, 0)) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "경로 " << kind << ": 시작 시각 0초로 변경\n";

    return true;
}

/// 장면 객체와 초기 상태를 구성한다.
bool Console::initScene() {
    demo_ = Demo::Motion;

    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    paused_ = false;
    spinEnabled_ = true;
    globalEnabled_ = false;
    reflection_ = 1;
    insertedLayer_.reset();

    const auto first = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
    const auto second = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});

    if (!first || !second) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!scene.setTint(*first, {1, .35f, .2f}) || !scene.setTint(*second, {.2f, .7f, 1})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& motions = scene.getMotions();

    pathId_ = motions.createMotion(graphics::Motion{});

    graphics::RotationMotion spin;

    spin.degreesPerSecond = 90;
    spinId_ = motions.createSpin(spin);

    graphics::RotationMotion global;

    global.degreesPerSecond = 15;
    globalId_ = motions.createOrbit(global);

    if (!pathId_ || !spinId_ || !globalId_ || !choosePath(1)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, false) ||
        !motions.bindMotion(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), *globalId_)) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto normalPath = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer());
    const auto reflection = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});

    if (!normalPath || !reflection) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig mirror;

    mirror.transform = *reflection;
    mirrorLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), mirror);

    if (!mirrorLayer_) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto mirroredPath = motions.pushLayer(*mirrorLayer_);

    normalSpinLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(*normalPath);

    if (!mirroredPath || !normalSpinLayer_) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto mirroredSpin = motions.pushLayer(*mirroredPath);

    if (!mirroredSpin || !motions.bindMotion(*normalPath, *pathId_) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredPath, *pathId_) ||
        !motions.bindMotion(*normalSpinLayer_, *spinId_) || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredSpin, *spinId_) ||
        !scene.bindEntity(*first, *normalSpinLayer_) || !scene.bindEntity(*second, *mirroredSpin)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *first) ||
        !scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *second) ||
        !scene.addLine({0, -1.4f, 0}, {0, 1.4f, 0}, {.5f, .5f, .5f})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& camera = app_.getCamera();

    camera.position = {5, 3, 8};
    camera.target = {0, 0, 0};
    camera.up = {0, 1, 0};
    printHelp();

    return true;
}

/// 반사 변환을 순서대로 변경한다.
bool Console::changeReflection() {
    const int next = (reflection_ + 1) % 4;
    std::optional<glm::mat4> matrix = glm::mat4(1);

    if (next == 1) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 2) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 3) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectLine(graphics::AxisLine{});
    }

    if (!mirrorLayer_ || !matrix) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig config;

    config.transform = *matrix;

    if (!app_.getScene().getMotions().setLayer(*mirrorLayer_, config)) {
        return false;
    }
    reflection_ = next;

    const char* names[] = {"없음", "YZ평면 반사", "원점 점대칭", "Y축 직선대칭"};

    std::cout << "반사: " << names[reflection_] << '\n';

    return true;
}

/// 크기 레이어를 삽입하거나 제거한다.
bool Console::toggleInsertedLayer() {
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();

    if (insertedLayer_) {
        if (!motions.removeLayer(*insertedLayer_)) {
            return false;
        }
        insertedLayer_.reset();
        std::cout << "추가 크기 레이어 삭제\n";

        return true;
    }

    const auto scale = graphics::TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(1.5f));

    if (!normalSpinLayer_ || !scale) {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::LayerConfig config;

    config.space = graphics::TransformSpace::Local;
    config.transform = *scale;
    insertedLayer_ = motions.insertLayer(*normalSpinLayer_, config);

    if (!insertedLayer_) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "첫 객체에 1.5배 Local 크기 레이어 삽입\n";

    return true;
}

/// 키 입력에 따라 동작을 변경한다.
void Console::onKey(int key, int action) {
    if (action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_7 || key == GLFW_KEY_8 || key == GLFW_KEY_9 || key == GLFW_KEY_0) {
        const bool ok = key == GLFW_KEY_7   ? initGrid()
                        : key == GLFW_KEY_8 ? initRoom()
                        : key == GLFW_KEY_9 ? initAnimation()
                                            : initScene();

        if (!ok)
            app_.requestClose();

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_V) {
        threeViews_ = !threeViews_;

        return;
    }

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_D) {
        trace_ = !trace_;
        graphics::Diagnostics::configure({true, trace_});

        return;
    }

    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation && handleJointKey(key))
        return;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_F && demo_ == Demo::Room) {
        floorOpen_ = !floorOpen_;

        return;
    }

    if (demo_ != Demo::Motion) {
        if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE)
            clock_.playing = !clock_.playing;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE)
            app_.requestClose();
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H)
            printHelp();

        return;
    }

    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();
    bool ok = true;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
        app_.requestClose();
    } else if (key >= GLFW_KEY_1 && key <= GLFW_KEY_6) {
        ok = choosePath(key - GLFW_KEY_1 + 1);
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE) {
        const bool playing = paused_;

        ok = pathId_ && spinId_ && globalId_ && motions.setMotionPlaying(*pathId_, playing) &&
             motions.setMotionPlaying(*spinId_, playing) && motions.setMotionPlaying(*globalId_, playing);

        if (ok) {
            paused_ = !paused_;
            std::cout << (paused_ ? "시간 정지\n" : "시간 재개\n");
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_S) {
        ok = spinId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*spinId_, !spinEnabled_);

        if (ok) {
            spinEnabled_ = !spinEnabled_;
            std::cout << "자전 기여: " << spinEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_G) {
        ok = globalId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, !globalEnabled_);

        if (ok) {
            globalEnabled_ = !globalEnabled_;
            std::cout << "전체 공전 기여: " << globalEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_M) {
        ok = changeReflection();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_I) {
        ok = toggleInsertedLayer();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H) {
        printHelp();
    }

    if (!ok) {
        std::cerr << "샘플 명령 적용 실패. 장면을 종료합니다.\n";
        app_.requestClose();
    }
}

/// 마우스 버튼 입력을 처리한다.
void Console::onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {
    if (event.button != GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT || event.action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }

    std::optional<graphics::PickResult> hit;

    if (threeViews_) {
        const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

        if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
            return;

        const auto passes =
            graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions());

        for (const auto& pass : passes) {
            const auto& v = pass.viewport;
            const double top = size[1] - v.y - v.height;

            if (event.framebufferX >= v.x && event.framebufferX < v.x + v.width && event.framebufferY >= top &&
                event.framebufferY < top + v.height)
                hit = graphics::pick(app_.getScene(), pass.camera, event.framebufferX, event.framebufferY,
                                     {double(v.x), top, double(v.width), double(v.height)});
        }
    } else
        hit = app_.pickAt(event.windowX, event.windowY);

    if (hit) {
        std::cout << "entity=" << hit->entityId << " face=" << hit->faceIndex << '\n';
    }
}

/// 조작 방법을 출력한다.
void Console::printHelp() const {
    std::cout
        << "standard: 프레임워크 Motion·반사·레이어 샘플\n"
        << "1 타원 / 2 사각형 / 3 다각형 / 4 사용자 3D 경로+보간 / 5 원 / 6 왕복 꺾은선\n"
        << "Space 시간 정지·재개 / S 자전 기여 / G 전체 공전 기여\n"
        << "M 반사 변경 / I 크기 레이어 삽입·삭제 / H 도움말 / Esc 종료\n"
        << "초기 반사: YZ평면. M은 점대칭 -> Y축 직선대칭 -> 없음 -> YZ평면 순환\n"
        << "왼쪽 클릭: 물체의 entity/face ID 출력\n"
        << "7 Grid/거리 기반 배치 / 8 실내 미끄럼·낙하 / 0 기존 Motion 예제\n"
        << "9 선형보간·진자·사람형·그네→사람 계층 애니메이션\n"
        << "9번 관절: Tab/Backspace 선택, X/Y/Z 축, 좌우 회전, 상하 이동, PageUp/Down 피벗, +/- 균일 배율, R 복원\n"
        << "V 3뷰 전환 / D 단계 추적 로그 / F 실내 예제 바닥 개방 / Space 정지·재개\n";
}

/// 거리 기반 격자 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initGrid() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Grid;
    clock_.reset();
    gridBoxes_.clear();

    graphics::GridLayout grid;

    grid.origin = {-3, -.1f, -2};
    grid.extent = {6, .2f, 4};
    grid.cells = {6, 1, 4};

    for (int z = 0; z < 4; ++z)
        for (int x = 0; x < 6; ++x) {
            const auto center = grid.cellCenter({x, 0, z}), size = grid.relativeSize({.15f, 1, .22f});

            if (!center || !size ||
                !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, *size, *center,
                                                 (x + z) % 2 ? graphics::GlColor{.25f, .3f, .4f}
                                                             : graphics::GlColor{.4f, .45f, .55f}))
                return false;
        }

    if (!distancePath_.setPoints({{-2.5f, .35f, -1.5f}, {2.5f, .35f, -1.5f}, {2.5f, .35f, 1.5f}}))
        return false;

    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        const auto box =
            graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .5f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {.25f + .14f * i, .7f - .1f * i, .3f});

        if (!box || !graphics::PatternSystem::addNumber(scene, i + 1, {-.07f, -.1f, .205f}, .2f, {1, 1, 1}, *box))
            return false;
        gridBoxes_.push_back(*box);
    }
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {7, 6, 8};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 0, 0};
    app_.getCamera().orthographicHalfExtent = 4;

    return true;
}

/// 회전하는 방 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initRoom() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Room;
    clock_.reset();
    floorOpen_ = false;

    const auto room = graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene, {{-2, -1, -2}, {2, 2, 2}});
    const auto cube = graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.5f, .5f, .5f}, {0, -.75f, 0}, {1, .7f, .2f});

    if (!room || !cube)
        return false;
    room_ = *room;
    roomCube_ = *cube;
    roomBody_ = graphics::RoomBody{};
    roomBody_.localPosition = {0, -.75f, 0};
    roomBody_.halfExtent = {.25f, .25f, .25f};
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {5, 3, 8};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 0, 0};

    return true;
}

/// 선형보간과 그네·사람의 부모·자식 애니메이션을 구성한다.
bool Console::initAnimation() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();

    scene.clear();
    demo_ = Demo::Animation;
    clock_.reset();
    animation_.clear();
    jointControls_.clear();
    selectedJoint_ = 0;
    selectedAxis_ = 0;

    if (!graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {8, .15f, 6}, {0, -.15f, 0}, {.25f, .3f, .35f}))
        return false;

    graphics::SwingConfig swingConfig;

    swingConfig.pivot = {1, 3, 0};
    swingConfig.pendulum.amplitudeDegrees = 25;
    swingConfig.pendulum.periodSeconds = 3;

    const auto swing = graphics::AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider(scene, swingConfig);

    if (!swing)
        return false;
    animation_ = swing->animation;

    graphics::HumanoidConfig personConfig;

    personConfig.grid.frame = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), glm::vec3(-2, 0, 0));

    const auto person = graphics::AnimationSystem::addHumanoid(scene, personConfig);

    if (!person)
        return false;

    auto pose = graphics::HumanoidPose::seated();

    pose.hips = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};
    pose.knees = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};

    if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid(scene, *person, pose))
        return false;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        graphics::PendulumMotion sway;

        sway.amplitudeDegrees = 30;
        sway.periodSeconds = 2;
        sway.phaseDegrees = i == 0 ? 0 : 180;

        graphics::Motion motion;

        if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::pendulum(sway)))
            return false;

        const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, person->shoulders[i], motion);

        if (!track)
            return false;
        animation_.push_back(*track);
    }

    const auto marker = scene.addGroup();

    if (!marker || !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .4f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {1, .85f, .15f}, *marker))
        return false;

    graphics::LinearMotion move;

    move.from = {-2, .3f, 2};
    move.to = {2, .3f, 2};
    move.durationSeconds = 4;
    move.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;

    graphics::Motion motion;

    if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::linear(move)))
        return false;

    const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, *marker, motion);

    if (!track)
        return false;
    animation_.push_back(*track);

    const auto control = [&](graphics::EntityId id, const std::string& name) {
        std::size_t index = 0;

        while (index < animation_.size() && animation_[index].joint != id)
            ++index;

        if (index == animation_.size()) {
            auto fixed = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, id, graphics::Motion{});

            if (!fixed)
                return false;
            animation_.push_back(*fixed);
        }
        jointControls_.push_back({index, name, animation_[index].rest});

        return true;
    };

    const auto personControls = [&](const graphics::HumanoidRig& rig, const std::string& prefix) {
        if (!control(rig.root, prefix + " root") || !control(rig.pelvis, prefix + " pelvis") ||
            !control(rig.torso, prefix + " torso") || !control(rig.neck, prefix + " neck"))
            return false;

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
            const auto side = prefix + (i == 0 ? " left " : " right ");

            if (!control(rig.shoulders[i], side + "shoulder") || !control(rig.elbows[i], side + "elbow") ||
                !control(rig.hands[i], side + "hand") || !control(rig.hips[i], side + "hip") ||
                !control(rig.knees[i], side + "knee") || !control(rig.feet[i], side + "foot"))
                return false;
        }

        return true;
    };

    if (!control(swing->frame, "swing frame") || !control(swing->hinge, "swing hinge") ||
        !control(swing->seat, "swing seat") || !personControls(swing->person, "rider") ||
        !personControls(*person, "standing"))
        return false;
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {8, 5, 10};
    app_.getCamera().target = {0, 1.4f, 0};
    std::cout << "9: 노란 상자는 4초 편도 선형보간, 그네는 3초 주기 진자, 좌석의 사람은 계층 변환을 따릅니다.\n";
    printSelectedJoint();

    return graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, 0);
}

/// 선택한 관절의 기준 자세를 편집하고 같은 시각의 애니메이션에 반영한다.
bool Console::handleJointKey(int key) {
    if (jointControls_.empty())
        return false;

    if (key == GLFW_KEY_TAB)
        selectedJoint_ = (selectedJoint_ + 1) % jointControls_.size();
    else if (key == GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE)
        selectedJoint_ = (selectedJoint_ + jointControls_.size() - 1) % jointControls_.size();
    else if (key == GLFW_KEY_X || key == GLFW_KEY_Y || key == GLFW_KEY_Z)
        selectedAxis_ = key == GLFW_KEY_X ? 0 : key == GLFW_KEY_Y ? 1 : 2;
    else {
        auto& track = animation_[jointControls_[selectedJoint_].track];
        const auto saved = track.rest;

        if (key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT || key == GLFW_KEY_RIGHT) {
            glm::vec3 axis(0);

            axis[selectedAxis_] = 1;
            track.rest.rotation =
                track.rest.rotation *
                glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), glm::radians(key == GLFW_KEY_LEFT ? -5.0f : 5.0f), axis));
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_UP || key == GLFW_KEY_DOWN)
            track.rest.basis[selectedAxis_] += key == GLFW_KEY_UP ? .05f : -.05f;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP || key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_DOWN)
            track.rest.localBasis[selectedAxis_] += key == GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP ? .05f : -.05f;
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD || key == GLFW_KEY_MINUS ||
                 key == GLFW_KEY_KP_SUBTRACT) {
            const bool grow = key == GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key == GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD;

            track.rest.scale = glm::vec3(std::clamp(track.rest.scale.x * (grow ? 1.1f : 1 / 1.1f), .1f, 3.0f));
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_R)
            track.rest = jointControls_[selectedJoint_].initialRest;
        else
            return false;

        if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(app_.getScene(), animation_, clock_.seconds)) {
            track.rest = saved;
            std::cerr << "관절 편집 거절: 기준 자세를 복원했습니다.\n";
        }
    }
    printSelectedJoint();

    return true;
}

/// 선택 관절·축·이동·피벗·균일 배율을 출력한다.
void Console::printSelectedJoint() const {
    if (jointControls_.empty())
        return;

    const auto& control = jointControls_[selectedJoint_];
    const auto& rest = animation_[control.track].rest;

    std::cout << "joint " << selectedJoint_ + 1 << '/' << jointControls_.size() << ": " << control.name
              << " axis=" << "XYZ"[selectedAxis_] << " position=" << rest.basis.x << ',' << rest.basis.y << ','
              << rest.basis.z << " pivot=" << rest.localBasis.x << ',' << rest.localBasis.y << ',' << rest.localBasis.z
              << " scale=" << rest.scale.x << '\n';
}

/// 프레임 시간으로 상태와 장면을 갱신한다.
void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    if (demo_ == Demo::Motion || !clock_.playing)
        return;

    if (!clock_.advance(dt)) {
        app_.requestClose();

        return;
    }

    auto& scene = app_.getScene();
    bool ok = true;

    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation) {
        ok = graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, clock_.seconds);
    } else if (demo_ == Demo::Grid) {
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < gridBoxes_.size(); ++i)
            ok = graphics::PatternSystem::follow(scene, gridBoxes_[i], distancePath_,
                                                 clock_.seconds + distancePath_.length() * i / 5) &&
                 ok;
    } else {
        const float angle = .4f * float(std::sin(clock_.seconds));
        const auto matrix = glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1), angle, glm::vec3(0, 0, 1));
        graphics::Transform3D roomTransform;

        roomTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix);
        roomTransform.basis = glm::vec3(matrix * glm::vec4(0, .5f, 0, 1));
        ok =
            scene.setTransform(room_, roomTransform) && scene.setFaceVisible(room_, 5, !floorOpen_) &&
            graphics::CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(roomBody_, {{-2, -1, -2}, {2, 2, 2}}, matrix, dt, floorOpen_, false,
                                                    {0, -9.8f, 0}, {0, 0, .4f * float(std::cos(clock_.seconds))});

        graphics::Transform3D cubeTransform;

        cubeTransform.basis = roomBody_.position(matrix);

        if (!roomBody_.released)
            cubeTransform.rotation = glm::mat3(matrix);
        ok = scene.setTransform(roomCube_, cubeTransform) && ok;
    }

    if (!ok)
        app_.requestClose();
}

/// 현재 장면을 지정한 뷰로 렌더링한다.
bool Console::onRender() {
    if (!threeViews_)
        return app_.renderScene();

    const auto size = app_.getFramebufferSize();

    if (size[0] < 3 || size[1] < 2)
        return true;

    return app_.renderViews(
        graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(), size[0], size[1], app_.getRenderOptions()));
}

2026-11-02 소스 기준. 작성 예제·소스 발췌는 빌드·테스트·브라우저 시각 확인을 수행하지 않았다. 사용자가 실행하여 확인한다.
